        ,[ordVerifierId]

		,[ordDailyCounter]/*RZ14011109-Add ordDailyCounter*/

		/*RZ14030305- Add ordCurrencyUnitIdI and amounts*/
		,[ordCurrencyUnitIdI]
		,[ordCurrencyUnitIRate]
		/*RZ14030305- End*/
		)
    VALUES
        (@OrderOprID
        ,@PreOrderId
        ,@PreOrderVerifierId
        ,@ordTitle
        ,@ordNumber
        ,@ordDate
        ,@ordCompanyId
        ,@ordCompanyPersonId
        ,@OrderProjectId
        ,@ordSubject        
        ,@ordTaxIndex
        ,@ordCalculateTax
        ,NULL/*ordIsVerified*/
        ,NULL /*ordVerifiedRegisterDate*/
        ,NULL/*ordVerifiedRegisterDatePersian*/
        ,1/*ordStatusId*/
        ,@ordIsFixed
        ,@ordGuarantyId
        ,@ordSpecialWord
        ,@ordDescriptionI
        ,@ordDescriptionII        
        ,@OrderPrintFormatID
        ,@ordRLogoId
        ,@ordLLogoId
        ,@ordSigningI
        ,@ordSigningII
        ,@ordSigningIII
        ,@ordAccountNumberI
        ,@ordAccountShabaNumberI
        ,@ordAccountNumberII
        ,@ordAccountShabaNumberII
        ,@ordSignatureId
        ,@RegisterDatePersian
        ,@UserId
        ,@UserId
        ,@FPId
        ,@Month1
        ,@RegisterDate
        ,@RegisterDatePersian
        ,NULL/*ordVerifierId*/
        ,@ordSettlementTypeId
        ,@ordDay
        ,@ordTime                
        ,@OrderReferUserId        
        ,@ordNote
        ,@ordDateTime
        ,@RegisterDate        
        ,@ordSerial
        ,@AppType
        ,@ordVerifierRequestId
        ,@DefaultLanguageID
        ,@UserId
        ,@RegisterDatePersian
        ,@RegisterDate
        ,@ordVerificationWorkFlowId        
        ,@ordCurrencyUnitId
        ,@ordDeliveryTermId                
        ,@ordDefWHId
        ,@ordDefPrdPriceGroupId
        ,@ordSellerId
        ,@ordEmailUsersInfoId
        ,@ordEmailUserDefaultSettingId
        ,@ordSaleCenterId
        ,@ordDefPayTypeId
		,@ordDailyCounter/*RZ14011109-Add ordDailyCounter*/
		,@ordDefaultSettingId/*RZ14020324- Add ordDefaultSettingId*/
		,@ordTspSettingId/*RZ14020419- Add ordTspSettingId*/
		/*RZ14030305- Add ordCurrencyUnitIdI and amounts*/
		,@ordCurrencyUnitIdI
		,@ordCurrencyUnitIRate
		/*RZ14030305- End*/
		)
	SET @InsertedOrderID = SCOPE_IDENTITY()
	SET @InsertedOrderNumber = @ordNumber

	IF(ISNULL(@InsertedOrderID,0) > 0)
	BEGIN
	/**/
		IF(ISNULL(@OrderDefaultSettingId,0) <> 0)
		BEGIN
		/**/
			INSERT INTO [dbo].[WSOrder_Lang]
				([OrderID]
				,[ordTitle]
				,[ordSubject]
				,[ordSpecialWord]
				,[ordDescriptionI]
				,[ordDescriptionII]
				,[ordSigningI]
				,[ordSigningII]
				,[ordSigningIII]           
				,[ordNote]           
				,[LanguageID])
			SELECT 
				@InsertedOrderID
				,[Title]
				,[Subject]
				,[SpecialWord]
				,[DescriptionI]
				,[DescriptionII]
				,[SigningI]
				,[SigningII]
				,[SigningIII]           
				,ISNULL((SELECT TOP(1) [Value] 
						FROM OPENJSON (ISNULL(@OrderNoteLang,'[]'))
						WITH(
							[Value] NVARCHAR(4000) '$.Value',
							[LanguageId] INT '$.LanguageId'
						) JS
						WHERE JS.LanguageId = SD.LanguageID						
						),[Note])
				,[LanguageID]
			FROM [dbo].[WSOrderDefaultSetting_Lang] SD
			WHERE [OrderSettingID] =@OrderDefaultSettingId 
			AND SD.LanguageID <> @DefaultLanguageID		
		/**/
		END
		ELSE IF(ISNULL(@OrderDefaultSettingId,0) = 0)
		BEGIN
		/**/
			INSERT INTO [dbo].[WSOrder_Lang]
				([OrderID]
				,[ordNote]           
				,[LanguageID])
			SELECT 
				@InsertedOrderID
				,[Value]
				,[LanguageId]
			FROM OPENJSON (ISNULL(@OrderNoteLang,'[]'))
			WITH(
				[Value] NVARCHAR(4000) '$.Value',
				[LanguageId] INT '$.LanguageId'
			) JS
			WHERE JS.LanguageId <> @DefaultLanguageID		
		/**/
		END
	/**/
	END

	/*------------------- tran1 --------------------*/
	IF (@@ERROR > 0)
	BEGIN 
		SET @ErrorFrom='Insert Into WSOrders' 
		SET @ErrorNumber=@@ERROR 		
		ROLLBACK TRAN 
		SELECT 0 AS 'Result'
	END		
	ELSE 
	COMMIT TRAN 
	/*------------------- tran1 --------------------*/
	/*----------------------------------------------*/
	/*----------------------------------------------*/
	/*------------------- tran2 --------------------*/
	IF(ISNULL(@InsertedOrderID,0) > 0)
	BEGIN
	/**/
		/*--------------------------------------------*/
		DECLARE @DocItemId INT = NULL,@ordIPrice NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
		@ordIPrdPriceGroupID INT = NULL
		BEGIN TRAN
			DECLARE CrsrOrdItem CURSOR LOCAL FOR
			/**/				
				SELECT 
				*
				FROM OPENJSON(ISNULL(@DocItemList,'[]'))
				WITH(
					DocItemId INT '$.DocItemId',
					PrdPrice NUMERIC(18,2) '$.PrdPrice',
					PrdPriceGroupId INT '$.PrdPriceGroupId'				
				)
			/**/
			OPEN CrsrOrdItem
			FETCH NEXT FROM CrsrOrdItem INTO @DocItemId,@ordIPrice,@ordIPrdPriceGroupID
			WHILE @@Fetch_Status = 0
			BEGIN
			/**/
				/*-----------------------------------------------------*/
				DECLARE @OrderItemID INT = NULL,@ordIPrdId INT = NULL,@ItemCount INT=NULL,
				@ordIQuantity FLOAT = NULL,
				@ordITotalPrice NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,@ordIDescription NVARCHAR(4000) = NULL,
				@ordIPrdUnitId INT = NULL, @ordIOrderView INT = NULL,
				@ordIFCR FLOAT = NULL,@ordIFCRI FLOAT = NULL,@ordIWHId INT = NULL,		
				@ordITracing NVARCHAR(200) = NULL,@ordIPriceSum NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
				@ordIQuantityTotal FLOAT = NULL,@ordIPrdUnitIdI INT = NULL,
				@ordIPrdUnitIRateResult FLOAT = NULL,
				@ordIPrdNickname NVARCHAR(1000) = NULL/*RZ14011021- Add PrdNickname*/
				/*-------------------------------------------*/
				SELECT  TOP(1)				
					 @ordIPrdId = [PrdId]
					,@ordIQuantity = [Quantity]					
					,@ordIDescription = [Description]					
					,@ordIWHId = [WHId]										
					,@ordIFCR = [FCR]
					,@ordIFCRI = [FCRI]					
					,@ordITracing = [Tracing]
					,@ordIPrdUnitId = [PrdUnitId]
					,@ordIQuantityTotal = [QuantityTotal]
					,@ordIPrdUnitIdI = [PrdUnitIdI]
					,@ordIPrdUnitIRateResult = [PrdUnitIRateResult]
					,@ordIPrdNickname = [PrdNickname] /*RZ14011021- Add PrdNickname*/
				FROM [dbo].[WSDocItems]
				WHERE [DocItemID] = @DocItemId
				/*-------------------------------------------*/
				IF(ISNULL(@ordIQuantityTotal,0) <> 0 )
				BEGIN
					SET @ordIPriceSum = ISNULL(@ordIPrice,0) * ISNULL(@ordIQuantityTotal,0)
				END
				ELSE
				BEGIN
					SET @ordIPriceSum = (ISNULL(@ordIQuantity,1) * ISNULL(@ordIFCR,1) * ISNULL(@ordIFCRI,1)) * ISNULL(@ordIPrice,0)
				END
				/**/
				/*SET @ordITotalPrice = @ordIPriceSum*/
				/*RZ14021203- Add OrderItemId to WSDocItems and add TaxIndexItem*/
				IF(ISNULL(@ordICalculateTax,0) = 1)
				BEGIN
				/**/
					IF(ISNULL(@ordIPriceSum,0) > 0 AND ISNULL(@ordITaxIndex,0) > 0)
					BEGIN
					/**/
						SET @ordITaxSum = ((ISNULL(@ordIPriceSum,0)) * ISNULL(@ordITaxIndex,0) / 100)
						SET @ordITax = (ISNULL(@ordIPrice,0) * ISNULL(@ordITaxIndex,0) / 100)

					/**/
					END
				/**/
				END
				
				SET @ordITotalPrice = ISNULL(@ordIPriceSum,0) + ISNULL(@ordITaxSum,0) 

				IF(ISNULL(@cuUnitIsDecimal,0) = 0)
				BEGIN
					SET @ordIPrice = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@ordIPrice))					
					SET @ordITaxSum = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@ordITaxSum))
					SET @ordITax = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@ordITax))			
					SET @ordIPriceSum = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@ordIPriceSum))
					SET @ordITotalPrice = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@ordITotalPrice))					
				END
				/*RZ14021203- End*/

				SET @ItemCount = (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM [dbo].[WSOrderItems] OI WHERE OI.OrderId = @InsertedOrderID)
				SET @ordIOrderView = (ISNULL(@ItemCount,0) + 1) + (ISNULL(@ItemCount,0) * 4 ) - 1
				/*-------------------------------------------*/
				INSERT INTO [dbo].[WSOrderItems]
					([OrderId]
					,[ordIPrdId]
					,[ordIQuantity]
					,[ordIPrice]
					,[ordITotalPrice]
					,[ordIDescription]
					,[UserId]
					,[FPId]
					,[Month1]
					,[RegisterDate]
					,[RegisterDatePersian]
					,[ordIPrdUnitId]
					,[ordICurrencyUnitId]
					,[ordIOrderView]
					,[ordIFCR]
					,[ordIFCRI]
					,[ordIWHId]
					,[AppType]
					,[LanguageID]
					,[FirstRegisterUserId]
					,[FirstRegisterDatePersian]
					,[FirstRegisterDate]
					,[ordIPrdPriceGroupID]
					,[ordITracing]
					,[ordIPriceSum]
					,[ordIQuantityTotal]
					,[ordIPrdUnitIdI]
					,[ordIPrdUnitIRateResult]
					,[ordIPrdNickname]/*RZ14011021- Add PrdNickname*/
					/*RZ14021203- Add OrderItemId to WSDocItems and add TaxIndexItem*/
					,[ordITax] 
					,[ordITaxIndex] 
					,[ordICalculateTax] 
					,[ordITaxSum] 
					/*RZ14021203- End*/
					)
				VALUES
					(@InsertedOrderID
					,@ordIPrdId
					,@ordIQuantity
					,@ordIPrice
					,@ordITotalPrice
					,@ordIDescription
					,@UserId
					,@FPId
					,@Month1
					,@RegisterDate
					,@RegisterDatePersian
					,@ordIPrdUnitId
					,@ordCurrencyUnitId
					,@ordIOrderView
					,@ordIFCR
					,@ordIFCRI
					,@ordIWHId
					,@AppType
					,@DefaultLanguageID
					,@UserId
					,@RegisterDatePersian
					,@RegisterDate
					,@ordIPrdPriceGroupID
					,@ordITracing
					,@ordIPriceSum
					,@ordIQuantityTotal
					,@ordIPrdUnitIdI
					,@ordIPrdUnitIRateResult
					,@ordIPrdNickname /*RZ14011021- Add PrdNickname*/
					/*RZ14021203- Add OrderItemId to WSDocItems and add TaxIndexItem*/
					,@ordITax
					,@ordITaxIndex
					,@ordICalculateTax
					,@ordITaxSum
					/*RZ14021203- End*/
					)
				SET @OrderItemID = SCOPE_IDENTITY()
				/*-------------------------------------------*/
				IF(ISNULL(@OrderItemID,0) > 0)
				BEGIN
				/**/
					INSERT INTO [dbo].[WSOrderItems_Lang]
						([OrderItemID]
						,[ordIDescription]						
						,[LanguageID]
						,[ordIPrdNickname] /*RZ14011021- Add PrdNickname*/
						)
					SELECT
						 @OrderItemID
						,[Description]
						,[LanguageID]
						,[PrdNickname] /*RZ14011021- Add PrdNickname*/
					FROM [dbo].[WSDocItems_Lang]
					WHERE DocItemID = @DocItemId
					AND LanguageID <> @DefaultLanguageID
					/*-------------------------------------------*/
					/*RZ14021203- Add OrderItemId to WSDocItems*/
					UPDATE [dbo].[WSDocItems]
					SET [OrderItemId] = @OrderItemID
					WHERE [DocItemID] = @DocItemId
					/*RZ14021203- End*/					
					/*-------------------------------------------*/
					DECLARE @OrderItemSerialID INT = NULL,@SerialId INT = NULL,
					@OrdItemSerialDesc NVARCHAR(300) = NULL,@OrdItemSerialWHId INT = NULL,
					@DocItemSerialID INT = NULL
					


					DECLARE CrsrORDItemSerial CURSOR LOCAL FOR
					/**/				
						SELECT 
							 [DocItemSerialID]
							,[Description]
							,[WHId]
							,[SerialId]							
						FROM [dbo].[WSDocItemSerials]
						WHERE [ItemId] = @DocItemId
						AND ISNULL([SerialId],0) <> 0
					/**/
					OPEN CrsrORDItemSerial
					FETCH NEXT FROM CrsrORDItemSerial INTO @DocItemSerialID,@OrdItemSerialDesc,@OrdItemSerialWHId,@DocItemSerialID
					WHILE @@Fetch_Status = 0
					BEGIN
					/**/
						/*-----------------------------------------------------*/
						SET @OrderItemSerialID = NULL
						INSERT INTO [dbo].[WSOrderItemSerials]
							([OrderItemId]
							,[SerialId]
							,[Description]
							,[UserId]
							,[Month1]
							,[FPId]
							,[RegisterDate]
							,[RegisterDatePersian]							
							,[WHId]
							,[AppType]
							,[LanguageID]
							,[FirstRegisterUserId]
							,[FirstRegisterDatePersian]
							,[FirstRegisterDate])
						VALUES
							(@OrderItemId
							,@DocItemSerialID
							,@OrdItemSerialDesc
							,@UserId
							,@Month1
							,@FPId
							,@RegisterDate
							,@RegisterDatePersian
							,@OrdItemSerialWHId
							,@AppType
							,@DefaultLanguageID
							,@UserId
							,@RegisterDatePersian
							,@RegisterDate)
						SET @OrderItemSerialID = SCOPE_IDENTITY()
						/*-------------------------------------------*/
						IF(ISNULL(@OrderItemSerialID,0) > 0 )
						BEGIN
						/**/
							INSERT INTO [dbo].[WSOrderItemSerials_Lang]
								([OrderItemSerialID]
								,[LanguageID]
								,[Description])
							SELECT
								 @OrderItemSerialID
								,[LanguageID]
								,[Description]
							FROM [dbo].[WSDocItemSerials_Lang]
							WHERE DocItemSerialID = @DocItemSerialID
							AND LanguageID <> @DefaultLanguageID							
						/**/
						END

						/*-----------------------------------------------------*/														    				 					
					/**/	
					FETCH NEXT FROM CrsrORDItemSerial INTO @DocItemSerialID,@OrdItemSerialDesc,@OrdItemSerialWHId,@DocItemSerialID
					END
					CLOSE CrsrORDItemSerial
					DEALLOCATE CrsrORDItemSerial
					/*-------------------------------------------*/
				/**/
				END
				/*-------------------------------------------*/							
				/*-----------------------------------------------------*/														    				 					
			/**/	
			FETCH NEXT FROM CrsrOrdItem INTO @DocItemId,@ordIPrice,@ordIPrdPriceGroupID
			END
			CLOSE CrsrOrdItem
			DEALLOCATE CrsrOrdItem
		/*------------------- tran2 --------------------*/
		IF (@@ERROR > 0)
		BEGIN 
			SET @ErrorFrom='Insert Into WSOrdersItems' 
			SET @ErrorNumber=@@ERROR 	
			SET @InsertedOrderID = 0
			ROLLBACK TRAN 
			SELECT 0 AS 'Result'
		END		
		ELSE 
		COMMIT TRAN

		/*--------------------------------------------*/
		IF(ISNULL(@InsertedOrderID,0) > 0)
		BEGIN
		/**/
			EXEC [dbo].[MobspCalcAndUpdateOrderAmounts]
			@OrderId = @InsertedOrderID,
			@Month1 = @Month1,
			@LanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID,
			@DefaultLanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID
			/**/
			UPDATE [dbo].[WSDoc]
			SET OrderId = @InsertedOrderID
			WHERE DocID = @DocId
			/**/
			SELECT 1 AS 'Result'
		/**/
		END
		


		/*--------------------------------------------*/
	/**/
	END

	/*------------------- tran2 --------------------*/
	/*----------------------------------------------*/
/**/
END

/*---------------------------------------------------*/
/*---------------------------------------------------*/
IF (@ErrorNumber > 0)
BEGIN
/**/
	RAISERROR('Error From TransAction %S', 16, 1, @ErrorFrom)
	ROLLBACK TRANSACTION Main
	SET @InsertedOrderID = 0
	SET @InsertedOrderNumber = 0
	SELECT 0 AS 'Result'
/**/
END
ELSE
BEGIN
/**/
	COMMIT TRANSACTION Main
	SELECT 1 AS 'Result'
/**/
END	
/*---------------------------------------------------*/
/*---------------------------------------------------*/
/**/
END

Also for @ordSettlementTypeId the same problem exists and the solution is the same*/
/*AI990231 field ordDay is getitng pordDay mistakenly so that we declare an variable and fill it with the day getting from @OrderDate and user it instead of pordDay*/

/*AI14000225 - Add DeliveryEndDate - Modify ImportDate in OrderPayment*/
/*AI14000428 - Remove ����*/

Create PROCEDURE [dbo].[MobspIssueOrderFromPreOrder]
(		
/* 
	EXEC MobspIssueOrderFromPreOrder  18, '13890714',1, 1, 3, 7, 1, '13890714'
*/
@PreOrderID INT,
@OrderDate VARCHAR(8),
@OrderOprId INT,
@OrderPrintFormatID INT,
@OrderReferUserId INT,
@UserId INT,
@Month1 INT,
@FPId INT,
@RegisterDatePersian VARCHAR(8),
@AppType NVARCHAR(50), 
/*950403*/
@ordNote NVARCHAR(4000)= NULL, 
@ordSettlementTypeId INT= NULL,
@OrderDefaultSettingId INT= NULL,  
/**/
@InsertedOrderID INT OUTPUT ,
@InsertedOrderNumber BIGINT OUTPUT,/*RZ990811- Change type from int to bigint*/
/*ADBF951130*/
@LanguageID INT,
/*RZ960218 for multi language And add length variable*/
@ordNoteLang NVARCHAR(MAX)= NULL,
/*RZ14010531- Add project and add manual quantity for every product in multi order*/
@OrdProjectId INT = NULL,
@PordItemList NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL,
@IsTransferFreePay BIT = NULL,
@IsTransferFreeComm BIT = NULL,
/*RZ14010531- End*/
@ordCompanyPersonId INT = NULL,/*RZ14020531- Add person and issue orderindex*/
/*RZ14020604- Add parameters*/
@ordIssueSettingByPrdId INT = NULL,
@ordIssueIndexedByPrdId INT = NULL
/*RZ14020604- End*/
)
AS
BEGIN
/*---------------------------------------------------*/
/*RZ14010531- Add project and add manual quantity for every product in multi order*/
IF(ISNULL(@OrdProjectId,0) = 0 )
BEGIN
/**/
	SET @OrdProjectId = (SELECT pordProjectId FROM dbo.WSPreOrder PO WHERE PO.PreOrderId = @PreOrderID)
/**/
END
/**/
IF(ISNULL(@PordItemList,'') = '')
BEGIN
/**/	
	/*---------------------------------------*/
	/*---------- Issue one order ------------*/
	SET @IsTransferFreePay = 1
	SET @IsTransferFreeComm = 1
	/*---------------------------------------*/
	IF(ISNULL((SELECT TOP(1) OrderId FROM WSOrder WHERE PreOrderId = @PreOrderID ),0) = 0) /*PreOrder not have any order*/
	BEGIN
	/**/
		SET @PordItemList = (SELECT 
								PreOrderItemID [Id],			
								pordIQuantity [Value],
								/*RZ14020604- Add parameters and expireDate in items*/
								pordIValidityExpireDate [ValidityExpireDate],
								pordIValidityExpireDatePersian [ValidityExpireDatePersian],
								pordIPrdAutoCalcRate [PrdAutoCalcRate]
								/*RZ14020604- End*/
							FROM dbo.WSPreOrderItems
							WHERE PreOrderId = @PreOrderID		
							ORDER BY ISNULL(pordIOrderView, PreOrderItemID)	
							FOR JSON PATH)
	/**/
	END
	ELSE /*IF(ISNULL((SELECT TOP(1) OrderId FROM WSOrder WHERE PreOrderId = @PreOrderID ),0) > 0)*/ /*PreOrder have order*/
	BEGIN
	/**/
		SET @PordItemList = (SELECT 
								PreOrderItemID [Id],			
								(ISNULL(pordIQuantity,0) - 
								ISNULL((SELECT SUM(ordIQuantity) 
										FROM dbo.WSOrderItems OI
										WHERE OrderId IN(SELECT O.OrderId 
														 FROM dbo.WSOrder O 
														 WHERE O.PreOrderId = POI.PreOrderId
														 )
										/*AND ordIWHId = pordIWHId
										AND ordIPrdId = pordIPrdId
										AND ordIPrice = pordIPrice*/
										/*RZ14021202- Add PreOrderItemId to WSOrderItems*/
										AND OI.PreOrderItemId = POI.PreOrderItemID
										/*RZ14021202- End*/
														 ),0)
	
								) [Value],
								/*RZ14020604- Add parameters and expireDate in items*/
								pordIValidityExpireDate [ValidityExpireDate],
								pordIValidityExpireDatePersian [ValidityExpireDatePersian],
								pordIPrdAutoCalcRate [PrdAutoCalcRate]
								/*RZ14020604- End*/
							FROM dbo.WSPreOrderItems POI
							WHERE PreOrderId = @PreOrderID								
							ORDER BY ISNULL(pordIOrderView, PreOrderItemID)	
							FOR JSON PATH)
		/*AI14020710- Add condition*/
		IF(ISNULL(@PordItemList,'') <>'')
		BEGIN
		/**/
			SET @PordItemList = (SELECT 
									 [Id]
									,[Value]
									,[ValidityExpireDate]
									,[ValidityExpireDatePersian]
									,[PrdAutoCalcRate]
								FROM OPENJSON(@PordItemList)
								WITH (
								[Id] INT '$.Id',
								[Value] FLOAT '$.Value'	,
								[ValidityExpireDate] DATETIME '$.ValidityExpireDate',
								[ValidityExpireDatePersian] NVARCHAR(8) '$.ValidityExpireDatePersian',
								[PrdAutoCalcRate] NUMERIC(18,2) '$.PrdAutoCalcRate'			
								) AS JS	
								WHERE ISNULL([Value],0) > 0	
								FOR JSON PATH)
		/**/
		END
		/*AI14020710- End*/
	/**/
	END	
/**/
END
/**/
DECLARE @cuUnitIsDecimal BIT
SET @cuUnitIsDecimal = (SELECT TOP 1 [IsDecimal]
						FROM [dbo].[WSCurrencyUnits] 
						WHERE [CurrencyUnitID] = (SELECT pordCurrencyUnitId 
												  FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrder] 
												  WHERE PreOrderID = @PreOrderID))
/*RZ14010531- End*/
/*---------------------------------------------------*/
DECLARE @InsertedOldPreOrderID INT
/*SELECT @InsertedOldPreOrderID = OrderID FROM dbo.WSOrder Ord WHERE Ord.PreOrderId = @PreOrderID

	-
    ISNULL((SELECT SUM(ISNULL(ordIQuantity,0)) FROM dbo.WSOrderItems OI WHERE OI.OrderId IN(SELECT O.OrderId FROM [dbo].[WSOrder] O WHERE O.PreOrderId = @PreOrderID)),0)
	),0) <= 0 /*AI14020710 - Change condition from = 0 to <= 0*/

	OR ISNULL(@PordItemList,'') = ''/*AI14020710- Add condition*/
)
BEGIN
	RETURN -1
END

	,@pordIDiscountSum NUMERIC(18,2)
	,@pordIDiscountISum NUMERIC(18,2),@pordITaxSum NUMERIC(18,2)
	/*RZ990815- End*/
	/*RZ990916- Add fields and commission*/
	,@pordIDeliveryTermId INT,@pordIDeliveryDate DATETIME,@pordIDeliveryDatePersian NVARCHAR(8)
	/*RZ990916- End*/

	 ,@pordIPrdColorId INT/*RZ14010612- Add prdColorId*/

	 ,@PrdUnitIRate FLOAT,@PrdUnitIRateCalcAuto BIT,@PrdQtyNoFCR BIT 
	 /*RZ14010620- End*/
	 ,@pordIPrdNickname NVARCHAR(1000) = NULL/*RZ14011021- Add PrdNickname*/
	/*RZ14020324- Add new fields*/
	,@pordIDiscountDesc NVARCHAR(1000) = NULL
	,@pordIDeliveryTime NVARCHAR(10) = NULL
	,@pordIDeliveryDay NVARCHAR(20) = NULL
	,@pordIDeliveryEndDate DATETIME = NULL
	,@pordIDeliveryEndDatePersian NVARCHAR(8) = NULL
	,@pordIDeliveryEndTime NVARCHAR(10) = NULL
	,@pordIDeliveryEndDay NVARCHAR(20) = NULL 			
	/*RZ14020324- End*/
	,@pordILinkAccWHIdStr NVARCHAR(50) = NULL/*RZ14020427- Add pordILinkAccWHIdStr*/
	/*RZ14020510- Add fields*/
	,@pordIValidityExpireDate DATETIME = NULL
	,@pordIValidityExpireDatePersian NVARCHAR(8) = NULL,
	@pordIPrdAutoCalcRate NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,@pordIPrdAutoCalcInflationRateInfo NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,/*RZ14020528- Change PrdAutoBill*/
	@pordIDescriptionI NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL
	/*RZ14020510- End*/	
	/*-------------------------- End define variables --------------------------------*/
	Declare Crsr_Add_Item CURSOR For
		SELECT 
			PreOrderItemID,
			pordIPrdId, 
			pordIQuantity, 
			pordIQuantityDesc,
			pordIPrice, 
			pordIDiscount,
			pordIDiscountI,
			pordITotalPrice, 
			pordIDescription, 
			pordIDiscountPercent,
			pordIDiscountIPercent,

			pordIRoundPlus,

			pordICurrencyUnitId,
			pordIFCR, 
			pordIFCRI,
			pordIOrderView,
			pordITax, 
			pordITaxIndex, 
			pordICalculateTax, 
			pordINotVisible, 
			pordINotInSum,
			pordIWHId
			/*ADBF990126*/
			, pordIPrdPriceGroupID
			,pordITracing /*RZ990318- Add Tracing*/
			/*RZ990815- Add fields to orderItems and preorderItems*/
				,[pordIPriceSum]
				,[pordIDiscountSum]
				,[pordIDiscountISum]
				,[pordITaxSum]
			/*RZ990815- End*/
			/*RZ990916- Add fields and commission*/
			,[pordIDeliveryTermId],[pordIDeliveryDate],[pordIDeliveryDatePersian]
			/*RZ990916- End*/
			,[pordIQuantityTotal] /*RZ14010524- Add QuantityTotal*/
			,[pordIPrdColorId]/*RZ14010612- Add prdColorId*/
			/*RZ14010620- Add PrdUnitIdI*/
			,[pordIPrdUnitIdI]
			,[pordIPrdUnitIRateResult]
			/*RZ14010620- End*/
			,[pordIPrdNickname] /*RZ14011021- Add PrdNickname*/	
			/*RZ14020324- Add new fields*/
			,[pordIDiscountDesc]
			,[pordIDeliveryTime]
			,[pordIDeliveryDay]
			,[pordIDeliveryEndDate]
			,[pordIDeliveryEndDatePersian]
			,[pordIDeliveryEndTime]
			,[pordIDeliveryEndDay] 			
			/*RZ14020324- End*/
			,[pordILinkAccWHIdStr]/*RZ14020427- Add pordILinkAccWHIdStr*/
			/*RZ14020510- Add fields*/
			,[pordIValidityExpireDate]
			,[pordIValidityExpireDatePersian]
			/*RZ14020528- Change PrdAutoBill*/	
			,[pordIPrdAutoCalcRate]
			,[pordIPrdAutoCalcInflationRateInfo]
			/*RZ14020528- End*/				
			,[pordIDescriptionI]
			/*RZ14020510- End*/
		FROM dbo.WSPreOrderItems
		WHERE PreOrderId = @PreOrderID
		/*RZ14010531- Add project and add manual quantity for every product in multi order*/
		AND PreOrderItemID IN(SELECT [Id]
								FROM OPENJSON(@PordItemList)
								WITH (
									[Id] INT '$.Id',
									[Value] FLOAT '$.Value'	
								) AS JS	
								/*WHERE ISNULL([Value],0) > 0*/)
		/*RZ14010531- End*/		
		ORDER BY ISNULL(pordIOrderView, PreOrderItemID)	

	OPEN Crsr_Add_Item
	FETCH NEXT FROM Crsr_Add_Item INTO @PreOrderItemID, @pordIPrdId, @pordIQuantity, 
								@pordIQuantityDesc, @pordIPrice, @pordIDiscount, @pordIDiscountI,
								@pordITotalPrice, @pordIDescription, @pordIDiscountPercent, @pordIDiscountIPercent, 
								@pordIRound, @pordIRoundPlus, @pordIPrdUnitId, @pordICurrencyUnitId, @pordIFCR,
								@pordIFCRI, @pordIOrderView, @pordITax, @pordITaxIndex, @pordICalculateTax,
								@pordINotVisible, @pordINotInSum, @pordIWHId
								/*ADBF990126*/
								, @pordIPrdPriceGroupID,@pordITracing /*RZ990318- Add Tracing*/
								,@pordIPriceSum,@pordIDiscountSum,@pordIDiscountISum,@pordITaxSum/*RZ990815- Add fields to orderItems and preorderItems*/								
								,@pordIDeliveryTermId,@pordIDeliveryDate,@pordIDeliveryDatePersian/*RZ990916- Add fields and commission*/
								,@pordIQuantityTotal /*RZ14010524- Add QuantityTotal*/
								,@pordIPrdColorId /*RZ14010612- Add prdColorId*/
								,@pordIPrdUnitIdI,@pordIPrdUnitIRateResult/*RZ14010620- Add PrdUnitIdI*/	
								,@pordIPrdNickname /*RZ14011021- Add PrdNickname*/
								/*RZ14020324- Add new fields*/
								,@pordIDiscountDesc,@pordIDeliveryTime,@pordIDeliveryDay,@pordIDeliveryEndDate
								,@pordIDeliveryEndDatePersian,@pordIDeliveryEndTime,@pordIDeliveryEndDay 			
								/*RZ14020324- End*/
								,@pordILinkAccWHIdStr /*RZ14020427- Add pordILinkAccWHIdStr*/
								/*RZ14020510- Add fields*/
								,@pordIValidityExpireDate,@pordIValidityExpireDatePersian
								,@pordIPrdAutoCalcRate,@pordIPrdAutoCalcInflationRateInfo/*RZ14020528- Change PrdAutoBill*/								
								,@pordIDescriptionI
								/*RZ14020510- End*/


	WHILE @@Fetch_Status = 0
	BEGIN
	/**/
		/*----------------------------------------*/
		/*RZ14010531- Add project and add manual quantity for every product in multi order*/
		DECLARE @CurrQty FLOAT = NULL
		/*SET @CurrQty = (SELECT TOP(1) [Value]
						FROM OPENJSON(@PordItemList)
						WITH (
							[Id] INT '$.Id',
							[Value] FLOAT '$.Value'	
						) AS JS	
						WHERE [Id] = @PreOrderItemID)*/


		/*RZ14020604- Add parameters and expireDate in items*/
		DECLARE @jsValidityExpireDate DATETIME = NULL,@jsValidityExpireDatePersian NVARCHAR(8) = NULL,
		@jsPrdAutoCalcRate NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL
		/**/
		SELECT TOP(1) 
			@CurrQty = [Value],
			@jsValidityExpireDate = [ValidityExpireDate],
			@jsValidityExpireDatePersian = [ValidityExpireDatePersian],
			@jsPrdAutoCalcRate = [PrdAutoCalcRate]
		FROM OPENJSON(@PordItemList)
		WITH (
			[Id] INT '$.Id',
			[Value] FLOAT '$.Value'	,
			[ValidityExpireDate] DATETIME '$.ValidityExpireDate',
			[ValidityExpireDatePersian] NVARCHAR(8) '$.ValidityExpireDatePersian',
			[PrdAutoCalcRate] NUMERIC(18,2) '$.PrdAutoCalcRate'			
		) AS JS	
		WHERE [Id] = @PreOrderItemID	
		/*-------------------------------------------*/
		IF(ISNULL(@HasActionCanChangeValidityExpireDate,0) = 1)
		BEGIN
		/**/
			SET @pordIValidityExpireDate = @jsValidityExpireDate
			SET @pordIValidityExpireDatePersian = @jsValidityExpireDatePersian
		/**/
		END
		/**/
		IF(ISNULL(@HasActionCanChangePrdAutoCalcRate,0) = 1)
		BEGIN
		/**/
			SET @pordIPrdAutoCalcRate = @jsPrdAutoCalcRate
		/**/
		END
		/**/
		/*RZ14020604- End*/		

		/*----------------------------------------------------*/
		/*----------------- Calculate ------------------------*/
		DECLARE @ordIQuantity FLOAT = NULL,@ordIPrice NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
		@ordITotalPrice NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
		@ordIDiscount NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,@ordIDiscountPercent NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
		@ordIOrderView INT = NULL,@ordIFCR FLOAT = NULL,@ordITax NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
		@ordITaxIndex NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL, @ordICalculateTax BIT = NULL, 
		@ordIFCRI FLOAT = NULL,@ordIRound NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,@ordIRoundPlus NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
		@ordIPriceSum NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,@ordIDiscountSum NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
		@ordITaxSum NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,@ordIQuantityTotal FLOAT = NULL,
		@PordTmpI NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
		@TmpI NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,@TmpII NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,@ItemCount INT = NULL,
		@TmpIII NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,@TmpPrice NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
		@ExtraCalcItem NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,@ExtraCalcItemPercent FLOAT = NULL
			

		/*----------------------------------------------------*/
		SET @ordIPrice = @pordIPrice
		/*Set OrderView*/
		SET @ItemCount = (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM [dbo].[WSOrderItems] OI WHERE OI.OrderId = @OrderId)
		SET @ordIOrderView = (@ItemCount + 1) + (@ItemCount * 4 ) - 1
		/**/
		SET @ordICalculateTax = @pordICalculateTax
		SET @ordIFCR = @pordIFCR
		SET @ordIFCRI = @pordIFCRI
		SET @ordIRound = @pordIRound
		SET @ordIRoundPlus = @pordIRoundPlus
		SET @ordITaxIndex = @pordITaxIndex
		/*------------*/
		IF(ISNULL(@CurrQty,0) = 0 )
		BEGIN
		/**/
			SET @ordIQuantity = @pordIQuantity
			SET @ordITotalPrice = @pordITotalPrice
			SET @ordIDiscount = @pordIDiscount
			SET @ordIDiscountPercent = @pordIDiscountPercent			
			SET @ordITax = @pordITax			
			SET @ordIPriceSum = @pordIPriceSum
			SET @ordIDiscountSum = @pordIDiscountSum
			SET @ordITaxSum = @pordITaxSum
			SET @ordIQuantityTotal = @pordIQuantityTotal 			
		/**/
		END
		ELSE IF(ISNULL(@CurrQty,0) <> 0 )
		BEGIN
		/**/
			SET @ordIQuantity = ISNULL(@CurrQty,0)
			IF(ISNULL(@pordIQuantityTotal,0) <> 0)
			BEGIN
				SET @ordIQuantityTotal = @ordIQuantity * ISNULL(@ordIFCR,1)
			END
			/**/
			SET @TmpI = ISNULL(@ordIQuantity,1) * ISNULL(@ordIFCR,1) * ISNULL(@ordIFCRI,1)
			SET @PordTmpI = ISNULL(@pordIQuantity,1) * ISNULL(@pordIFCR,1) * ISNULL(@pordIFCRI,1)

			/*----------------------------------*/
			/*RZ14010620- Add PrdUnitIdI*/
			SET @PrdUnitIRate = NULL
			SET @PrdUnitIRateCalcAuto = NULL
			SET @PrdQtyNoFCR = NULL			
			/*--------------*/			
			SELECT 				
				@PrdUnitIRate = [PrdUnitIRate],
				@PrdUnitIRateCalcAuto = [PrdUnitIRateCalcAuto],
				@PrdQtyNoFCR = [PrdQtyNoFCR]				
			FROM [dbo].[WSProducts]
			WHERE ProductId = @pordIPrdId
			/*--------------*/
			IF(ISNULL(@PrdQtyNoFCR,0) = 1)
			BEGIN
			/**/
				SET @TmpI = ISNULL(@ordIQuantity,1) 
				SET @PordTmpI = ISNULL(@pordIQuantity,1) 
			/**/
			END
			/*----------------------------------------------------*/
			IF(/*ISNULL(@PrdUnitIRateCalcAuto,0) = 1 AND*/ ISNULL(@PrdUnitIRate,0) <> 0 AND ISNULL(@pordIPrdUnitIRateResult,0) <> 0) /*AI14020912- Calculate RateResult in fiexd PrdUnitIRateCalcAuto*/
			BEGIN
			/**/
				/*SET @pordIPrdUnitIRateResult = (1 / ISNULL(@PrdUnitIRate,0)) * ISNULL(@TmpI,1)*/
				SET @pordIPrdUnitIRateResult = (1 / ISNULL(@PrdUnitIRate,0)) * ISNULL(@ordIQuantity,1)/*RZ14010628- Neutralize PrdQtyNoFCR and FRCS in PrdUnitIRateResult*/

				IF(ISNULL(@pordIPrdUnitIRateResult,0) <> 0)
				BEGIN
				/**/
					SET @pordIPrdUnitIRateResult = CAST((FORMAT(@pordIPrdUnitIRateResult, '000.000')) AS FLOAT) 
				/**/
				END
			/**/
			END
			/*RZ14010620- End*/
			/*----------------------------------*/
			IF(ISNULL(@pordIDiscountSum,0) > 0)
			BEGIN
			/**/
				SET @ExtraCalcItemPercent = (ISNULL(@pordIDiscountSum,0) / (ISNULL(@PordTmpI,1)))
				SET @ordIDiscountSum = ISNULL(@ExtraCalcItemPercent,0) * @TmpI
				SET @ordIDiscount = ISNULL(@ExtraCalcItemPercent,0)
				IF(ISNULL(@ordIPrice,0) > 0)
					SET @ordIDiscountPercent = (ISNULL(@ordIDiscount,0) * 100) / ISNULL(@ordIPrice,0)
				/**/
				IF(ISNULL(@cuUnitIsDecimal,0) = 0)
				BEGIN					
					/*RZ14020626-Change Ceiling to Round*/
					SET @ordIDiscountSum = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@ordIDiscountSum))
					SET @ordIDiscount = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@ordIDiscount))
					/*RZ14020626-End*/
				END
			/**/
			END
			/*-------------------------------------------------------*/
			SET @TmpPrice = ISNULL(@ordIPrice,0) - ISNULL(@ordIDiscount,0) - ISNULL(@ordIRound,0) + ISNULL(@ordIRoundPlus,0)			
			SET @TmpIII = ((ISNULL(@ordIPrice,0) - ISNULL(@ordIRound,0) + ISNULL(@ordIRoundPlus,0)) * ISNULL(@TmpI,0)) - ISNULL(@ordIDiscountSum,0)	
			/*-------------------------------------------------------*/
			IF(ISNULL(@ordICalculateTax,0) = 1)
			BEGIN
			/**/
				SET @ordITaxSum = (ISNULL(@TmpIII,0) * ISNULL(@ordITaxIndex,0)) / 100
				SET @ordITax = (ISNULL(@TmpPrice,0) * ISNULL(@ordITaxIndex,0)) / 100	
				IF(ISNULL(@cuUnitIsDecimal,0) = 0)
				BEGIN	
					/*RZ14020626-Change Ceiling to Round*/
					SET @ordITaxSum = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@ordITaxSum))
					SET @ordITax = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@ordITax))
					/*RZ14020626- End*/
				END
			/**/
			END

			/*-------------------------------------------------------*/
			SET @ordIPriceSum = @TmpIII
			SET @ordITotalPrice = ISNULL(@ordIPriceSum,0) + ISNULL(@ordITaxSum,0)
			IF(ISNULL(@cuUnitIsDecimal,0) = 0)
			BEGIN			
				/*RZ14020626-Change Ceiling to Round*/
				SET @ordIPriceSum = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@ordIPriceSum))
				SET @cuUnitIsDecimal = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@cuUnitIsDecimal))
				/*RZ14020626-End*/
			END 
		/**/
		END	
		/*----------------------------------------------------*/
		/*RZ14010531- End*/
		/*----------------------------------------*/

		/*RZ14010319- Add ordIValidityExpireDate*/
		DECLARE @ordIValidityExpireDate DATETIME = NULL,@ordIValidityExpireDatePersian NVARCHAR(8) = NULL
		,@PrdValidityDay INT = NULL,@PrdValidityTime NVARCHAR(10) = NULL,@IsCalcValidityExpireDate BIT = NULL
		,@PrdValidityTimeHour INT = NULL,@PrdValidityTimeMinute INT = NULL		
		SELECT  
				@PrdValidityDay = [PrdValidityDay]
			,@PrdValidityTime = [PrdValidityTime]      
		FROM [dbo].[WSProducts]
		WHERE [ProductID] = @pordIPrdId
		AND ISNULL([PrdValidity],0) = 1

		IF((@PrdValidityDay IS NOT NULL))
		BEGIN
		/**/
			SET @IsCalcValidityExpireDate = 1
		/**/
		END

		IF(ISNULL(@PrdValidityTime,'') <> '')
		BEGIN
		/**/
			SET @IsCalcValidityExpireDate = 1
			SET @PrdValidityTimeHour = CAST((SUBSTRING(@PrdValidityTime,1,2)) AS INT)
			SET @PrdValidityTimeMinute = CAST((SUBSTRING(@PrdValidityTime,4,2)) AS INT)
		/**/
		END

		IF(ISNULL(@IsCalcValidityExpireDate,0) = 1)
		BEGIN
		/**/
			SET @ordIValidityExpireDate = DATEADD(DAY, ISNULL(@PrdValidityDay,0), @OrdDateTime)
			SET @ordIValidityExpireDate = DATEADD(HOUR, ISNULL(@PrdValidityTimeHour,0), @ordIValidityExpireDate)
			SET @ordIValidityExpireDate = DATEADD(MINUTE, ISNULL(@PrdValidityTimeMinute,0), @ordIValidityExpireDate)
			SET @ordIValidityExpireDatePersian = (SELECT dbo.[fnGregorianToPersianDateWithOutSlash](@ordIValidityExpireDate))
		/**/
		END		
		/*RZ14010319- End*/
		/*----------------------------------------*/
		/*RZ14020406- Add ordIPrdValidityCounting*/
		DECLARE @ordIPrdValidityCountingNum INT = NULL,@ordIPrdValidityCountingMin INT = NULL
		SELECT TOP (1) 					
				@ordIPrdValidityCountingNum = (CASE WHEN (ISNULL([PrdValidityCountingNumIsActive],0) = 1)
												THEN [PrdValidityCountingNum]
												ELSE NULL
											END)
			,@ordIPrdValidityCountingMin = (CASE WHEN (ISNULL([PrdValidityCountingMinIsActive],0) = 1)
												THEN [PrdValidityCountingMin]
												ELSE NULL
											END)
		FROM [dbo].[WSProducts]
		WHERE [ProductID] = @pordIPrdId
		/*RZ14020406- End*/
		
		/*----------------------------------------*/
		INSERT INTO dbo.WSOrderItems (
			OrderId,
			ordIPrdId,
			ordIQuantity,
			ordIQuantityDesc,
			ordIPrice,
			ordIDiscount,
			ordIDiscountI,
			ordITotalPrice,
			ordIDescription,
			/*--*/
			ordIDiscountPercent,
			ordIDiscountIPercent,
			ordIRound,
			/*9512*/
			ordIRoundPlus,
			ordIPrdUnitId,
			ordICurrencyUnitId,
			ordIFCR, ordIFCRI,
			ordIOrderView,
			ordITax, 
			ordITaxIndex, 
			ordICalculateTax, 
			ordINotVisible, 
			ordINotInSum,
			UserId,	
			FPId, 
			Month1, 
			RegisterDate,
			RegisterDatePersian, 
			AppType, 
			ordIWHId,	
			/*ADBF951130*/	
			LanguageID,
			FirstRegisterDatePersian,
			FirstRegisterUserId,
			FirstRegisterDate 
			/*ADBF990126*/
			, ordIPrdPriceGroupID
			,ordITracing /*RZ990318- Add Tracing*/
			/*RZ990815- Add fields to orderItems and preorderItems*/
			,[ordIPriceSum]
			,[ordIDiscountISum]
			,[ordIDiscountSum]
			,[ordITaxSum]
			/*RZ990815- End*/
			/*RZ990916- Add fields and commission*/
			,[ordIDeliveryTermId]
			,[ordIDeliveryDate]
			,[ordIDeliveryDatePersian]
			/*RZ990916- End*/
			/*RZ14010319- Add ordIValidityExpireDate*/
			/*,[ordIValidityExpireDate]
			,[ordIValidityExpireDatePersian]*/		/*RZ14020510*/
			/*RZ14010319- End*/
			,[ordIQuantityTotal] /*RZ14010524- Add QuantityTotal*/
			,[ordIPrdColorId] /*RZ14010612- Add prdColorId*/
			/*RZ14010620- Add PrdUnitIdI*/
			,[ordIPrdUnitIdI]
			,[ordIPrdUnitIRateResult]
			/*RZ14010620- End*/	
			,[ordIPrdNickname] /*RZ14011021- Add PrdNickname*/
			/*RZ14020324- Add new fields*/
			,[ordIDiscountDesc]
			,[ordIDeliveryTime]
			,[ordIDeliveryDay]
			,[ordIDeliveryEndDate]
			,[ordIDeliveryEndDatePersian]
			,[ordIDeliveryEndTime]
			,[ordIDeliveryEndDay] 			
			/*RZ14020324- End*/
			/*RZ14020406- Add ordIPrdValidityCounting*/
			,[ordIPrdValidityCountingNum] 
			,[ordIPrdValidityCountingMin]
			/*RZ14020406- End*/
			,[ordILinkAccWHIdStr] /*RZ14020427- Add pordILinkAccWHIdStr*/
			/*RZ14020510- Add fields*/
			,[ordIValidityExpireDate]
			,[ordIValidityExpireDatePersian]			
			/*RZ14020528- Change PrdAutoBill*/	
			,[ordIPrdAutoCalcRate]
			,[ordIPrdAutoCalcInflationRateInfo]
			/*RZ14020528- End*/	
			,[ordIDescriptionI]
			/*RZ14020510- End*/
			,[PreOrderItemId]/*RZ14021202- Add PreOrderItemId to WSOrderItems*/	
		) 
		VALUES( 
			@OrderId,
			@pordIPrdId, 
			@ordIQuantity,/*@pordIQuantity*/ /*RZ14010531- Add project and add manual quantity for every product in multi order*/
			@pordIQuantityDesc,
			@ordIPrice, /*@pordIPrice*//*RZ14010531- Add project and add manual quantity for every product in multi order*/
			@ordIDiscount,/*@pordIDiscount*/ /*RZ14010531- Add project and add manual quantity for every product in multi order*/
			@pordIDiscountI,
			@ordITotalPrice,/*@pordITotalPrice*//*RZ14010531- Add project and add manual quantity for every product in multi order*/ 
			@pordIDescription, 
			/*--*/
			@ordIDiscountPercent,/*@pordIDiscountPercent*//*RZ14010531- Add project and add manual quantity for every product in multi order*/
			@pordIDiscountIPercent,
			@ordIRound,/*@pordIRound*//*RZ14010531- Add project and add manual quantity for every product in multi order*/
			/*9512*/
			@ordIRoundPlus,/*@pordIRoundPlus*//*RZ14010531- Add project and add manual quantity for every product in multi order*/
			@pordIPrdUnitId,
			@pordICurrencyUnitId,
			@ordIFCR,/*@pordIFCR*/ /*RZ14010531- Add project and add manual quantity for every product in multi order*/
			@ordIFCRI,/*@pordIFCRI*//*RZ14010531- Add project and add manual quantity for every product in multi order*/
			@ordIOrderView,/*@pordIOrderView*//*RZ14010531- Add project and add manual quantity for every product in multi order*/
			@ordITax, /*@pordITax*//*RZ14010531- Add project and add manual quantity for every product in multi order*/
			@ordITaxIndex,/*@pordITaxIndex*/ /*RZ14010531- Add project and add manual quantity for every product in multi order*/
			@ordICalculateTax,/*@pordICalculateTax*/ /*RZ14010531- Add project and add manual quantity for every product in multi order*/
			@pordINotVisible, 
			@pordINotInSum,
			@UserId, 
			@FPId,
			@Month1, 
			GETDATE(), 
			@RegisterDatePersian, 
			@AppType, 
			@pordIWHId,
			/*ADBF951130*/	
			@LanguageID,
			@RegisterDatePersian,
			@UserId,
			GETDATE()
			/*ADBF990126*/
			, @pordIPrdPriceGroupID
			,@pordITracing /*RZ990318- Add Tracing*/
			/*RZ990815- Add fields to orderItems and preorderItems*/
			,@ordIPriceSum /*@pordIPriceSum*/ /*RZ14010531- Add project and add manual quantity for every product in multi order*/
			,@pordIDiscountISum
			,@ordIDiscountSum/*@pordIDiscountSum*//*RZ14010531- Add project and add manual quantity for every product in multi order*/
			,@ordITaxSum /*@pordITaxSum*//*RZ14010531- Add project and add manual quantity for every product in multi order*/
			/*RZ990815- End*/
			/*RZ990916- Add fields and commission*/
			,@pordIDeliveryTermId
			,@pordIDeliveryDate
			,@pordIDeliveryDatePersian
			/*RZ990916- End*/
			/*RZ14010319- Add ordIValidityExpireDate*/
			/*,@ordIValidityExpireDate
			,@ordIValidityExpireDatePersian	*/ /*RZ14020510*/
			/*RZ14010319- End*/
			,@ordIQuantityTotal /*@pordIQuantityTotal*//*RZ14010524- Add QuantityTotal*//*RZ14010531- Add project and add manual quantity for every product in multi order*/
			,@pordIPrdColorId /*RZ14010612- Add prdColorId*/
			/*RZ14010620- Add PrdUnitIdI*/
			,@pordIPrdUnitIdI
			,@pordIPrdUnitIRateResult
			/*RZ14010620- End*/
			,@pordIPrdNickname /*RZ14011021- Add PrdNickname*/
			/*RZ14020324- Add new fields*/
			,@pordIDiscountDesc
			,@pordIDeliveryTime
			,@pordIDeliveryDay
			,@pordIDeliveryEndDate
			,@pordIDeliveryEndDatePersian
			,@pordIDeliveryEndTime
			,@pordIDeliveryEndDay			
			/*RZ14020324- End*/
			/*RZ14020406- Add ordIPrdValidityCounting*/
			,@ordIPrdValidityCountingNum 
			,@ordIPrdValidityCountingMin
			/*RZ14020406- End*/
			,@pordILinkAccWHIdStr /*RZ14020427- Add pordILinkAccWHIdStr*/
			/*RZ14020510- Add fields*/
			,@pordIValidityExpireDate
			,@pordIValidityExpireDatePersian			
			/*RZ14020528- Change PrdAutoBill*/	
			,@pordIPrdAutoCalcRate
			,@pordIPrdAutoCalcInflationRateInfo
			/*RZ14020528- End*/	
			,@pordIDescriptionI
			/*RZ14020510- End*/
			,@PreOrderItemID/*RZ14021202- Add PreOrderItemId to WSOrderItems*/	
		)

		SET @ItemId = SCOPE_IDENTITY()
		/*Insert records in lang table*/
		INSERT INTO dbo.WSOrderItems_Lang (
			OrderItemId
			,LanguageID
			,ordIDescription
			,ordIQuantityDesc	
			,ordIPrdNickname /*RZ14011021- Add PrdNickname*/
			/*RZ14020324- Add new fields*/
			,[ordIDiscountDesc]			
			/*RZ14020324- End*/
			/*RZ14020510- Add fields*/
		,[ordIDescriptionI]
		/*RZ14020510- End*/
		) 
		SELECT
			@ItemId,
			POILN.LanguageID,
			POILN.pordIDescription,
			POILN.pordIQuantityDesc,
			POILN.pordIPrdNickname /*RZ14011021- Add PrdNickname*/
			/*RZ14020324- Add new fields*/
			,POILN.[pordIDiscountDesc]				
			/*RZ14020324- End*/
			/*RZ14020510- Add fields*/			
			,[pordIDescriptionI]
			/*RZ14020510- End*/
		FROM dbo.WSPreOrderItems_Lang POILN 
		WHERE  POILN.PreOrderItemID = @PreOrderItemID
		
		FETCH NEXT FROM Crsr_Add_Item INTO  @PreOrderItemID, @pordIPrdId, @pordIQuantity, 
								@pordIQuantityDesc, @pordIPrice, @pordIDiscount, @pordIDiscountI,
								@pordITotalPrice, @pordIDescription, @pordIDiscountPercent, @pordIDiscountIPercent, 
								@pordIRound, @pordIRoundPlus, @pordIPrdUnitId, @pordICurrencyUnitId, @pordIFCR,
								@pordIFCRI, @pordIOrderView, @pordITax, @pordITaxIndex, @pordICalculateTax,
								@pordINotVisible, @pordINotInSum, @pordIWHId
								/*ADBF990126*/
								, @pordIPrdPriceGroupID,@pordITracing /*RZ990318- Add Tracing*/
								,@pordIPriceSum,@pordIDiscountSum,@pordIDiscountISum,@pordITaxSum/*RZ990815- Add fields to orderItems and preorderItems*/
								,@pordIDeliveryTermId,@pordIDeliveryDate,@pordIDeliveryDatePersian/*RZ990916- Add fields and commission*/
								,@pordIQuantityTotal /*RZ14010524- Add QuantityTotal*/
								,@pordIPrdColorId /*RZ14010612- Add prdColorId*/
								,@pordIPrdUnitIdI,@pordIPrdUnitIRateResult/*RZ14010620- Add PrdUnitIdI*/
								,@pordIPrdNickname /*RZ14011021- Add PrdNickname*/
								/*RZ14020324- Add new fields*/
								,@pordIDiscountDesc,@pordIDeliveryTime,@pordIDeliveryDay,@pordIDeliveryEndDate
								,@pordIDeliveryEndDatePersian,@pordIDeliveryEndTime,@pordIDeliveryEndDay 			
								/*RZ14020324- End*/
								,@pordILinkAccWHIdStr /*RZ14020427- Add pordILinkAccWHIdStr*/
								/*RZ14020510- Add fields*/
								,@pordIValidityExpireDate,@pordIValidityExpireDatePersian
								,@pordIPrdAutoCalcRate,@pordIPrdAutoCalcInflationRateInfo/*RZ14020528- Change PrdAutoBill*/								
								,@pordIDescriptionI
								/*RZ14020510- End*/

	/**/
	END
	CLOSE Crsr_Add_Item
	DEALLOCATE Crsr_Add_Item
	/*----------------- END user cursor for insert items in order -------------------------------*/

	/*------------------- tran2*/
	IF (@@ERROR > 0)
	BEGIN 
		SET @ErrorFrom='Insert Into WSOrdersItems' 
		SET @ErrorNumber=@@ERROR 
		/*RZ960218 for multi language And add length variable*/
		CLOSE Crsr_Add_Item
		DEALLOCATE Crsr_Add_Item
	ROLLBACK TRAN 
	END		
	ELSE 
	COMMIT TRAN
	/*------------------- tran2-----------------------*/
	/*------------------- tran2
	--==============================================
	--==============================================
	--==============================================
	------------------- tran3*/
	BEGIN TRAN
	/*------------------- tran3*/
	/*RZ960218 for multi language And add length variable*/
	/*----------------- Begin user cursor for insert items in order -----------------------------*/
	/*-------------------------- Begin define variables ------------------------------*/
	DECLARE @pordpayTypeId INT
	DECLARE @pordpayDocId INT
	DECLARE @pordpayDocTypeId INT
	DECLARE @pordpayAmount NUMERIC(18,2) /*RZ991008- Convert Numeric(18,0) to Numeric(18,2)*/
	DECLARE @pordpayDocNumber NVARCHAR(50)
	DECLARE @pordpayDocDate NVARCHAR(8)
	DECLARE @pordpayDocAmount NUMERIC(18,2)/*RZ991008- Convert Numeric(18,0) to Numeric(18,2)*/
	/*RZ960829- add length nvarchar to max*/
	DECLARE @pordpayDescription NVARCHAR(MAX)
	DECLARE @pordpayFirstRegisterUserId INT
	DECLARE @pordpayFirstRegisteDatePersian NVARCHAR(8)
	DECLARE @pordpayFirstRegisteDate DATETIME
	DECLARE @pordpayDocDateTime DATETIME
	DECLARE @PreOrderPaymentId INT
	DECLARE @PayId INT
	/*RZ14000225- Modify ImportDate in OrderPayment*/
	DECLARE @pordpayImportDate NVARCHAR(8),
	@pordpayImportDateTime DATETIME,
	/*RZ14000225- End*/
	@pordpayDeActive BIT /*RZ14010916- Add ordpayDeActive*/
	/*RZ14020129- Add fields to payment*/
	,@pordpayAmountTemp NUMERIC(18,2)
	/*RZ14020129- End*/
	,@pordpayAccountNumber NVARCHAR(50)/*RZ14020530- Add pordpayAccountNumber*/
	/*RZ14030129- Add ordpayDocDateTemp*/
	,@pordpayDocDateTemp NVARCHAR(8) = NULL,@pordpayDocDateTimeTemp DATETIME = NULL

				,[ImportDate]

			WHERE pordpayPreOrderId = @PreOrderID

											FROM dbo.WSOrder O

											@pordpayDocAmount, @pordpayDescription, @pordpayFirstRegisterUserId,

				/*RZ14020129- Add fields to payment*/
				@pordpayAmountTemp,
				/*RZ14020129- End*/
				@pordpayAccountNumber/*RZ14020530- Add pordpayAccountNumber*/
				/*RZ14030129- Add ordpayDocDateTemp*/
				,@pordpayDocDateTemp
				,@pordpayDocDateTimeTemp
				/*RZ14030129- End*/
			)

			/*Insert records in lang table*/
			INSERT INTO dbo.WSOrderPayments_Lang (
			   OrderPaymentId

			  ,ordpayDescription		 	    			  
			)

			FROM dbo.WSPreOrderPayments_Lang POPLN 
			WHERE  POPLN.PreOrderPaymentId = @PreOrderPaymentId
		
			FETCH NEXT FROM Crsr_Add_Payment INTO  @pordpayTypeId, @pordpayDocId, @pordpayDocTypeId,
											@pordpayAmount, @pordpayDocNumber, @pordpayDocDate, 					
											@pordpayDocAmount, @pordpayDescription, @pordpayFirstRegisterUserId,
											@pordpayFirstRegisteDatePersian, @pordpayFirstRegisteDate, @pordpayDocDateTime, @PreOrderPaymentId,

											/*RZ14030129- Add ordpayDocDateTemp*/
											,@pordpayDocDateTemp,@pordpayDocDateTimeTemp
											/*RZ14030129- End*/
		END
		CLOSE Crsr_Add_Payment
		DEALLOCATE Crsr_Add_Payment

						,@ordVerifierId
						,[ordSettlementTypeId]
						,[ordDay]
						,[ordTime]
						,[ordReferUserId]
						,[ordDiscountTitle]
						,[ordNote]
						,[ordcmpTitle]
						,[ordDateTime]
						,[ordImportDateTime]
						,[ordDeliveryDate]
						,[ordDeliveryDateTime]
						,[ordDeliveryTime]
						,[AppType]
						,[ordVerifierRequestId]
						,[LanguageID]
						,[FirstRegisterUserId]
						,[FirstRegisterDatePersian]
						,[FirstRegisterDate]
						,[ordVerificationWorkFlowId]
						,[ordCurrencyUnitId]
						,[ordDeliveryTermId]
						,[ordDeliveryEndDate]
						,[ordDeliveryEndDateTime]
						,[ordDeliveryEndTime]
						,[ordDefWHId]
						,[ordDefPrdPriceGroupId]
						,[ordSellerId]
						,[ordEmailUsersInfoId]
						,[ordEmailUserDefaultSettingId]
						,[ordSaleCenterId]
						,[ordDefPayTypeId]
						,@IndexedOrdDailyCounter
						,[ordDeadlineDate]
						,[ordDeadlineDatePersian]
						,[ordTspSettingId]
						,[ordDefaultSettingId]
						,[ordDeliveryDay]
						,[ordDeliveryEndDay]
						/*RZ14030301- Add pordCurrencyUnitIdI and amounts*/
						,[ordCurrencyUnitIdI]
						,[ordCurrencyUnitIRate]
						/*RZ14030301- End*/
					FROM [dbo].[WSOrder]
					WHERE OrderID = @InsertedOrderID

					SET @IndexedOrdId = SCOPE_IDENTITY()

					IF(ISNULL(@IndexedOrdId ,0) > 0)
					BEGIN
					/**/
						/*------------------------------------*/	
						/*------------ MultiLang -------------*/	
						INSERT INTO [dbo].[WSOrder_Lang]
							   ([OrderID]
							   ,[ordTitle]
							   ,[ordSubject]
							   ,[ordSpecialWord]
							   ,[ordDescriptionI]
							   ,[ordDescriptionII]
							   ,[ordSigningI]
							   ,[ordSigningII]
							   ,[ordSigningIII]
							   ,[ordDiscountTitle]
							   ,[ordNote]
							   ,[ordcmpTitle]
							   ,[LanguageID])
						 SELECT
								@IndexedOrdId
							   ,[ordTitle]
							   ,[ordSubject]
							   ,[ordSpecialWord]
							   ,[ordDescriptionI]
							   ,[ordDescriptionII]
							   ,[ordSigningI]
							   ,[ordSigningII]
							   ,[ordSigningIII]
							   ,[ordDiscountTitle]
							   ,[ordNote]
							   ,[ordcmpTitle]
							   ,[LanguageID]
							FROM [dbo].[WSOrder_Lang]
							WHERE [OrderID] = @InsertedOrderID
						/*------------------------------------*/	
						/*------------ OrderItem -------------*/
						DECLARE @IndexedOrderItemId INT = NULL,
						@IndexedOrdIPrdId INT = NULL,@IndexedOrdIQuantity FLOAT = NULL,
						@IndexedOrdICurrencyUnitId INT = NULL,@IndexedOrdIOrderView INT = NULL,
						@IndexedOrdIWHId INT = NULL,@IndexedOrdIPrdPriceGroupID INT = NULL,
						@IndexedOrdIPrdNickname NVARCHAR(1000) = NULL,
						@IndexedOrdIValidityExpireDate DATETIME = NULL,@IndexedOrdIValidityExpireDatePersian NVARCHAR(8) = NULL,
						@IndexedOrdIPrdAutoCalcRate NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,@IndexedOrdIPrdAutoCalcInflationRateInfo NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
						@IndexedOrdItemCount INT = NULL
						/**/					
						/*------------------------------------*/
						SELECT TOP(1) 
							 @IndexedOrdICurrencyUnitId = ordCurrencyUnitId
							,@IndexedOrdIWHId = ordDefWHId
							,@IndexedOrdIPrdPriceGroupID = ordDefPrdPriceGroupId
						FROM [dbo].[WSOrder]
						WHERE OrderID = @IndexedOrdId
						/*---------*/
						IF(ISNULL(@IndexedOrdIWHId,0) = 0)
						BEGIN
						/**/
							SET @IndexedOrdIWHId = (SELECT TOP 1 [WareHouseID] FROM [dbo].[WsWareHouse] WHERE ISNULL(WrhsDeActive,0) = 0)
						/**/
						END
						/*--------*/
						IF(ISNULL(@IndexedOrdIPrdPriceGroupID,0) = 0)
						BEGIN
						/**/
							SET @IndexedOrdIPrdPriceGroupID = (SELECT TOP 1 ProductPriceGroupId FROM dbo.WSProductPriceGroup ORDER BY OrderView)
						/**/
						END
						/*--------*/
						SET @IndexedOrdIPrdId = @PrdAutoCalcIndexedPrdId
						SET @IndexedOrdIQuantity = 1
						/*Set OrderView*/
						SET @IndexedOrdItemCount = (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM [dbo].[WSOrderItems] OI WHERE OI.OrderId = @IndexedOrdId)
						SET @IndexedOrdIOrderView = (@IndexedOrdItemCount + 1) + (@IndexedOrdItemCount * 4 ) - 1
						/*-------*/
						SET @PrdValidityDay = NULL
						SET @PrdValidityTime = NULL
						SET @IsCalcValidityExpireDate = NULL
						SET @PrdValidityTimeHour = NULL
						SET @PrdValidityTimeMinute = NULL
						/**/
						SELECT  
							 @PrdValidityDay = [PrdValidityDay]
							,@PrdValidityTime = [PrdValidityTime] 
							,@IndexedOrdIPrdAutoCalcRate = [PrdAutoCalcPriceFromOrdItemRate]/*RZ14020621- Change fields name*/
							,@IndexedOrdIPrdAutoCalcInflationRateInfo = [PrdAutoCalcInflationLastDurationRate]/*RZ14020621- Change fields name*/
						FROM [dbo].[WSProducts]
						WHERE [ProductID] = @IndexedOrdIPrdId
						AND ISNULL([PrdValidity],0) = 1

						IF((@PrdValidityDay IS NOT NULL))
						BEGIN
						/**/
							SET @IsCalcValidityExpireDate = 1
						/**/
						END

						IF(ISNULL(@PrdValidityTime,'') <> '')
						BEGIN
						/**/
							SET @IsCalcValidityExpireDate = 1
							SET @PrdValidityTimeHour = CAST((SUBSTRING(@PrdValidityTime,1,2)) AS INT)
							SET @PrdValidityTimeMinute = CAST((SUBSTRING(@PrdValidityTime,4,2)) AS INT)
						/**/
						END

						IF(ISNULL(@IsCalcValidityExpireDate,0) = 1)
						BEGIN
						/**/
							SET @IndexedOrdIValidityExpireDate = DATEADD(DAY, ISNULL(@PrdValidityDay,0), @OrdDateTime)
							SET @IndexedOrdIValidityExpireDate = DATEADD(HOUR, ISNULL(@PrdValidityTimeHour,0), @IndexedOrdIValidityExpireDate)
							SET @IndexedOrdIValidityExpireDate = DATEADD(MINUTE, ISNULL(@PrdValidityTimeMinute,0), @IndexedOrdIValidityExpireDate)
							SET @IndexedOrdIValidityExpireDatePersian = (SELECT dbo.[fnGregorianToPersianDateWithOutSlash](@IndexedOrdIValidityExpireDate))
						/**/
						END		
						/*------------------------------------*/						
						INSERT INTO [dbo].[WSOrderItems]
							([OrderId]
							,[ordIPrdId]
							,[ordIQuantity]
							,[ordIPrice]
							,[ordITotalPrice]
							,[UserId]
							,[FPId]
							,[Month1]
							,[RegisterDate]
							,[RegisterDatePersian]						
							,[ordICurrencyUnitId]
							,[ordIOrderView]						
							,[ordIWHId]
							,[AppType]
							,[LanguageID]
							,[FirstRegisterUserId]
							,[FirstRegisterDatePersian]
							,[FirstRegisterDate]
							,[ordIPrdPriceGroupID]
							,[ordIPriceSum]						
							,[ordIValidityExpireDate]
							,[ordIValidityExpireDatePersian]
							,[ordIPrdNickname]						
							,[ordIPrdAutoCalcRate]
							,[ordIPrdAutoCalcInflationRateInfo])
						VALUES
							(@IndexedOrdId
							,@IndexedOrdIPrdId
							,@IndexedOrdIQuantity
							,0
							,0
							,@UserId
							,@FPId
							,@Month1
							,@RegisterDate
							,@RegisterDatePersian					
							,@IndexedOrdICurrencyUnitId
							,@IndexedOrdIOrderView						
							,@IndexedOrdIWHId
							,@AppType
							,@LanguageID
							,@UserId
							,@RegisterDatePersian
							,@RegisterDate
							,@IndexedOrdIPrdPriceGroupID
							,0						
							,@IndexedOrdIValidityExpireDate
							,@IndexedOrdIValidityExpireDatePersian
							,@IndexedOrdIPrdNickname						
							,@IndexedOrdIPrdAutoCalcRate
							,@IndexedOrdIPrdAutoCalcInflationRateInfo)
						SET @IndexedOrderItemId = SCOPE_IDENTITY()
						IF(ISNULL(@IndexedOrderItemId,0) > 0 )
						BEGIN
						/**/
							INSERT INTO [dbo].[WSOrderItems_Lang]
								([OrderItemID]
								,[LanguageID]
								,[ordIPrdNickname])
							SELECT 
								 @IndexedOrderItemId
								,[LanguageID]
								,[PrdNickname]
							FROM [dbo].[WSProducts_Lang]
							WHERE ProductID = @IndexedOrdIPrdId
						/**/
						ENd
						/*------------------------------------*/
						IF(ISNULL(@InsertedOrderID,0) > 0)
						BEGIN
						/**/
							EXEC [dbo].[MobspCalcAndUpdateOrderAmounts] /*AI14020705*/
							@OrderId = @IndexedOrdId,
							@Month1 = @Month1,
							@LanguageID = @LanguageID,
							@DefaultLanguageID = @LanguageID
						/**/
						END
						/*------------------------------------*/	
					/**/
					END
					/*------------------------------------*/		
				/*------------------- tran6 --------------------*/
				IF (@@ERROR > 0)
				BEGIN 
					SET @ErrorFrom='Insert Into WSOrder For PrdIndexed' 
					SET @ErrorNumber=@@ERROR 				
				ROLLBACK TRAN 
				END		
				ELSE 
				COMMIT TRAN 		
				/*----------------------------------------*/
				/*----------------------------------------------*/
			/**/
			END
			/*----------------------------------------------*/
		/**/
		END				
		/*----------------------------------------------*/
		/*----------------------------------------------*/
		/*---------------- OrdIndex END ----------------*/
		/*----------------------------------------------*/
		/*----------------------------------------------*/
		/*-------- Update prjRemindDate START ----------*/
		/*----------------------------------------------*/
		SET @ProductId = NULL
		SET @PrdValidityUpdatePrjRemindDateAfterIssueOrd = NULL
		SET @prjRemindDate = NULL
		SET @prjRemindDateTime = NULL
		/*----------------------------------------------*/
		/*------- Find product validty for update prjRemindDate ---------*/
		SELECT TOP(1) 
			 @ProductId = ProductID
			,@PrdValidityUpdatePrjRemindDateAfterIssueOrd = [PrdValidityUpdatePrjRemindDateAfterIssueOrd]			
		FROM [dbo].[WSProducts] P
		WHERE ProductID IN(SELECT TOP(1) pordIPrdId			
							FROM dbo.WSPreOrderItems
							WHERE PreOrderId = @PreOrderID
							AND PreOrderItemID IN(SELECT [Id]
												FROM OPENJSON(@PordItemList)
												WITH (
													[Id] INT '$.Id',
													[Value] FLOAT '$.Value'	
												) AS JS)				  			
							ORDER BY ISNULL(pordIOrderView, PreOrderItemID))
		AND ISNULL([PrdValidityUpdatePrjRemindDateAfterIssueOrd],0) = 1
		AND ISNULL([PrdValidity],0) = 1
		/*----------------------------------------------*/
		IF(ISNULL(@ProductId,0) <> 0 AND ISNULL(@PrdValidityUpdatePrjRemindDateAfterIssueOrd,0) =1)
		BEGIN
		/**/
			SELECT TOP(1)
				 @prjRemindDateTime = ordIValidityExpireDate
				,@prjRemindDate = ordIValidityExpireDatePersian
			FROM [dbo].[WSOrderItems]
			WHERE OrderId = @InsertedOrderID
			AND ordIPrdId = @ProductId
			AND ordIValidityExpireDate IS NOT NULL
			/**/
			IF(@prjRemindDateTime IS NOT NULL)
			BEGIN
			/**/
				UPDATE [dbo].[CrmProjects]
				SET prjRemindDateTime = @prjRemindDateTime
					,prjRemindDate = @prjRemindDate
				WHERE ProjectID = @OrdProjectId
			/**/
			END
		/**/
		END			   
		/*----------------------------------------------*/
		/*-------- Update prjRemindDate END ------------*/
		/*----------------------------------------------*/
		/*----------------------------------------------*/
	/**/
	END	
	/*RZ14020531- End*/
	/*----------------------------------------------*/
	/*----------------------------------------------*/
/**/
END

ELSE IF(ISNULL(@OrdProjectId,0) = 0)/*RZ14020531- Add person and issue orderindex*/
BEGIN
/**/
	RETURN -2
/**/
END
/*---------------------------------------------------*/
/*
--==============================================
--==============================================
--==============================================
--************/
IF (@ErrorNumber > 0)
begin
	RAISERROR('Error From TransAction %S', 16, 1, @ErrorFrom)
	ROLLBACK TRANSACTION Main
	Set @OrderId = 0
	Return @OrderId
end
ELSE
BEGIN
	COMMIT TRANSACTION Main
	Return @OrderId
END	
/*--****/
END

-- [dbo].[MobspOrderVerifyWithPreChecks]






/*RZ971011*/
/*RZ971114- Change price to decimals*/
/*AI14001030 - Modify Calculate discount in item*/
/*AI14010522 - Changed sp name from MobspOrderVerify because it contains pre checks*/
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[MobspOrderVerifyWithPreChecks]
/* 
	DECLARE	@Result INT, @Limit NUMERIC(18,2)
	EXEC MobspOrderVerifyWithPreChecks 17362, 1, 3, '14000811', '2021-11-02 12:48:17', @LimitOrderDiscountPercent = @Limit OUTPUT, @Result = @Result OUTPUT
	SELECT @Result as Result, @Limit as Limit
*/		
(
@OrderId INT,
@RollId INT,
@VerifierUserId INT,
@RegisterDatePersian VARCHAR(20),
@RegisterDate DATETIME,
@LimitOrderDiscountPercent NUMERIC(18,2) OUTPUT,
@Result INT OUTPUT
)
AS
BEGIN
/**/
/**************** Find MaxDiscountPercent For Roll ********************/
	DECLARE @LimitOrderDiscountActive BIT

	SELECT 
		@LimitOrderDiscountActive = LimitOrderDiscountActive, 
		@LimitOrderDiscountPercent = LimitOrderDiscountPercent
	FROM dbo.CrmsysRolls
	WHERE ID = @RollId

	IF(@LimitOrderDiscountPercent > 0 AND @LimitOrderDiscountPercent IS NOT NULL AND ISNULL(@LimitOrderDiscountActive,0) = 1)
	BEGIN
		DECLARE @SumPriceAmount NUMERIC(18, 2) = 0, /*RZ971114- Change price to decimals*/
				@SumDiscount NUMERIC(18, 2) = 0 , 
				@ordDiscount NUMERIC(18, 2) = 0 ,
				@DiscountPercent NUMERIC(18, 2) = 0 
				/*AI14001030- Modify Calculate discount in item*/
				,@TotalPriceSumWithoutDiscount NUMERIC(18,2) = 0
				,@TotalDiscountSum NUMERIC(18,2) = 0
				/*AI14001030- End*/	
		SELECT @ordDiscount = [ordDiscount] FROM [dbo].[WSOrder] O WHERE O.[OrderID] = @OrderId		
		SELECT 
			 @SumPriceAmount = [SumPriceAmount] 
			,@SumDiscount = [SumDiscount] 
			/*AI14001030- Modify Calculate discount in item*/
			,@TotalPriceSumWithoutDiscount = TotalPriceSumWithoutDiscount 
			,@TotalDiscountSum = TotalDiscountSum
			/*AI14001030- End*/ 
		FROM [dbo].[MobfnReturnOrderSumFooter](@OrderId) 
	
		IF(@SumPriceAmount IS NOT NULL AND @SumPriceAmount > 0 AND ISNULL(@TotalPriceSumWithoutDiscount,0) <> 0)/*AI14001030- Modify Calculate discount in item*/
		BEGIN 
			/*SET @DiscountPercent = (@SumDiscount * 100) / @SumPriceAmount*/
			SET @DiscountPercent = (ISNULL(@TotalDiscountSum,0) * 100) / ISNULL(@TotalPriceSumWithoutDiscount,0)/*AI14001030- Modify Calculate discount in item*/

			IF(@DiscountPercent > @LimitOrderDiscountPercent)
			BEGIN			
				SET @Result = -2 /*MaxDiscountPercentInItems*/								
			END
			ELSE
			BEGIN
				SET @DiscountPercent = 0
				SET @DiscountPercent = (ISNULL(@ordDiscount,0) * 100) / @SumPriceAmount				
				IF(@DiscountPercent > @LimitOrderDiscountPercent)
				BEGIN
					SET @Result = -3 /*MaxDiscountPercentInOrder*/					
				END
				ELSE
				BEGIN
					SET @Result = 1
				END				
			END			
		END
		ELSE
			SET @Result = -1 /*DivisionByZero*/		
	END
	ELSE
		SET @Result = 1 				                                               
	/**************** Order Verify *************************************/
	IF(@Result = 1)
	BEGIN
		UPDATE dbo.WSOrder
		SET 
		ordIsVerified = 1,
		ordVerifierId = @VerifierUserId,
		ordIsFixed = 1,
		ordVerifiedRegisterDate = @RegisterDate,
		ordVerifiedRegisterDatePersian = @RegisterDatePersian
		WHERE OrderID = @OrderId					
	END	
/**/
END

-- [dbo].[MobspPreOrderVerifyExit]






/*RZ970508*/
/*AI14011005 - Set NULL VerifiedRegisterDate*/
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[MobspPreOrderVerifyExit]
/* 
	EXEC MobspPreOrderVerifyExit  2084, 3, '13970507','2018-07-29'
*/		
(
@PreOrderId INT,
@VerifierUserId INT,
@RegisterDatePersian VARCHAR(20),
@RegisterDate DATETIME
)
AS
BEGIN
/**/
	UPDATE dbo.WSPreOrder
		SET 
		pordIsVerified = 0,
		pordVerifierId = NULL,
		pordIsFixed = 0,
		/*AI14011005*/
		/*pordVerifiedRegisterDate = @RegisterDate,
		pordVerifiedRegisterDatePersian = @RegisterDatePersian*/
		pordVerifiedRegisterDate = NULL,
		pordVerifiedRegisterDatePersian = NULL
		/*AI14011005-End*/
	WHERE PreOrderID = @PreOrderId
/**/
END







GO
-- [dbo].[MobspPreOrderVerifyWithPreChecks]







/*RZ970312*/
/*RZ971114- Change price to decimals*/
/*AI14001030 - Modify Calculate discount in item*/
/*AI14010522 - Changed sp name from MobspPreOrderVerify because it contains pre checks*/
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[MobspPreOrderVerifyWithPreChecks]
/* 
	EXEC MobspPreOrderVerifyWithPreChecks  		
*/		
(
@PreOrderId INT,
@RollId INT,
@VerifierUserId INT,
@RegisterDatePersian VARCHAR(20),
@RegisterDate DATETIME,
@LimitPreOrderDiscountPercent NUMERIC(18,2) OUTPUT,
@Result INT OUTPUT
)
AS
BEGIN
/**/
/**************** Find MaxDiscountPercent For Roll ********************/
	DECLARE @LimitPreOrderDiscountActive BIT

	SELECT 
		@LimitPreOrderDiscountActive = LimitPreOrderDiscountActive, 
		@LimitPreOrderDiscountPercent = LimitPreOrderDiscountPercent
	FROM dbo.CrmsysRolls
	WHERE ID = @RollId

	IF(@LimitPreOrderDiscountPercent > 0 AND @LimitPreOrderDiscountPercent IS NOT NULL AND ISNULL(@LimitPreOrderDiscountActive,0) = 1)
	BEGIN
		DECLARE @SumPriceAmount NUMERIC(18, 2) = 0, /*RZ971114- Change price to decimals*/
				@SumDiscount NUMERIC(18, 2) = 0 , 
				@PordDiscount NUMERIC(18, 2) = 0 ,
				@DiscountPercent NUMERIC(18, 2) = 0
				/*AI14001030- Modify Calculate discount in item*/
				,@TotalPriceSumWithoutDiscount NUMERIC(18,2) = 0
				,@TotalDiscountSum NUMERIC(18,2) = 0
				/*AI14001030- End*/ 	
		SELECT @PordDiscount = [pordDiscount] FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrder] PO WHERE PO.[PreOrderID] = @PreOrderId		
		SELECT 
			 @SumPriceAmount = [SumPriceAmount] 
			,@SumDiscount = [SumDiscount]
			/*AI14001030- Modify Calculate discount in item*/
			,@TotalPriceSumWithoutDiscount = TotalPriceSumWithoutDiscount 
			,@TotalDiscountSum = TotalDiscountSum
			/*AI14001030- End*/ 
		FROM [dbo].[MobfnReturnPreOrderSumFooter](@PreOrderId) 
	
		IF(@SumPriceAmount IS NOT NULL AND @SumPriceAmount > 0 AND ISNULL(@TotalPriceSumWithoutDiscount,0) <> 0)/*AI14001030- Modify Calculate discount in item*/
		BEGIN 
			/*SET @DiscountPercent = (@SumDiscount * 100) / @SumPriceAmount*/
			
			SET @DiscountPercent = (ISNULL(@TotalDiscountSum,0) * 100) / ISNULL(@TotalPriceSumWithoutDiscount,0)/*AI14001030- Modify Calculate discount in item*/


			IF(@DiscountPercent > @LimitPreOrderDiscountPercent)
			BEGIN			
				SET @Result = -2 /*MaxDiscountPercentInItems*/								
			END
			ELSE
			BEGIN
				SET @DiscountPercent = 0
				SET @DiscountPercent = (ISNULL(@PordDiscount,0) * 100) / @SumPriceAmount				
				IF(@DiscountPercent > @LimitPreOrderDiscountPercent)
				BEGIN
					SET @Result = -3 /*MaxDiscountPercentInPreOrder*/					
				END
				ELSE
				BEGIN
					SET @Result = 1
				END				
			END			
		END
		ELSE
			SET @Result = -1 /*DivisionByZero*/		
	END
	ELSE
		SET @Result = 1                                                    
	/**************** PreOrder Verify *************************************/
	IF(@Result = 1)
	BEGIN
		UPDATE dbo.WSPreOrder
		SET 
		pordIsVerified = 1,
		pordVerifierId = @VerifierUserId,
		pordIsFixed = 1,
		pordVerifiedRegisterDate = @RegisterDate,
		pordVerifiedRegisterDatePersian = @RegisterDatePersian
		WHERE PreOrderID = @PreOrderId		
	END	

/**/
END








GO
-- [dbo].[MobspReturnCompanyProjectOrderView]







/*AI14021005 - Copied from web*/
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[MobspReturnCompanyProjectOrderView]
(
/*
	declare @prjOrderView INT = 0
	EXEC MobspReturnCompanyProjectOrderView 
	@CompanyId  = 6156,
	@prjOrderView = @prjOrderView  OUTPUT
	select @prjOrderView
*/
@CompanyId INT,
@prjOrderView INT OUTPUT
)
AS
BEGIN
/**/	
	DECLARE @ItemCount INT=NULL
	SET @ItemCount = (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM [dbo].[CrmProjects] WHERE prjCompanyId = @CompanyId)
	SET @prjOrderView = (ISNULL(@ItemCount,0) + 1) + (ISNULL(@ItemCount,0) * 9 ) - 1
/**/
END

-- [dbo].[MobspReturnDocNumRef]






/*AI14020529*/
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[MobspReturnDocNumRef]
(	
/* 
	EXEC MobspReturnDocNumRef 6156,6891,3,45888,1033,1065	
*/
@DocCompanyId INT,
@DocProjectId INT,
@UserId INT,
@DocNumber BIGINT,
@LanguageID INT = NULL,
@DefaultLanguageID INT = NULL,
@SelectedNumRef NVARCHAR(MAX) OUTPUT
)
AS
BEGIN
/**/
/*------------------------ Define variabels -------------------*/
DECLARE 
@ValueForCmpName NVARCHAR(1000),
@ValueForCmpNameEng NVARCHAR(1000),
@ValueForCmpNumber NVARCHAR(1000),
@ValueForCmpCode NVARCHAR(1000),
@ValueForPrjInquiryNum NVARCHAR(1000),
@ValueForPrjNumR NVARCHAR(1000),
@ValueForPrjNumRef NVARCHAR(1000),
@ValueForSysNum NVARCHAR(1000),
@ValueForSysNumR NVARCHAR(1000),
@ValueForUserFName NVARCHAR(1000),
@ValueForUserLName NVARCHAR(1000),
@ValueForUserFullName NVARCHAR(1000),
@ValueForUserPersCode NVARCHAR(1000),
@docNumRef NVARCHAR(150),
@docNumRefFormula NVARCHAR(4000)
/*-----------------------------------------------------------*/
SELECT TOP 1 
    @docNumRefFormula = [docNumRefFormula]    
FROM [dbo].[CrmNumRefSetting]
/*------------------ Check prjNumRefFormula -----------------*/
IF(ISNULL(@docNumRefFormula,'') <> '')
BEGIN
/**/
	/*------------------------ Set variabels -------------------*/
	IF(@LanguageID IS NULL OR @LanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID)
	BEGIN
	/**/
		SELECT 
			@ValueForCmpName = (CASE WHEN ISNULL(C.[cmpKind],0) = 0
									   THEN ((ISNULL((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyTitle] CT WHERE CT.[CompanyTitleID] = C.[cmpTitleId]),'') + ' ' + 
											  ISNULL(C.[cmpName],'')))
									   ELSE	(SELECT ISNULL((SELECT PersonTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmPersonTitle] PT WHERE PT.PersonTitleID = CCP.prsTitleId),'')  + ' ' +
													ISNULL(prsFName, '') + ' ' + ISNULL(prsLName,'')
											FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CCP
											WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = C.[cmpMainPersonId]		
											)				
								 END), 
			@ValueForCmpNameEng = cmpNameEng,
			@ValueForCmpNumber = CAST(cmpNumber AS NVARCHAR(50)),
			@ValueForCmpCode = cmpCode			
		FROM [dbo].[CrmCompany] C
		WHERE CompanyID = @DocCompanyId
	/**/
	END
	ELSE IF(@LanguageID IS NOT NULL AND @LanguageID <> @DefaultLanguageID)
	BEGIN
	/**/
		SELECT 
			@ValueForCmpName = (CASE WHEN ISNULL(C.[cmpKind],0) = 0
									THEN ((ISNULL((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyTitle_Lang] CT WHERE CT.[CompanyTitleID] = C.[cmpTitleId] AND CT.LanguageID = @LanguageID),'') + ' ' + 
											ISNULL((SELECT CLN.[cmpName] FROM [dbo].[CrmCompany_Lang] CLN WHERE CLN.CompanyID = C.CompanyID AND CLN.LanguageID = @LanguageID),'')))
									ELSE	(SELECT ISNULL((SELECT PersonTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmPersonTitle_Lang] PT WHERE PT.PersonTitleID = CCP.prsTitleId AND PT.LanguageID = @LanguageID),'')  + ' ' +
												ISNULL((SELECT ISNULL(CCPLN.prsFName, '') + ' ' + ISNULL(CCPLN.prsLName,'') FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons_Lang] CCPLN WHERE CCPLN.CompanyPersonID = CCP.CompanyPersonID AND CCPLN.LanguageID = @LanguageID),'')
										FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CCP
										WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = C.[cmpMainPersonId]		
										)				
								END), 
			@ValueForCmpNameEng = cmpNameEng,
			@ValueForCmpNumber = CAST(cmpNumber AS NVARCHAR(50)),
			@ValueForCmpCode = cmpCode			
		FROM [dbo].[CrmCompany] C
		WHERE CompanyID = @DocCompanyId
	/**/
	END	

	SELECT 
		@ValueForPrjInquiryNum = prjInquiryNum, 
		@ValueForPrjNumR = CAST(prjNumR AS NVARCHAR(50)),			
		@ValueForPrjNumRef = prjNumRef
	FROM [dbo].[CrmProjects] 
	WHERE ProjectID = @DocProjectId

	SET @ValueForSysNum = @DocNumber

	IF(@LanguageID IS NULL OR @LanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID)
	BEGIN
	/**/
		SELECT 
			@ValueForUserFName = [FName],
			@ValueForUserLName = [LName],
			@ValueForUserFullName = LTRIM(RTRIM(ISNULL([FName],'') + ' ' +  ISNULL([LName],''))),
			@ValueForUserPersCode = [PersCode]    
		FROM [dbo].[Users] U
		WHERE U.[UserId] = @UserId
	/**/
	END
	ELSE IF(@LanguageID IS NOT NULL AND @LanguageID <> @DefaultLanguageID)
	BEGIN
	/**/
		SELECT 
			@ValueForUserFName = ISNULL((SELECT [FName] FROM [dbo].[Users_Lang] ULN WHERE ULN.UserId = U.UserId AND ULN.LanguageID = @LanguageID),''),
			@ValueForUserLName = ISNULL((SELECT [LName] FROM [dbo].[Users_Lang] ULN WHERE ULN.UserId = U.UserId AND ULN.LanguageID = @LanguageID),''),
			@ValueForUserFullName =  LTRIM(RTRIM(ISNULL((SELECT [FName] FROM [dbo].[Users_Lang] ULN WHERE ULN.UserId = U.UserId AND ULN.LanguageID = @LanguageID),'') 
								  + ' ' 
								  +  ISNULL((SELECT [LName] FROM [dbo].[Users_Lang] ULN WHERE ULN.UserId = U.UserId AND ULN.LanguageID = @LanguageID),''))),
			@ValueForUserPersCode = [PersCode]    
		FROM [dbo].[Users] U
		WHERE U.[UserId] = @UserId
	/**/
	END	
	/*------------------------ Set NumRef ---------------------*/
	SET @docNumRef = @docNumRefFormula
	SET @docNumRef = REPLACE(@docNumRef, N'[#CmpName#]', ISNULL(@ValueForCmpName, '') )
	SET @docNumRef = REPLACE(@docNumRef, N'[#CmpNameEng#]', ISNULL(@ValueForCmpNameEng, '') )
	SET @docNumRef = REPLACE(@docNumRef, N'[#CmpNumber#]', ISNULL(@ValueForCmpNumber, '') )
	SET @docNumRef = REPLACE(@docNumRef, N'[#CmpCode#]', ISNULL(@ValueForCmpCode, '') )
	SET @docNumRef = REPLACE(@docNumRef, N'[#SysNum#]', ISNULL(@ValueForSysNum, '') )
	SET @docNumRef = REPLACE(@docNumRef, N'[#SysNumR#]', ISNULL(@ValueForSysNumR, '') )
	SET @docNumRef = REPLACE(@docNumRef, N'[#PrjInquiryNum#]', ISNULL(@ValueForPrjInquiryNum, '') )
	SET @docNumRef = REPLACE(@docNumRef, N'[#PrjNumR#]', ISNULL(@ValueForPrjNumR, '') )
	SET @docNumRef = REPLACE(@docNumRef, N'[#PrjNumRef#]', ISNULL(@ValueForPrjNumRef, '') )
	SET @docNumRef = REPLACE(@docNumRef, N'[#UserFName#]', ISNULL(@ValueForUserFName, '') )
	SET @docNumRef = REPLACE(@docNumRef, N'[#UserLName#]', ISNULL(@ValueForUserLName, '') )
	SET @docNumRef = REPLACE(@docNumRef, N'[#UserFullName#]', ISNULL(@ValueForUserFullName, '') )
	SET @docNumRef = REPLACE(@docNumRef, N'[#UserPersCode#]', ISNULL(@ValueForUserPersCode, '') )	
/**/
END
/**/
SET @SelectedNumRef = @docNumRef
/**/
END

CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[MobspReturnPreOrderNumRef]

			@ValueForCmpNumber = CAST(cmpNumber AS NVARCHAR(50)),
			@ValueForCmpCode = cmpCode			
		FROM [dbo].[CrmCompany] C
		WHERE CompanyID = @PordCompanyId

	END	
	/*AI14010505- End*/

		@ValueForPrjInquiryNum = prjInquiryNum, 
		@ValueForPrjNumR = CAST(prjNumR AS NVARCHAR(50)),			
		@ValueForPrjNumRef = prjNumRef
	FROM [dbo].[CrmProjects]

	SELECT 
		@ValueForPreOrderOpr = [PreOrderOpr]

								  + ' ' 
								  +  ISNULL((SELECT [LName] FROM [dbo].[Users_Lang] ULN WHERE ULN.UserId = U.UserId AND ULN.LanguageID = @LanguageID),''))),
			@ValueForUserPersCode = [PersCode]    
		FROM [dbo].[Users] U
		WHERE U.[UserId] = @PordReferUserId
	/**/
	END	
	/*RZ14000616- End*/
	/*------------------------ Set NumRef ---------------------*/
	SET @pordNumRef = @pordNumRefFormula
	SET @pordNumRef = REPLACE(@pordNumRef, N'[#CmpName#]', ISNULL(@ValueForCmpName, '') )
	SET @pordNumRef = REPLACE(@pordNumRef, N'[#CmpNameEng#]', ISNULL(@ValueForCmpNameEng, '') )
	SET @pordNumRef = REPLACE(@pordNumRef, N'[#CmpNumber#]', ISNULL(@ValueForCmpNumber, '') )
	SET @pordNumRef = REPLACE(@pordNumRef, N'[#CmpCode#]', ISNULL(@ValueForCmpCode, '') )
	SET @pordNumRef = REPLACE(@pordNumRef, N'[#SysNum#]', ISNULL(@ValueForSysNum, '') )
	SET @pordNumRef = REPLACE(@pordNumRef, N'[#SysNumR#]', ISNULL(@ValueForSysNumR, '') )
	SET @pordNumRef = REPLACE(@pordNumRef, N'[#PrjInquiryNum#]', ISNULL(@ValueForPrjInquiryNum, '') )
	SET @pordNumRef = REPLACE(@pordNumRef, N'[#PrjNumR#]', ISNULL(@ValueForPrjNumR, '') )
	SET @pordNumRef = REPLACE(@pordNumRef, N'[#PrjNumRef#]', ISNULL(@ValueForPrjNumRef, '') )

	SET @pordNumRef = REPLACE(@pordNumRef, N'[#PordOprId#]', ISNULL(@ValueForPreOrderOpr, '') )
	SET @pordNumRef = REPLACE(@pordNumRef, N'[#PordTypeId#]', ISNULL(@ValueForPreOrderType, '') )

	SET @pordNumRef = REPLACE(@pordNumRef, N'[#UserFName#]', ISNULL(@ValueForUserFName, '') )
	SET @pordNumRef = REPLACE(@pordNumRef, N'[#UserLName#]', ISNULL(@ValueForUserLName, '') )
	SET @pordNumRef = REPLACE(@pordNumRef, N'[#UserFullName#]', ISNULL(@ValueForUserFullName, '') )
	SET @pordNumRef = REPLACE(@pordNumRef, N'[#UserPersCode#]', ISNULL(@ValueForUserPersCode, '') )	
/**/
END
/**/
SET @selectedNumRef = @pordNumRef
/**/
END

											ISNULL((SELECT CLN.[cmpName] FROM [dbo].[CrmCompany_Lang] CLN WHERE CLN.CompanyID = C.CompanyID AND CLN.LanguageID = @LanguageID),'')))
									ELSE	(SELECT ISNULL((SELECT PersonTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmPersonTitle_Lang] PT WHERE PT.PersonTitleID = CCP.prsTitleId AND PT.LanguageID = @LanguageID),'')  + ' ' +
												ISNULL((SELECT ISNULL(CCPLN.prsFName, '') + ' ' + ISNULL(CCPLN.prsLName,'') FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons_Lang] CCPLN WHERE CCPLN.CompanyPersonID = CCP.CompanyPersonID AND CCPLN.LanguageID = @LanguageID),'')
										FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CCP
										WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = C.[cmpMainPersonId]		
										)				
								END), 
			@ValueForCmpNameEng = cmpNameEng,
			@ValueForCmpNumber = CAST(cmpNumber AS NVARCHAR(50)),
			@ValueForCmpCode = cmpCode			
		FROM [dbo].[CrmCompany] C
		WHERE CompanyID = @PrjCompanyId
	/**/
	END	
	/*AI14020412- End*/

	SET @ValueForSysNum = @PrjInquiryNum
	SET @ValueForSysNumR =  CAST(@PrjNumR AS NVARCHAR(50))

	SET @ValueForPrjInquiryNum = @PrjInquiryNum
	SET @ValueForPrjNumR =  CAST(@PrjNumR AS NVARCHAR(50))

	/*SELECT 
		@ValueForUserFName = [FName],
		@ValueForUserLName = [LName],
		@ValueForUserFullName = LTRIM(RTRIM(ISNULL([FName],'') + ' ' +  ISNULL([LName],''))),
		@ValueForUserPersCode = [PersCode]    
	FROM [dbo].[Users] U
	WHERE U.[UserId] = @PrjStarterUserId AI14020412*/

	/*AI14020412- Add multiLang*/
	IF(@LanguageID IS NULL OR @LanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID)
	BEGIN
	/**/
		SELECT 
			@ValueForUserFName = [FName],
			@ValueForUserLName = [LName],
			@ValueForUserFullName = LTRIM(RTRIM(ISNULL([FName],'') + ' ' +  ISNULL([LName],''))),
			@ValueForUserPersCode = [PersCode]    
		FROM [dbo].[Users] U
		WHERE U.[UserId] = @PrjStarterUserId
	/**/
	END
	ELSE IF(@LanguageID IS NOT NULL AND @LanguageID <> @DefaultLanguageID)
	BEGIN
	/**/
		SELECT 
			@ValueForUserFName = ISNULL((SELECT [FName] FROM [dbo].[Users_Lang] ULN WHERE ULN.UserId = U.UserId AND ULN.LanguageID = @LanguageID),''),
			@ValueForUserLName = ISNULL((SELECT [LName] FROM [dbo].[Users_Lang] ULN WHERE ULN.UserId = U.UserId AND ULN.LanguageID = @LanguageID),''),
			@ValueForUserFullName =  LTRIM(RTRIM(ISNULL((SELECT [FName] FROM [dbo].[Users_Lang] ULN WHERE ULN.UserId = U.UserId AND ULN.LanguageID = @LanguageID),'') 
								  + ' ' 
								  +  ISNULL((SELECT [LName] FROM [dbo].[Users_Lang] ULN WHERE ULN.UserId = U.UserId AND ULN.LanguageID = @LanguageID),''))),
			@ValueForUserPersCode = [PersCode]    
		FROM [dbo].[Users] U
		WHERE U.[UserId] = @PrjStarterUserId
	/**/
	END	
	/*AI14020412- End*/

	/*------------------------ Set NumRef ---------------------*/
	SET @prjNumRef = @prjNumRefFormula
	SET @prjNumRef = REPLACE(@prjNumRef, N'[#CmpName#]', ISNULL(@ValueForCmpName, '') )
	SET @prjNumRef = REPLACE(@prjNumRef, N'[#CmpNameEng#]', ISNULL(@ValueForCmpNameEng, '') )
	SET @prjNumRef = REPLACE(@prjNumRef, N'[#CmpNumber#]', ISNULL(@ValueForCmpNumber, '') )
	SET @prjNumRef = REPLACE(@prjNumRef, N'[#CmpCode#]', ISNULL(@ValueForCmpCode, '') )
	SET @prjNumRef = REPLACE(@prjNumRef, N'[#SysNum#]', ISNULL(@ValueForSysNum, '') )
	SET @prjNumRef = REPLACE(@prjNumRef, N'[#SysNumR#]', ISNULL(@ValueForSysNumR, '') )
	SET @prjNumRef = REPLACE(@prjNumRef, N'[#PrjInquiryNum#]', ISNULL(@ValueForPrjInquiryNum, '') )
	SET @prjNumRef = REPLACE(@prjNumRef, N'[#PrjNumR#]', ISNULL(@ValueForPrjNumR, '') )
	SET @prjNumRef = REPLACE(@prjNumRef, N'[#UserFName#]', ISNULL(@ValueForUserFName, '') )
	SET @prjNumRef = REPLACE(@prjNumRef, N'[#UserLName#]', ISNULL(@ValueForUserLName, '') )
	SET @prjNumRef = REPLACE(@prjNumRef, N'[#UserFullName#]', ISNULL(@ValueForUserFullName, '') )
	SET @prjNumRef = REPLACE(@prjNumRef, N'[#UserPersCode#]', ISNULL(@ValueForUserPersCode, '') )	
/**/
END
/**/
SET @selectedNumRef= @prjNumRef
/**/
END
