-- [dbo].[MobspSelectClosedCalendarEvents]






/*AI14020126 - Copied from hrm*/
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[MobspSelectClosedCalendarEvents]
(
	/*
		EXEC MobspSelectClosedCalendarEvents '2022-03-21 00:00:00', '2023-03-20 00:00:00', 1065, 1065
	*/
	@FromDate DATETIME,
	@ToDate DATETIME,
	@LanguageID INT = NULL,
	@DefaultLanguageID INT = NULL 
)
AS
BEGIN

	/**/
	DECLARE @MonthDays TABLE(
		MyDays DATE,
		MyDays_EN VARCHAR(20),
		MyDays_FA VARCHAR(20),
		MyDays_FA_Year INT,
		MyDays_FA_Month INT,
		MyDays_FA_Day INT,
		MyDays_AR VARCHAR(20),
		MyDays_AR_Year INT,
		MyDays_AR_Month INT,
		MyDays_AR_Day INT
	)
	/*=================================*/
	DECLARE @FDate_tmp DATE = CAST(@FromDate AS DATE), @TDate_tmp DATE = CAST(@ToDate AS DATE)
	/*=================================*/
	WHILE(@FDate_tmp <= @TDate_tmp)
	BEGIN
		/**/
		INSERT INTO @MonthDays(
			MyDays,
			MyDays_EN,
			MyDays_FA,
			MyDays_FA_Year, 
			MyDays_FA_Month,
			MyDays_FA_Day,
			MyDays_AR,
			MyDays_AR_Year, 
			MyDays_AR_Month, 
			MyDays_AR_Day
		)
		VALUES(
			@FDate_tmp, 
			FORMAT(@FDate_tmp, 'yyyy-MM-dd', 'en-US'),
			FORMAT(@FDate_tmp, 'yyyy/MM/dd', 'fa-IR'),
			SUBSTRING(CONVERT(VARCHAR(20), FORMAT(@FDate_tmp, 'yyyy/MM/dd HH:mm:ss', 'fa-IR'), 120), 0, 5),/*Year*/
			SUBSTRING(CONVERT(VARCHAR(20), FORMAT(@FDate_tmp, 'yyyy/MM/dd HH:mm:ss', 'fa-IR'), 120), 6, 2),/*Month*/
			SUBSTRING(CONVERT(VARCHAR(20), FORMAT(@FDate_tmp, 'yyyy/MM/dd HH:mm:ss', 'fa-IR'), 120), 9, 2),/*Day*/
			/**/
			FORMAT(DATEADD(DAY, -1, @FDate_tmp), 'yyyy/MM/dd', 'ar-SA'),
			SUBSTRING(CONVERT(VARCHAR(20), FORMAT(DATEADD(DAY, -1, @FDate_tmp), 'yyyy/MM/dd HH:mm:ss', 'ar-SA'), 120), 0, 5),/*Year*/
			SUBSTRING(CONVERT(VARCHAR(20), FORMAT(DATEADD(DAY, -1, @FDate_tmp), 'yyyy/MM/dd HH:mm:ss', 'ar-SA'), 120), 6, 2),/*Month*/
			SUBSTRING(CONVERT(VARCHAR(20), FORMAT(DATEADD(DAY, -1, @FDate_tmp), 'yyyy/MM/dd HH:mm:ss', 'ar-SA'), 120), 9, 2)/*Day*/
			)
		/**/
		SET @FDate_tmp = DATEADD(DAY, 1, @FDate_tmp)
		/**/
	END
	/*=================================*/
	/*=================================*/
	IF (@LanguageID IS NULL OR @LanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID)
	BEGIN
		/**/
		SELECT
			MD.MyDays AS [SingleDate]
			,MyDays_EN AS [SingleDateEN]
			,MD.MyDays_FA AS [SingleDateFA]
			,MD.MyDays_AR AS [SingleDateAR]
			,(
				CASE WHEN @LanguageID = 1065 THEN
						(SELECT WD.PersianDay FROM CrmsysWeekDays WD WHERE WD.WeekDayID = DATEPART(WEEKDAY, MD.MyDays))
					WHEN @LanguageID = 1033 THEN
						(SELECT WD.[Day] FROM CrmsysWeekDays WD WHERE WD.WeekDayID = DATEPART(WEEKDAY, MD.MyDays))
					WHEN @LanguageID = 1025 THEN
						(SELECT WD.ArabicDay FROM CrmsysWeekDays WD WHERE WD.WeekDayID = DATEPART(WEEKDAY, MD.MyDays))
					ELSE 
						NULL
				END
			) AS [DayName]
			/**/
			,(CASE WHEN 
				(
					SELECT
						COUNT(*)
					FROM CrmsysCalendarEvents CE
					WHERE 
						IsClosed = 1
						AND 
						(
							(/*FA*/
									MonthNum = MD.MyDays_FA_Month
								AND DayNum = MD.MyDays_FA_Day
								AND LanguageID = 1065
							) OR 
							(/*EN*/
									MonthNum = MONTH(MD.MyDays)
								AND DayNum = DAY(MD.MyDays)
								AND LanguageID = 1033
							) OR 
							(/*AR*/
									MonthNum = MD.MyDays_AR_Month
								AND DayNum =MD.MyDays_AR_Day
								AND LanguageID = 1025
							)
						)
				) >= 1
					THEN
						CAST(1 AS BIT)
					ELSE 
						0
				END
			) AS [IsClosed]
			/**/
			,(
				(
					ISNULL(
					(
						SELECT 
							ISNULL(CE.[Description] + ' - ', NULL)
						FROM CrmsysCalendarEvents CE
						WHERE MonthNum = MD.MyDays_FA_Month
							AND DayNum = MD.MyDays_FA_Day
							AND LanguageID = 1065
					)
					, '')
				)
				+
				(
					ISNULL(
					(
						SELECT 
							ISNULL(CE.[Description] + ' - ', NULL)
						FROM CrmsysCalendarEvents CE
						WHERE MonthNum = MD.MyDays_AR_Month
						  AND DayNum = MD.MyDays_AR_Day
						  AND LanguageID = 1025
					)
					, '')
				)
				+
				(
					ISNULL(
					(
						SELECT 
							CE.[Description]
						FROM CrmsysCalendarEvents CE
						WHERE MonthNum = MONTH(MD.MyDays)
							AND DayNum = DAY(MD.MyDays)
							AND LanguageID = 1033
					)
					, '')
				)
			) AS [IsClosedDesc]
		FROM @MonthDays MD
		WHERE (CASE WHEN 
			(
				SELECT
					COUNT(*)
				FROM CrmsysCalendarEvents CE
				WHERE 
					IsClosed = 1
					AND 
					(
						(/*FA*/
								MonthNum = MD.MyDays_FA_Month
							AND DayNum = MD.MyDays_FA_Day
							AND LanguageID = 1065
						) OR 
						(/*EN*/
								MonthNum = MONTH(MD.MyDays)
							AND DayNum = DAY(MD.MyDays)
							AND LanguageID = 1033
						) OR 
						(/*AR*/
								MonthNum = MD.MyDays_AR_Month
							AND DayNum =MD.MyDays_AR_Day
							AND LanguageID = 1025
						)
					)
			) >= 1
				THEN
					CAST(1 AS BIT)
				ELSE 
					0
			END
			) = 1
		/**/
	END
	ELSE IF (@LanguageID IS NOT NULL AND @LanguageID <> @DefaultLanguageID)
	BEGIN
		/**/
		SELECT
			MD.MyDays AS [SingleDate]
			,MyDays_EN AS [SingleDateEN]
			,MD.MyDays_FA AS [SingleDateFA]
			,MD.MyDays_AR AS [SingleDateAR]
			,(
				CASE WHEN @LanguageID = 1065 THEN
						(SELECT WD.PersianDay FROM CrmsysWeekDays WD WHERE WD.WeekDayID = DATEPART(WEEKDAY, MD.MyDays))
					WHEN @LanguageID = 1033 THEN
						(SELECT WD.[Day] FROM CrmsysWeekDays WD WHERE WD.WeekDayID = DATEPART(WEEKDAY, MD.MyDays))
					WHEN @LanguageID = 1025 THEN
						(SELECT WD.ArabicDay FROM CrmsysWeekDays WD WHERE WD.WeekDayID = DATEPART(WEEKDAY, MD.MyDays))
					ELSE 
						NULL
				END
			) AS [DayName]
			/**/
			,(CASE WHEN 
				(
					SELECT
						COUNT(*)
					FROM CrmsysCalendarEvents CE
					WHERE 
						IsClosed = 1
						AND 
						(
							(/*FA*/
									MonthNum = TRY_CAST(FORMAT(MD.MyDays, 'MM', 'fa-IR') AS INT)
								AND DayNum = TRY_CAST(FORMAT(MD.MyDays, 'dd', 'fa-IR') AS INT)
								AND LanguageID = 1065
							) OR 
							(/*EN*/
									MonthNum = MONTH(MD.MyDays)
								AND DayNum = DAY(MD.MyDays)
								AND LanguageID = 1033
							) OR 
							(/*AR*/
									MonthNum = TRY_CAST(FORMAT(MD.MyDays, 'MM', 'ar-SA') AS INT)
								AND DayNum+1 = TRY_CAST(FORMAT(MD.MyDays, 'dd', 'ar-SA') AS INT)
								AND LanguageID = 1025
							)
						)
				) >= 1
					THEN
						CAST(1 AS BIT)
					ELSE 
						NULL
				END
			) AS [IsClosed]
			/**/
			,(
				(
					ISNULL(
					(
						SELECT 
							ISNULL(CE.[Description] + ' - ', NULL)
						FROM CrmsysCalendarEvents CE
						WHERE MonthNum = TRY_CAST(FORMAT(MD.MyDays, 'MM', 'fa-IR') AS INT)
							AND DayNum = TRY_CAST(FORMAT(MD.MyDays, 'dd', 'fa-IR') AS INT)
							AND LanguageID = 1065
					)
					, '')
				)
				+
				(
					ISNULL(
					(
						SELECT 
							ISNULL(CE.[Description] + ' - ', NULL)
						FROM CrmsysCalendarEvents CE
						WHERE MonthNum = TRY_CAST(FORMAT(MD.MyDays, 'MM', 'ar-SA') AS INT)
							AND DayNum+1 = TRY_CAST(FORMAT(MD.MyDays, 'dd', 'ar-SA') AS INT)
							AND LanguageID = 1025
					)
					, '')
				)
				+
				(
					ISNULL(
					(
						SELECT 
							CE.[Description]
						FROM CrmsysCalendarEvents CE
						WHERE MonthNum = MONTH(MD.MyDays)
							AND DayNum = DAY(MD.MyDays)
							AND LanguageID = 1033
					)
					, '')
				)
			) AS [IsClosedDesc]
			FROM @MonthDays MD
			WHERE (CASE WHEN 
				(
					SELECT
						COUNT(*)
					FROM CrmsysCalendarEvents CE
					WHERE 
						IsClosed = 1
						AND 
						(
							(/*FA*/
									MonthNum = MD.MyDays_FA_Month
								AND DayNum = MD.MyDays_FA_Day
								AND LanguageID = 1065
							) OR 
							(/*EN*/
									MonthNum = MONTH(MD.MyDays)
								AND DayNum = DAY(MD.MyDays)
								AND LanguageID = 1033
							) OR 
							(/*AR*/
									MonthNum = MD.MyDays_AR_Month
								AND DayNum =MD.MyDays_AR_Day
								AND LanguageID = 1025
							)
						)
				) >= 1
					THEN
						CAST(1 AS BIT)
					ELSE 
						0
				END
				) = 1
		/**/
	END
	
	/**/
END








GO

/*AI14020529 - Copied from web*/
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[MobspSelectDocItemTracing]
( 
/* 
	EXEC MobspSelectDocItemTracing N'BB-10-20',0
*/
@Tracing NVARCHAR(200),
@PrdId INT
)
AS
BEGIN
/**/
IF(ISNULL(@PrdId,0) > 0)
BEGIN
/**/
	SELECT DISTINCT /*TOP (20) */
		[Tracing]      
	FROM [dbo].[WSDocItems]	
	WHERE [PrdId] = @PrdId
	AND ISNULL([Tracing],'') <> ''
	AND (REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE([Tracing] , NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)))
		 = 
		(REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(@Tracing , NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)))				
/**/
END
ELSE /*IF(ISNULL(@PrdId,0) > 0)*/
BEGIN
/**/
	SELECT DISTINCT /*TOP (20) */
		[Tracing]      
	FROM [dbo].[WSDocItems]		
	WHERE ISNULL([Tracing],'') <> ''
	AND (REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE([Tracing] , NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)))
		 = 
		(REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(@Tracing , NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)))		
/**/
END
/**/
END

-- [dbo].[MobspSelectProductIssueOrdFromIndexedByPordId]







/*AI14020705 - Copied from web*/
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[MobspSelectProductIssueOrdFromIndexedByPordId]
/* 
	EXEC MobspSelectProductIssueOrdFromIndexedByPordId 4358, 1033,1065
*/	
(
@PreOrderId INT,
@LanguageID INT = NULL,
@DefaultLanguageID INT = NULL
)
AS 
BEGIN
/**/
IF(@LanguageID IS NULL OR @LanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID)
BEGIN
/**/
	SELECT 
		 [ProductID]
		,[PrdGroupID]
		,[PrdCode]
		,[PrdName]
		,(SELECT [SGName] FROM [dbo].[WSProductSecondGroup] SG WHERE SG.[PrdSecondGroupID] = P.[PrdGroupID]) [PrdSGroupName]
		,(SELECT [SGCode] FROM [dbo].[WSProductSecondGroup] SG WHERE SG.[PrdSecondGroupID] = P.[PrdGroupID]) [PrdSGroupCode]
	    ,(SELECT [FirstGroupId] FROM [dbo].[WSProductSecondGroup] SG WHERE SG.[PrdSecondGroupID] = P.[PrdGroupID]) [PrdFGroupId]  
	    ,(SELECT [FGName] FROM [dbo].[WSProductFirstGroup] FG WHERE FG.[PrdFirstGroupID] =
			(SELECT [FirstGroupId] FROM [dbo].[WSProductSecondGroup] SG WHERE SG.[PrdSecondGroupID] = P.[PrdGroupID])) [PrdFGroupName]  
		,(SELECT [FGCode] FROM [dbo].[WSProductFirstGroup] FG WHERE FG.[PrdFirstGroupID] =
			(SELECT [FirstGroupId] FROM [dbo].[WSProductSecondGroup] SG WHERE SG.[PrdSecondGroupID] = P.[PrdGroupID])) [PrdFGroupCode] 
		
		,[PrdAutoCalcIssueOrdFromIndexed]
		,[PrdAutoCalcIssueOrdFromIndexedOprId]
		,(SELECT TOP (1) [Name] FROM [dbo].[WSOrderOpr] OPR WHERE OPR.[OrderOprID] = [PrdAutoCalcIssueOrdFromIndexedOprId]) [PrdAutoCalcIssueOrdFromIndexedOpr]
	    ,[PrdAutoCalcIndexedPrdId]
		,(SELECT PP.[PrdName] FROM [dbo].[WSProducts] PP WHERE PP.[ProductID] = P.[PrdAutoCalcIndexedPrdId])[PrdAutoCalcIndexedPrdName]
		,(SELECT PP.[PrdCode] FROM [dbo].[WSProducts] PP WHERE PP.[ProductID] = P.[PrdAutoCalcIndexedPrdId])[PrdAutoCalcIndexedPrdCode]
		,(SELECT [SGName] FROM [dbo].[WSProductSecondGroup] SG WHERE SG.[PrdSecondGroupID] = 
			(SELECT PP.[PrdGroupID] FROM [dbo].[WSProducts] PP WHERE PP.[ProductID] = P.[PrdAutoCalcIndexedPrdId])) [PrdAutoCalcIndexedPrdSGName]
		,(SELECT [SGCode] FROM [dbo].[WSProductSecondGroup] SG WHERE SG.[PrdSecondGroupID] = 
			(SELECT PP.[PrdGroupID] FROM [dbo].[WSProducts] PP WHERE PP.[ProductID] = P.[PrdAutoCalcIndexedPrdId])) [PrdAutoCalcIndexedPrdSGCode]
		,(SELECT [FGName] FROM [dbo].[WSProductFirstGroup] FG WHERE FG.[PrdFirstGroupID] =
			(SELECT [FirstGroupId] FROM [dbo].[WSProductSecondGroup] SG WHERE SG.[PrdSecondGroupID] = 
				(SELECT PP.[PrdGroupID] FROM [dbo].[WSProducts] PP WHERE PP.[ProductID] = P.[PrdAutoCalcIndexedPrdId]))) [PrdAutoCalcIndexedPrdFGName] 
		,(SELECT [FGCode] FROM [dbo].[WSProductFirstGroup] FG WHERE FG.[PrdFirstGroupID] =
			(SELECT [FirstGroupId] FROM [dbo].[WSProductSecondGroup] SG WHERE SG.[PrdSecondGroupID] = 
				(SELECT PP.[PrdGroupID] FROM [dbo].[WSProducts] PP WHERE PP.[ProductID] = P.[PrdAutoCalcIndexedPrdId]))) [PrdAutoCalcIndexedPrdFGCode]  

	FROM [dbo].[WSProducts] P
	WHERE ISNULL([PrdAutoCalcIssueOrdFromIndexed],0) = 1
	AND P.ProductID IN(SELECT pordIPrdId
					   FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrderItems] POI 
					   WHERE POI.PreOrderId = @PreOrderId)
	ORDER BY ISNULL([OrderView],[ProductId]) 
/**/
END
ELSE IF(@LanguageID IS NOT NULL AND @LanguageID <> @DefaultLanguageID)
BEGIN
/**/
	SELECT 
		 [ProductID]
		,[PrdGroupID]
		,[PrdCode]
		,(SELECT [PrdName] FROM [dbo].[WSProducts_Lang] PLN WHERE PLN.ProductID = P.ProductID AND PLN.LanguageID = @LanguageID) [PrdName]
		,(SELECT [SGName] FROM [dbo].[WSProductSecondGroup_Lang] SG WHERE SG.[PrdSecondGroupID] = P.[PrdGroupID] AND SG.LanguageID = @LanguageID) [PrdSGroupName]
		,(SELECT [SGCode] FROM [dbo].[WSProductSecondGroup] SG WHERE SG.[PrdSecondGroupID] = P.[PrdGroupID]) [PrdSGroupCode]
	    ,(SELECT [FirstGroupId] FROM [dbo].[WSProductSecondGroup] SG WHERE SG.[PrdSecondGroupID] = P.[PrdGroupID]) [PrdFGroupId]  
	    ,(SELECT [FGName] FROM [dbo].[WSProductFirstGroup_Lang] FG WHERE FG.[PrdFirstGroupID] =
			(SELECT [FirstGroupId] FROM [dbo].[WSProductSecondGroup] SG WHERE SG.[PrdSecondGroupID] = P.[PrdGroupID]) AND FG.LanguageID = @LanguageID) [PrdFGroupName]  
		,(SELECT [FGCode] FROM [dbo].[WSProductFirstGroup] FG WHERE FG.[PrdFirstGroupID] =
			(SELECT [FirstGroupId] FROM [dbo].[WSProductSecondGroup] SG WHERE SG.[PrdSecondGroupID] = P.[PrdGroupID])) [PrdFGroupCode]  
		,[PrdAutoCalcIssueOrdFromIndexed]
		,[PrdAutoCalcIssueOrdFromIndexedOprId]
		,(SELECT TOP (1) [Name] FROM [dbo].[WSOrderOpr_Lang] OPR WHERE OPR.[OrderOprID] = [PrdAutoCalcIssueOrdFromIndexedOprId] AND OPR.LanguageID = @LanguageID) [PrdAutoCalcIssueOrdFromIndexedOpr]
	    ,[PrdAutoCalcIndexedPrdId]
		,(SELECT PP.[PrdName] FROM [dbo].[WSProducts_Lang] PP WHERE PP.[ProductID] = P.[PrdAutoCalcIndexedPrdId] AND PP.LanguageID = @LanguageID)[PrdAutoCalcIndexedPrdName]
		,(SELECT PP.[PrdCode] FROM [dbo].[WSProducts] PP WHERE PP.[ProductID] = P.[PrdAutoCalcIndexedPrdId])[PrdAutoCalcIndexedPrdCode]
		,(SELECT [SGName] FROM [dbo].[WSProductSecondGroup_Lang] SG WHERE SG.[PrdSecondGroupID] = 
			(SELECT PP.[PrdGroupID] FROM [dbo].[WSProducts] PP WHERE PP.[ProductID] = P.[PrdAutoCalcIndexedPrdId]) AND SG.LanguageID = @LanguageID) [PrdAutoCalcIndexedPrdSGName]
		,(SELECT [SGCode] FROM [dbo].[WSProductSecondGroup] SG WHERE SG.[PrdSecondGroupID] = 
			(SELECT PP.[PrdGroupID] FROM [dbo].[WSProducts] PP WHERE PP.[ProductID] = P.[PrdAutoCalcIndexedPrdId])) [PrdAutoCalcIndexedPrdSGCode]
		,(SELECT [FGName] FROM [dbo].[WSProductFirstGroup_Lang] FG WHERE FG.[PrdFirstGroupID] =
			(SELECT [FirstGroupId] FROM [dbo].[WSProductSecondGroup] SG WHERE SG.[PrdSecondGroupID] = 
				(SELECT PP.[PrdGroupID] FROM [dbo].[WSProducts] PP WHERE PP.[ProductID] = P.[PrdAutoCalcIndexedPrdId])) AND FG.LanguageID = @LanguageID) [PrdAutoCalcIndexedPrdFGName] 
		,(SELECT [FGCode] FROM [dbo].[WSProductFirstGroup] FG WHERE FG.[PrdFirstGroupID] =
			(SELECT [FirstGroupId] FROM [dbo].[WSProductSecondGroup] SG WHERE SG.[PrdSecondGroupID] = 
				(SELECT PP.[PrdGroupID] FROM [dbo].[WSProducts] PP WHERE PP.[ProductID] = P.[PrdAutoCalcIndexedPrdId]))) [PrdAutoCalcIndexedPrdFGCode]  

	FROM [dbo].[WSProducts] P
	WHERE ISNULL([PrdAutoCalcIssueOrdFromIndexed],0) = 1
	AND P.ProductID IN(SELECT pordIPrdId
					   FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrderItems] POI 
					   WHERE POI.PreOrderId = @PreOrderId)
	ORDER BY ISNULL([OrderView],[ProductId]) 
/**/
END
/**/
END

-- [dbo].[MobspSelectProductOrdIssueByPordId]






/*AI14020705*/
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[MobspSelectProductOrdIssueByPordId]
/* 
	EXEC MobspSelectProductOrdIssueByPordId 4358, 1033,1065
*/	
(
@PreOrderId INT,
@LanguageID INT = NULL,
@DefaultLanguageID INT = NULL
)
AS 
BEGIN
/**/
IF(@LanguageID IS NULL OR @LanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID)
BEGIN
/**/
	SELECT 
		 [ProductID]
		,[PrdGroupID]
		,[PrdCode]
		,[PrdName]
		,(SELECT [SGName] FROM [dbo].[WSProductSecondGroup] SG WHERE SG.[PrdSecondGroupID] = P.[PrdGroupID]) [PrdSGroupName]
		,(SELECT [SGCode] FROM [dbo].[WSProductSecondGroup] SG WHERE SG.[PrdSecondGroupID] = P.[PrdGroupID]) [PrdSGroupCode]
	    ,(SELECT [FirstGroupId] FROM [dbo].[WSProductSecondGroup] SG WHERE SG.[PrdSecondGroupID] = P.[PrdGroupID]) [PrdFGroupId]  
	    ,(SELECT [FGName] FROM [dbo].[WSProductFirstGroup] FG WHERE FG.[PrdFirstGroupID] =
			(SELECT [FirstGroupId] FROM [dbo].[WSProductSecondGroup] SG WHERE SG.[PrdSecondGroupID] = P.[PrdGroupID])) [PrdFGroupName]  
		,(SELECT [FGCode] FROM [dbo].[WSProductFirstGroup] FG WHERE FG.[PrdFirstGroupID] =
			(SELECT [FirstGroupId] FROM [dbo].[WSProductSecondGroup] SG WHERE SG.[PrdSecondGroupID] = P.[PrdGroupID])) [PrdFGroupCode] 
		,[PrdOrdIssueSetSetting]
		,[PrdOrdIssuePrjTitleGroupId]
		,(SELECT TOP (1) [ProjectTitleGroup] FROM [dbo].[CrmProjectTitleGroups] PTG WHERE PTG.[ProjectTitleGroupId] = [PrdOrdIssuePrjTitleGroupId]) [PrdOrdIssuePrjTitleGroup]
		,[PrdOrdIssuePrjTitleId]
		,(SELECT TOP (1) [ProjectTitle] FROM [dbo].[CrmProjectTitles] PT WHERE PT.[ProjectTitleID] = [PrdOrdIssuePrjTitleId]) [PrdOrdIssuePrjTitle]
		,[PrdOrdIssuePrjPostFix]
		,[PrdOrdIssuePrjCnvsStatusGroupId]
		,(SELECT TOP (1) [ConversationStatusGroup] FROM [dbo].[CrmConversationStatusGroups] CSG WHERE CSG.[ConversationStatusGroupID] = [PrdOrdIssuePrjCnvsStatusGroupId]) [PrdOrdIssuePrjCnvsStatusGroup]
		,[PrdOrdIssuePrjRemindDay]
		,[PrdOrdIssuePrjFOCmpCanSee]
		,[PrdOrdIssuePrjIsTicket]
		,[PrdOrdIssuePrjUsedType]
		,[PrdOrdIssueUpdateCmpTypeId]
		,(SELECT TOP (1) [CompanyTypeName] FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyType] CT WHERE CT.[CompanyTypeID] = [PrdOrdIssueUpdateCmpTypeId]) [PrdOrdIssueUpdateCmpType]
		,[PrdOrdIssueUpdateCmpGroupII]
		,[PrdOrdIssueUpdateTaxCmpGroupIdII]
		,(SELECT TOP (1) [GroupIIName] FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyGroupsII] CG WHERE CG.CompanyGroupIIID = [PrdOrdIssueUpdateTaxCmpGroupIdII]) [PrdOrdIssueUpdateTaxCmpGroupII]
		,[PrdOrdIssueUpdateNoTaxCmpGroupIdII]
		,(SELECT TOP (1) [GroupIIName] FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyGroupsII] CG WHERE CG.CompanyGroupIIID = [PrdOrdIssueUpdateNoTaxCmpGroupIdII]) [PrdOrdIssueUpdateNoTaxCmpGroupII]
		,[PrdOrdIssueDateToCmpEffectiveDate]
	FROM [dbo].[WSProducts] P
	WHERE ISNULL([PrdOrdIssueSetSetting],0) = 1
	AND P.ProductID IN(SELECT pordIPrdId
					   FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrderItems] POI 
					   WHERE POI.PreOrderId = @PreOrderId)
	ORDER BY ISNULL([OrderView],[ProductId]) 
/**/
END
ELSE IF(@LanguageID IS NOT NULL AND @LanguageID <> @DefaultLanguageID)
BEGIN
/**/
	SELECT 
		 [ProductID]
		,[PrdGroupID]
		,[PrdCode]
		,(SELECT [PrdName] FROM [dbo].[WSProducts_Lang] PLN WHERE PLN.ProductID = P.ProductID AND PLN.LanguageID = @LanguageID) [PrdName]
		,(SELECT [SGName] FROM [dbo].[WSProductSecondGroup_Lang] SG WHERE SG.[PrdSecondGroupID] = P.[PrdGroupID] AND SG.LanguageID = @LanguageID) [PrdSGroupName]
		,(SELECT [SGCode] FROM [dbo].[WSProductSecondGroup] SG WHERE SG.[PrdSecondGroupID] = P.[PrdGroupID]) [PrdSGroupCode]
	    ,(SELECT [FirstGroupId] FROM [dbo].[WSProductSecondGroup] SG WHERE SG.[PrdSecondGroupID] = P.[PrdGroupID]) [PrdFGroupId]  
	    ,(SELECT [FGName] FROM [dbo].[WSProductFirstGroup_Lang] FG WHERE FG.[PrdFirstGroupID] =
			(SELECT [FirstGroupId] FROM [dbo].[WSProductSecondGroup] SG WHERE SG.[PrdSecondGroupID] = P.[PrdGroupID]) AND FG.LanguageID = @LanguageID) [PrdFGroupName]  
		,(SELECT [FGCode] FROM [dbo].[WSProductFirstGroup] FG WHERE FG.[PrdFirstGroupID] =
			(SELECT [FirstGroupId] FROM [dbo].[WSProductSecondGroup] SG WHERE SG.[PrdSecondGroupID] = P.[PrdGroupID])) [PrdFGroupCode]  
		,[PrdOrdIssueSetSetting]
		,[PrdOrdIssuePrjTitleGroupId]
		,(SELECT TOP (1) [ProjectTitleGroup] FROM [dbo].[CrmProjectTitleGroups_Lang] PTG WHERE PTG.[ProjectTitleGroupId] = [PrdOrdIssuePrjTitleGroupId] AND PTG.LanguageID = @LanguageID) [PrdOrdIssuePrjTitleGroup]
		,[PrdOrdIssuePrjTitleId]
		,(SELECT TOP (1) [ProjectTitle] FROM [dbo].[CrmProjectTitles_Lang] PT WHERE PT.[ProjectTitleID] = [PrdOrdIssuePrjTitleId] AND PT.LanguageID = @LanguageID) [PrdOrdIssuePrjTitle]
		,(SELECT TOP (1) [PrdOrdIssuePrjPostFix] FROM [dbo].[WSProducts_Lang] PLN WHERE PLN.[ProductID] = P.[ProductID] AND PLN.LanguageID = @LanguageID) [PrdOrdIssuePrjPostFix]
		,[PrdOrdIssuePrjCnvsStatusGroupId]
		,(SELECT TOP (1) [ConversationStatusGroup] FROM [dbo].[CrmConversationStatusGroups_Lang] CSG WHERE CSG.[ConversationStatusGroupID] = [PrdOrdIssuePrjCnvsStatusGroupId] AND CSG.LanguageID = @LanguageID) [PrdOrdIssuePrjCnvsStatusGroup]
		,[PrdOrdIssuePrjRemindDay]
		,[PrdOrdIssuePrjFOCmpCanSee]
		,[PrdOrdIssuePrjIsTicket]
		,[PrdOrdIssuePrjUsedType]
		,[PrdOrdIssueUpdateCmpTypeId]
		,(SELECT TOP (1) [CompanyTypeName] FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyType_Lang] CT WHERE CT.[CompanyTypeID] = [PrdOrdIssueUpdateCmpTypeId] AND CT.LanguageID = @LanguageID) [PrdOrdIssueUpdateCmpType]
		,[PrdOrdIssueUpdateCmpGroupII]
		,[PrdOrdIssueUpdateTaxCmpGroupIdII]
		,(SELECT TOP (1) [GroupIIName] FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyGroupsII_Lang] CG WHERE CG.CompanyGroupIIID = [PrdOrdIssueUpdateTaxCmpGroupIdII] AND CG.LanguageID = @LanguageID) [PrdOrdIssueUpdateTaxCmpGroupII]
		,[PrdOrdIssueUpdateNoTaxCmpGroupIdII]
		,(SELECT TOP (1) [GroupIIName] FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyGroupsII_Lang] CG WHERE CG.CompanyGroupIIID = [PrdOrdIssueUpdateNoTaxCmpGroupIdII] AND CG.LanguageID = @LanguageID) [PrdOrdIssueUpdateNoTaxCmpGroupII]
		,[PrdOrdIssueDateToCmpEffectiveDate]
	FROM [dbo].[WSProducts] P
	WHERE ISNULL([PrdOrdIssueSetSetting],0) = 1
	AND P.ProductID IN(SELECT pordIPrdId
					   FROM [dbo].[WSPreOrderItems] POI 
					   WHERE POI.PreOrderId = @PreOrderId)
	ORDER BY ISNULL([OrderView],[ProductId]) 
/**/
END
/**/
END 






GO
-- [dbo].[MobspSendSmsOrdFOLinkAfterVerify]






/*AI14020824 - Copied from web*/
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[MobspSendSmsOrdFOLinkAfterVerify]
(
	/*
		EXEC [MobspSendSmsOrdFOLinkAfterVerify]
	*/
	@OrderId INT,
	@FoLink NVARCHAR(MAX),
	@FoLinkWithLogin NVARCHAR(MAX),
	@FoLinkWithoutLogin NVARCHAR(MAX),
	@UserId INT,
	@Year1 INT,
	@Month1 INT,
	@RegisterDatePersian NVARCHAR(8),
	@AppType NVARCHAR(20),
	@LanguageID INT = NULL,
	@DefaultLanguageID INT = NULL,
	@Result INT OUTPUT
)
AS
BEGIN
	/**/
	DECLARE @CompanyId INT = NULL, @ProjectId INT = NULL, @SmsBodyPatternFormulaResult NVARCHAR(MAX)

	/**/
	DECLARE @SendSMSOrdFOLinkAfterVerify BIT,
			@SendSMSOrdFOLinkAfterVerifyType INT,
			@SendSMSOrdFOLinkAfterVerifyGSM BIT,
			@SendSMSOrdFOLinkAfterVerifyGSMSetId INT,
			@SendSMSOrdFOLinkAfterVerifyWeb BIT,
			@SendSMSOrdFOLinkAfterVerifyWebProviderId INT,
			@SendSMSOrdFOLinkAfterVerifyNumber NVARCHAR(50),
			@SendSMSOrdFOLinkAfterVerifyBodyPatternFormula NVARCHAR(1000)
	
	SELECT TOP 1 @SendSMSOrdFOLinkAfterVerify = SendSMSOrdFOLinkAfterVerify,
		   @SendSMSOrdFOLinkAfterVerifyType = SendSMSOrdFOLinkAfterVerifyType,
		   @SendSMSOrdFOLinkAfterVerifyGSM = SendSMSOrdFOLinkAfterVerifyGSM,
		   @SendSMSOrdFOLinkAfterVerifyGSMSetId = SendSMSOrdFOLinkAfterVerifyGSMSetId,
		   @SendSMSOrdFOLinkAfterVerifyWeb = SendSMSOrdFOLinkAfterVerifyWeb,
		   @SendSMSOrdFOLinkAfterVerifyWebProviderId = SendSMSOrdFOLinkAfterVerifyWebProviderId,
		   @SendSMSOrdFOLinkAfterVerifyNumber = SendSMSOrdFOLinkAfterVerifyNumber,
		   @SendSMSOrdFOLinkAfterVerifyBodyPatternFormula = SendSMSOrdFOLinkAfterVerifyBodyPatternFormula
	  FROM CrmsysSetting
	
	IF (ISNULL(@SendSMSOrdFOLinkAfterVerify, 0) = 1 AND ISNULL(@SendSMSOrdFOLinkAfterVerifyBodyPatternFormula, '') <> '')
	BEGIN
		/**/

		/********************* Define variables *****************/
		DECLARE @CmpTitel NVARCHAR(200) = '',@CmpName NVARCHAR(300) = '',@cmpMobile NVARCHAR(100) = '',@CmpId INT = NULL,
				@PrsTitel NVARCHAR(200) = '',@PrsFName NVARCHAR(100) = '',@PrsLName NVARCHAR(100) = '',
				@prsMobile NVARCHAR(100) = '',
				@PrsId INT = NULL,@prjTitle NVARCHAR(100) = '',@prjPostFix NVARCHAR(200) = '',
				@prjRemindDate NVARCHAR(10) = '',@prjRemindDateTime DATETIME = NULL,
				@PrjId INT = NULL,
				@ordNumber BIGINT = NULL,@ordDate NVARCHAR(10) = '',@ordDateTime DATETIME = NULL,
				@OrderOprID INT = NULL, @OrderOpr NVARCHAR(500) = '',
				@MobileNumber NVARCHAR(100) = '', @ordNumberStr NVARCHAR(200) ='',
				@cmpPreTel NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,@cmpTel  NVARCHAR(100) = NULL,@SmsBodyPatternFormula NVARCHAR(1000) = NULL
		

		/******************** Get formula ******************************/
		SELECT TOP 1 @SmsBodyPatternFormula = [SendSMSOrdFOLinkAfterVerifyBodyPatternFormula] FROM dbo.CrmsysSetting


		/***************************************************************/
		IF(@LanguageID IS NULL OR @LanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID)
		BEGIN
			/**/
			SELECT @ordNumber = [ordNumber]
				,@ordDate = [ordDate]
				,@CmpId = [ordCompanyId]
				,@PrsId = [ordCompanyPersonId]
				,@PrjId = [ordProjectId]					
				,@ordDateTime = [ordDateTime]					
				,@OrderOprID = [OrderOprID]
				,@OrderOpr = (SELECT TOP(1) [Name] FROM [dbo].[WSOrderOpr] OP WHERE OP.OrderOprID = O.OrderOprID)	
				,@CompanyId = O.ordCompanyId
				,@ProjectId = O.ordProjectId
			FROM [dbo].[WSOrder] O
			WHERE [OrderID] = @OrderId
			/**/
			SELECT TOP(1) 
				@CmpTitel = (CASE WHEN ISNULL(CC.[cmpKind],0) = 0
								THEN LTRIM(RTRIM(((ISNULL((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyTitle] CT WHERE CT.[CompanyTitleID] = CC.[cmpTitleId]),'')))))
								ELSE	(SELECT LTRIM(RTRIM(ISNULL((SELECT PersonTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmPersonTitle] PT WHERE PT.PersonTitleID = CCP.prsTitleId),'')))
										FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CCP
										WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = CC.[cmpMainPersonId]		
									)				
							END),
				@CmpName =  (CASE WHEN ISNULL(CC.[cmpKind],0) = 0
							THEN LTRIM(RTRIM(ISNULL(CC.[cmpName],'')))
							ELSE	(SELECT LTRIM(RTRIM(ISNULL(prsFName, '') + ' ' + ISNULL(prsLName,'')))
									FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CCP
									WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = CC.[cmpMainPersonId]		
								)				
						END),
				@cmpMobile = LTRIM(RTRIM(cmpMobile)),
				@cmpPreTel = cmpPreTel,
				@cmpTel = cmpTel
			FROM dbo.CrmCompany CC
			WHERE CompanyID = @CmpId	

			SELECT TOP(1)
				@PrsTitel = LTRIM(RTRIM(ISNULL((SELECT PersonTitle FROM dbo.CrmPersonTitle CPT WHERE CPT.PersonTitleId = CP.prsTitleId),''))),
				@PrsFName = LTRIM(RTRIM(prsFName)),
				@PrsLName = LTRIM(RTRIM(prsLName)),
				@prsMobile = LTRIM(RTRIM(prsMobile))
			FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CP
			WHERE CompanyPersonID = @PrsId

			SELECT TOP(1) 
				@prjTitle = LTRIM(RTRIM(prjTitle)),
				@prjPostFix = LTRIM(RTRIM(prjPostFix)),
				@prjRemindDate = prjRemindDate,
				@prjRemindDateTime = prjRemindDateTime
			FROM [dbo].[CrmProjects] P
			WHERE ProjectID = @PrjId
		/**/
		END
		ELSE IF(@LanguageID IS NOT NULL AND @LanguageID <> @DefaultLanguageID)
		BEGIN
			/**/
			SELECT @ordNumber = [ordNumber]
				,@ordDate = [ordDate]
				,@CmpId = [ordCompanyId]
				,@PrsId = [ordCompanyPersonId]
				,@PrjId = [ordProjectId]					
				,@ordDateTime = [ordDateTime]					
				,@OrderOprID = [OrderOprID]
				,@OrderOpr = (SELECT TOP(1) [Name] FROM [dbo].[WSOrderOpr_Lang] OP WHERE OP.OrderOprID = O.OrderOprID AND OP.LanguageID = @LanguageID)
				,@CompanyId = O.ordCompanyId
				,@ProjectId = O.ordProjectId
			FROM [dbo].[WSOrder] O
			WHERE [OrderID] = @OrderId
			/**/
			SELECT TOP(1) 
				@CmpTitel = (CASE WHEN ISNULL(CC.[cmpKind],0) = 0
								THEN LTRIM(RTRIM((ISNULL((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyTitle_Lang] CT WHERE CT.[CompanyTitleID] = CC.[cmpTitleId] AND CT.LanguageID = @LanguageID),''))))
								ELSE	(SELECT LTRIM(RTRIM(ISNULL((SELECT PersonTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmPersonTitle_Lang] PT WHERE PT.PersonTitleID = CCP.prsTitleId AND PT.LanguageID = @LanguageID),'') ))
									FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CCP
									WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = CC.[cmpMainPersonId]		
									)				
							END),
				@CmpName =  (CASE WHEN ISNULL(CC.[cmpKind],0) = 0
								THEN LTRIM(RTRIM(ISNULL((SELECT CLN.[cmpName] FROM [dbo].[CrmCompany_Lang] CLN WHERE CLN.CompanyID = CC.CompanyID AND CLN.LanguageID = @LanguageID),'')))
								ELSE	(SELECT LTRIM(RTRIM(ISNULL((SELECT ISNULL(CCPLN.prsFName, '') + ' ' + ISNULL(CCPLN.prsLName,'') FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons_Lang] CCPLN WHERE CCPLN.CompanyPersonID = CCP.CompanyPersonID AND CCPLN.LanguageID = @LanguageID),'')))
									FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CCP
									WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = CC.[cmpMainPersonId]		
									)				
							END),
				@cmpMobile = LTRIM(RTRIM(cmpMobile)),
				@cmpPreTel = cmpPreTel,
				@cmpTel = cmpTel
			FROM dbo.CrmCompany CC
			WHERE CompanyID = @CmpId	

			SELECT TOP(1)
				@PrsTitel = LTRIM(RTRIM(ISNULL((SELECT TOP(1) PersonTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmPersonTitle_Lang] CPT WHERE CPT.PersonTitleId = CP.prsTitleId AND CPT.LanguageID = @LanguageID),''))),
				@PrsFName = LTRIM(RTRIM(ISNULL((SELECT TOP(1) prsFName FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons_Lang] CPLN WHERE CPLN.CompanyPersonID = CP.CompanyPersonID AND CPLN.[LanguageID] = @LanguageID), ''))),
				@PrsLName = LTRIM(RTRIM(ISNULL((SELECT TOP(1) prsLName FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons_Lang] CPLN WHERE CPLN.CompanyPersonID = CP.CompanyPersonID AND CPLN.[LanguageID] = @LanguageID), ''))),
				@cmpMobile = LTRIM(RTRIM(prsMobile))
			FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CP
			WHERE CompanyPersonID = @PrsId

			SELECT TOP(1) 
				@prjTitle = LTRIM(RTRIM((SELECT TOP(1) prjTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmProjects_Lang] PLN WHERE PLN.ProjectID = P.ProjectId AND PLN.LanguageID = @LanguageID ))),
				@prjPostFix = LTRIM(RTRIM((SELECT TOP(1) prjPostFix FROM [dbo].[CrmProjects_Lang] PLN WHERE PLN.ProjectID = P.ProjectId AND PLN.LanguageID = @LanguageID ))),
				@prjRemindDate = prjRemindDate,
				@prjRemindDateTime = prjRemindDateTime
			FROM [dbo].[CrmProjects] P
			WHERE ProjectID = @PrjId
		/**/
		END		
		

		/***************************************************************/
		IF(ISNULL(@ordNumber,0) > 0)
		BEGIN
			SET @ordNumberStr = CAST(@ordNumber AS NVARCHAR(200))
		END


		/*------------ Date ----------*/
		IF(@LanguageID = 1065)/*Persian*/
		BEGIN
		/**/
			SET @prjRemindDate = (SELECT dbo.[fnGregorianToPersianDateWithSlash](@prjRemindDateTime))
			SET @ordDate = (SELECT dbo.[fnGregorianToPersianDateWithSlash](@ordDateTime))	
		/**/
		END
		ELSE IF(@LanguageID <> 1065)
		BEGIN
		/**/
			SET @prjRemindDate = CAST(CAST(@prjRemindDateTime AS DATE) AS NVARCHAR(10))
			SET @ordDate = CAST(CAST(@ordDateTime AS DATE) AS NVARCHAR(10))
		/**/
		END


		/********************** Set pattern for body *******************/
		SET @SmsBodyPatternFormulaResult = @SmsBodyPatternFormula
		/****/
		SET @SmsBodyPatternFormulaResult = REPLACE(@SmsBodyPatternFormulaResult, N'[#����� �����#]', ISNULL(@CmpTitel, ''))
		SET @SmsBodyPatternFormulaResult = REPLACE(@SmsBodyPatternFormulaResult, N'[#��� �����#]', ISNULL(@CmpName, ''))
		SET @SmsBodyPatternFormulaResult = REPLACE(@SmsBodyPatternFormulaResult, N'[#����� �����#]', ISNULL(@PrsTitel, ''))
		SET @SmsBodyPatternFormulaResult = REPLACE(@SmsBodyPatternFormulaResult, N'[#��� �捘 �����#]', ISNULL(@PrsFName, ''))
		SET @SmsBodyPatternFormulaResult = REPLACE(@SmsBodyPatternFormulaResult, N'[#��� �����ϐ� �����#]', ISNULL(@PrsLName, ''))
		SET @SmsBodyPatternFormulaResult = REPLACE(@SmsBodyPatternFormulaResult, N'[#����� ����#]', ISNULL(@prjTitle, ''))
		SET @SmsBodyPatternFormulaResult = REPLACE(@SmsBodyPatternFormulaResult, N'[#����� ����#]', ISNULL(@prjPostFix, ''))
		SET @SmsBodyPatternFormulaResult = REPLACE(@SmsBodyPatternFormulaResult, N'[#����� ������� ����#]', ISNULL(@prjRemindDate, ''))
		SET @SmsBodyPatternFormulaResult = REPLACE(@SmsBodyPatternFormulaResult, N'[#����� �ǘ���#]', ISNULL(@ordNumberStr, ''))
		SET @SmsBodyPatternFormulaResult = REPLACE(@SmsBodyPatternFormulaResult, N'[#����� ���� �ǘ���#]', ISNULL(@ordDate, ''))
		SET @SmsBodyPatternFormulaResult = REPLACE(@SmsBodyPatternFormulaResult, N'[#��� �ǘ���#]', ISNULL(@OrderOpr, ''))
		SET @SmsBodyPatternFormulaResult = REPLACE(@SmsBodyPatternFormulaResult, N'[#��� �ǘ��� �� ��Ԑ��#]', ISNULL(@FoLink, ''))
		SET @SmsBodyPatternFormulaResult = REPLACE(@SmsBodyPatternFormulaResult, N'[#��� �ǘ��� �� ���� (�ǐ��) �� ��Ԑ��#]', ISNULL(@FoLinkWithLogin, ''))
		SET @SmsBodyPatternFormulaResult = REPLACE(@SmsBodyPatternFormulaResult, N'[#��� �ǘ��� ���� ���� (�ǐ��) �� ��Ԑ��#]', ISNULL(@FoLinkWithoutLogin, ''))
		/**/
	END


	IF (ISNULL(@SmsBodyPatternFormulaResult, '') <> '')
	BEGIN
		/**/
		DECLARE @PersonMobileNumber VARCHAR(50),
				@SendType INT,
				@SendTopic NVARCHAR(50),
				@SID INT,
				@smsBody NVARCHAR(2000),
				@SmsBodyLen INT,
				@SmsBodyLangType VARCHAR(50),
				@SmsBodyPageCnt VARCHAR(50),
				@FromNumber VARCHAR(50),
				@SmsEnCost NUMERIC,
				@SmsFaCost NUMERIC,
				@SmsBodyCost NUMERIC,
				@NumberCount INT,
				@Numbers VARCHAR(MAX),
				@RefNumbers VARCHAR(MAX),
				@AllSmsCount INT,
				@UserCost NUMERIC,
				@SendUserId INT,
				@SendDay NVARCHAR(50),
				@SendDate VARCHAR(8),
				@SendTime NVARCHAR(50),
				@CompaniesId VARCHAR(MAX),
				@CompanyPersonsId VARCHAR(MAX),
				@SingleNumber VARCHAR(20) = NULL,
				@SingleCompanyId INT = NULL,
				@SingleCompanyPersonId INT = NULL,
				@SingleRefNumber BIGINT = NULL ,
				@StatusId INT,
				@CMessage INT,
				@SchedulDate DATETIME  = NULL,
				@SendPriority INT = NULL,
				@SendDateTime DATETIME

		DECLARE @SMSSendDate DATETIME = GETDATE()
		DECLARE @ValueForSMSSendDate NVARCHAR(1000) = (SELECT dbo.[fnGregorianToPersianDate](CAST(@SMSSendDate AS DATE)))
		DECLARE @calcSendDay NVARCHAR(50) = (SELECT DATEPART(dw, @SMSSendDate)) 
		DECLARE @calcSendDate VARCHAR(8) = (SELECT SUBSTRING(@ValueForSMSSendDate, 1, 4) + SUBSTRING(@ValueForSMSSendDate, 6, 2) + SUBSTRING(@ValueForSMSSendDate, 9, 2) )
		DECLARE @calcSendTime NVARCHAR(50) = (SELECT CONVERT(CHAR(8), @SMSSendDate, 108))
		DECLARE @calcSendDateTime DATETIME = @SMSSendDate

		/**/
		SET @PersonMobileNumber = ISNULL(@cmpMobile, @prsMobile) 
		SET @SendTopic = NULL
		SET @SID = (Select dbo.SmsfnReturnSID())
		SET @smsBody = @SMSBodyPatternFormulaResult
		SELECT 
			@SmsBodyLen = BodyLen, 
			@SmsBodyLangType = BodyLang, 
			@SmsBodyPageCnt = BodyPageCount
		FROM SmsReturnBodyTextLang(@smsBody)
		/**/
		SELECT TOP 1 
			@FromNumber = Number,
			@SmsEnCost = EnCost, 
			@SmsFaCost = FaCost FROM dbo.SmsSetting 
		/**/
		IF @SmsBodyLangType = 'En'
		SET @SmsBodyCost = ISNULL(@SmsEnCost, 0) * @SmsBodyPageCnt
		ELSE 
		IF @SmsBodyLangType = 'Fa'
		SET @SmsBodyCost = ISNULL(@SmsFaCost, 0) * @SmsBodyPageCnt
		/**/
		SET @NumberCount = 1
		SET @Numbers = @PersonMobileNumber
		SET @RefNumbers = '0,'
		SET @AllSmsCount = @NumberCount * @SmsBodyPageCnt
		SET @UserCost = @NumberCount * @SmsBodyPageCnt * @SmsBodyCost
		SET @SendUserId = @UserID
		SET @SendDay = @calcSendDay
		SET @SendDate = @calcSendDate
		SET @SendTime = @calcSendTime
		SET @CompaniesId = @CompanyId
		SET @CompanyPersonsId = @PrsId
		SET @SingleNumber = @Numbers
		SET @SingleCompanyId = @CompanyId
		SET @SingleCompanyPersonId = @PrsId
		SET @SingleRefNumber = 0
		SET @StatusId = -2000
		SET @CMessage = 1
		SET @SchedulDate = NULL 
		SET @SendPriority = 1
		SET @SendDateTime = @calcSendDateTime 

		IF (@SendSMSOrdFOLinkAfterVerifyWeb = 1) AND (@PersonMobileNumber <> '') AND (@smsBody <> '')
		BEGIN
			/**/
			IF (ISNULL(@SendSMSOrdFOLinkAfterVerifyNumber, '') <> '')
				SET @FromNumber = ISNULL(@SendSMSOrdFOLinkAfterVerifyNumber, @FromNumber)
			SET @SendType = 1
			EXEC dbo.MobspInsertSendedSms 
				@SendType,
				@SendTopic,
				@SID,
				@FromNumber,
				@SmsBodyPatternFormulaResult,
				@SmsBodyPageCnt,
				@SmsBodyLangType,
				@SmsBodyCost,
				@NumberCount,
				@Numbers,
				@RefNumbers,
				@AllSmsCount,
				@UserCost,
				@SendUserId,
				@SendDay,
				@SendDate,
				@SendTime,
				@SendDateTime, 
				@CompaniesId,
				@CompanyPersonsId,
				@UserId,
				@Year1,
				@Month1,
				@RegisterDatePersian,
				@SingleNumber,
				@SingleCompanyId,
				@SingleCompanyPersonId,
				@SingleRefNumber,
				@AppType,
				@StatusId,
				@CMessage,
				@SchedulDate,
				@SendPriority,
				@DefaultLanguageID,
				0,
				@ProjectId,
				null,
				null,
				@SendSMSOrdFOLinkAfterVerifyWebProviderId
			/**/
			SET @Result = 1
			/**/
		END
		IF (@SendSMSOrdFOLinkAfterVerifyGSM = 1) AND (@PersonMobileNumber <> '') AND (@smsBody <> '')
		BEGIN
			/**/
			SET @SendType = 21
			EXEC dbo.MobspInsertSendedSms 
				@SendType,
				@SendTopic,
				@SID,
				@FromNumber,
				@smsBody,
				@SmsBodyPageCnt,
				@SmsBodyLangType,
				@SmsBodyCost,
				@NumberCount,
				@Numbers,
				@RefNumbers,
				@AllSmsCount,
				@UserCost,
				@SendUserId,
				@SendDay,
				@SendDate,
				@SendTime,
				@SendDateTime, 
				@CompaniesId,
				@CompanyPersonsId,
				@UserId,
				@Year1,
				@Month1,
				@RegisterDatePersian,
				@SingleNumber,
				@SingleCompanyId,
				@SingleCompanyPersonId,
				@SingleRefNumber,
				@AppType,
				@StatusId,
				@CMessage,
				@SchedulDate,
				@SendPriority,
				@DefaultLanguageID,
				0,
				@ProjectId,
				null,
				null
			/**/
			SET @Result = 1
			/**/
		END
		/**/
	END
	/**/
END

-- [dbo].[MobspSetcmpDataPermission]







/*RZ961205*/
/*AI14000621 - Skip the operation for userId < 0*/
/*AI14000710 - Remove skip the operation for userId < 0 because of adding a record for user -20 at dbo.UserPermission*/
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[MobspSetcmpDataPermission]
(		
/* 
	EXEC MobspSetcmpDataPermission 3 
*/
@UserId INT
)
AS
BEGIN
	DECLARE @Term VARCHAR(4000)
	SET @Term = '';
	/*IF(@UserId > 0) /*AI14000621 - IF added*/
	BEGIN AI14000710*/
	/*--Province*/
	IF  (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM dbo.CrmCompanyForbiddenProvince
	WHERE CrmUserId = @UserId ) > 0
	SET @Term = @Term +
	' AND C.cmpProvinceId Not IN (
	SELECT ForbiddenProvinceId FROM dbo.CrmCompanyForbiddenProvince
	WHERE CrmUserId = '+CAST(@UserId AS VARCHAR(10))+')'
	IF  (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM dbo.CrmCompanyJustAccessProvince
	WHERE CrmUserId = @UserId ) > 0
	SET @Term = @Term +
	' AND C.cmpProvinceId IN (
	SELECT JustAccessProvinceId FROM dbo.CrmCompanyJustAccessProvince
	WHERE CrmUserId = '+CAST(@UserId AS VARCHAR(10))+')'
	/*--City*/
	IF  (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM dbo.CrmCompanyForbiddenCity
	WHERE CrmUserId = @UserId ) > 0
	SET @Term = @Term +
	' AND C.cmpCityId Not IN (
	SELECT ForbiddenCityId FROM dbo.CrmCompanyForbiddenCity
	WHERE CrmUserId = '+CAST(@UserId AS VARCHAR(10))+')'
	IF  (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM dbo.CrmCompanyJustAccessCity
	WHERE CrmUserId = @UserId ) > 0
	SET @Term = @Term +
	' AND C.cmpCityId IN (
	SELECT JustAccessCityId FROM dbo.CrmCompanyJustAccessCity
	WHERE CrmUserId = '+CAST(@UserId AS VARCHAR(10))+')'
	/*--GroupIV*/
	IF  (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM dbo.CrmCompanyForbiddenGroupIV
	WHERE CrmUserId = @UserId ) > 0
	SET @Term = @Term +
	' AND IsNull(C.cmpGroupIdIV, 0) Not IN (
	SELECT ForbiddenGroupIVId FROM dbo.CrmCompanyForbiddenGroupIV
	WHERE CrmUserId = '+CAST(@UserId AS VARCHAR(10))+')'
	IF  (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM dbo.CrmCompanyJustAccessGroupIV
	WHERE CrmUserId = @UserId ) > 0
	SET @Term = @Term +
	' AND IsNull(C.cmpGroupIdIV, 0) IN (
	SELECT JustAccessGroupIVId FROM dbo.CrmCompanyJustAccessGroupIV
	WHERE CrmUserId = '+CAST(@UserId AS VARCHAR(10))+')'
	/*--Company*/
	IF  (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM dbo.CrmCompanyForbiddenCompany
	WHERE CrmUserId = @UserId ) > 0
	SET @Term = @Term +
	' AND C.CompanyId Not IN (
	SELECT ForbiddenCompanyId FROM dbo.CrmCompanyForbiddenCompany
	WHERE CrmUserId = '+CAST(@UserId AS VARCHAR(10))+')'
	IF  (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM dbo.CrmCompanyJustAccessCompany
	WHERE CrmUserId = @UserId ) > 0
	SET @Term = @Term +
	' AND C.CompanyId IN (
	SELECT JustAccessCompanyId FROM dbo.CrmCompanyJustAccessCompany
	WHERE CrmUserId = '+CAST(@UserId AS VARCHAR(10))+')'
	/*--Group*/
	IF  (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM dbo.CrmCompanyForbiddenGroup
	WHERE CrmUserId = @UserId ) > 0
	SET @Term = @Term +
	' AND IsNull(C.cmpGroupId, 0) Not IN (
	SELECT ForbiddenGroupId FROM dbo.CrmCompanyForbiddenGroup
	WHERE CrmUserId = '+CAST(@UserId AS VARCHAR(10))+')'
	IF  (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM dbo.CrmCompanyJustAccessGroup
	WHERE CrmUserId = @UserId ) > 0
	SET @Term = @Term +
	' AND IsNull(C.cmpGroupId, 0) IN (
	SELECT JustAccessGroupId FROM dbo.CrmCompanyJustAccessGroup
	WHERE CrmUserId = '+CAST(@UserId AS VARCHAR(10))+')'
	/*--SubGroup*/
	IF  (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM dbo.CrmCompanyForbiddenSubGroup
	WHERE CrmUserId = @UserId ) > 0
	SET @Term = @Term +
	' AND IsNull(C.cmpSubGroupId, 0) Not IN (
	SELECT ForbiddenSubGroupId FROM dbo.CrmCompanyForbiddenSubGroup
	WHERE CrmUserId = '+CAST(@UserId AS VARCHAR(10))+')'
	IF  (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM dbo.CrmCompanyJustAccessSubGroup
	WHERE CrmUserId = @UserId ) > 0
	SET @Term = @Term +
	' AND IsNull(C.cmpSubGroupId, 0) IN (
	SELECT JustAccessSubGroupId FROM dbo.CrmCompanyJustAccessSubGroup
	WHERE CrmUserId = '+CAST(@UserId AS VARCHAR(10))+')'
	/*--*/
	/*--cmpReferUser*/
	SET @Term = @Term +
	' And C.cmpReferUserId IN( 
	(SELECT '+CAST(@UserId AS VARCHAR(10)) + ' UNION 
	SELECT LinkedUserId FROM dbo.CrmLinkedUsers WHERE CrmUserId = '+ 
	CAST(@UserId AS VARCHAR(10)) + ' AND LinkedTypeId = 14 ) ) '

	/*END AI14000710-End*/
	/*
	--
	--
	--
	--
	*/
	DELETE dbo.CrmsysDBTempInfo
	WHERE TypeId = 20
	AND UserId = @UserId
	/*--*/
	INSERT INTO dbo.CrmsysDBTempInfo (
		UserId,	TXT, TypeId)
	 VALUES ( @UserId, @Term, 20 ) 


END

-- [dbo].[MobspTransferPreOrderFileToOrderOneToOne]





/*AI14010711 - Copied from web*/
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[MobspTransferPreOrderFileToOrderOneToOne]
(		
	/*
		EXEC [MobspTransferPreOrderFileToOrderOneToOne] 66,3,2,4,'13960427','dasti'
	*/
	@PreOrderId INT, 
	@OrderId INT,
	@FileDirectoryId INT,
	@FileDirectoryTypeId INT,
	@UserId INT,	
	@FPId INT,
	@Year1 SMALLINT,
	@Month1 SMALLINT,
	@RegisterDate DATETIME,
	@RegisterDatePersian VARCHAR(8),
	@AppType NVARCHAR(50),
	@LanguageId INT,
	@InsertedId INT OUTPUT
)
As
BEGIN
	/**/
	DECLARE @ordProjectId INT = NULL
	SET @ordProjectId = (SELECT TOP(1) ordProjectId FROM WSOrder WHERE OrderID = @OrderId)
	
	INSERT INTO CrmFilesDirectory(
		FileDirectoryTypeId,
		MasterId,
		DetailId,
		SubDetailId,
		Title,
		FolderName,
		[FileName],
		FilePath,
		CopyFromPath,
		UserId,
		Year1,
		Month1,
		RegisterDate,
		RegisterDatePersian,
		DocDate,
		DocDay,
		DocTime,
		DocDateTime,
		TypeOprObjectId,
		AppType,
		LanguageID,
		FirstRegisterUserId,
		FirstRegisterDate,
		FirstRegisterDatePersian,
		OrderView,
		FileFOCmpCanSee,
		FileDirectoryParentId
	)
	SELECT 
		@FileDirectoryTypeId,
		FD.MasterId,
		FD.DetailId,
		@ordProjectId,
		FD.Title,
		FD.FolderName,
		FD.[FileName],
		FD.FilePath,
		FD.CopyFromPath,
		@UserId,
		@Year1,
		@Month1,
		@RegisterDate,
		@RegisterDatePersian,
		FD.DocDate,
		FD.DocDay,
		FD.DocTime,
		FD.DocDateTime,
		@OrderId,
		@AppType,
		@LanguageId,
		@UserId,
		@RegisterDate,
		@RegisterDatePersian,
		FD.OrderView,
		FileFOCmpCanSee,
		FD.FileDirectoryID
	FROM CrmFilesDirectory FD
	WHERE  fd.FileDirectoryID = @FileDirectoryId and
			fd.FileDirectoryID not in (
				select d.FileDirectoryParentId from CrmFilesDirectory d 
				where TypeOprObjectId = @OrderId and 
					d.FileDirectoryTypeId IN (
						SELECT FileDirectoryTypeID 
						FROM dbo.CrmFileDirectoryType 
						WHERE FileDirectoryTypeOprId = 20)
			)

	SET @InsertedId = SCOPE_IDENTITY()

	IF (ISNULL(@InsertedId, 0) > 0)
	BEGIN
		/**/
		INSERT INTO CrmFilesDirectory_Lang(
			FileDirectoryID,
			LanguageID,
			Title
		)
		SELECT 
			@InsertedId,
			FDL.LanguageID,
			FDL.Title
		FROM CrmFilesDirectory_Lang FDL
		WHERE FileDirectoryID = @FileDirectoryId
		/**/
	END
	/**/
END

-- [dbo].[MobspUpdateOrderLinkAccConflictByPay]






/*AI14020830 - Copied from web*/
/*AI14021005 - The date is not important in the type of Chq*/
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[MobspUpdateOrderLinkAccConflictByPay]
/* 
	EXEC MobspUpdateOrderLinkAccConflictByPay 41734
*/		
(
@OrderId INT
)
AS
BEGIN
/**/
/*----------------------------------------------*/
DECLARE @ordLinkAccConflict BIT = 0
/*--------------- Check Payment ----------------*/
IF(ISNULL((SELECT TOP (1) [OrderPaymentId]      
		  FROM [dbo].[WSOrderPayments]
		  WHERE [ordpayOrderId] = @OrderId
		  AND ISNULL([ordpayDeActive],0) = 0
		  AND (
				(ISNULL([ordpayDocAmount],0) <> 0 AND ISNULL([ordpayBankDocAmount],0) <> 0 AND ISNULL([ordpayDocAmount],0) <> ISNULL([ordpayBankDocAmount],0))
				OR
				([ordpayDocDateTime] IS NOT NULL AND [ordpayBankDocDate] IS NOT NULL AND CAST([ordpayDocDateTime] AS DATE) <> CAST([ordpayBankDocDate] AS DATE)
				 AND [ordpayTypeId] NOT IN(SELECT [OrderPaymentTypeId] FROM [dbo].[WSOrderPaymentTypes] WHERE ISNULL([ordpayTypeIsChq],0) = 1)/*AI14021005 - The date is not important in the type of Chq*/
				)
			  )
   ),0) <> 0)
BEGIN
/**/
	SET @ordLinkAccConflict = 1
/**/
END
/*--------------- Update Order -----------------*/
EXEC [dbo].[MobspUpdateOrderLinkAccConflict]
@OrderId = @OrderId,
@ordLinkAccConflict = @ordLinkAccConflict
/*------------------------------------------------*/
/**/
END

-- [dbo].[MobspUpdateProjectFromIssueOrderFromPreOrder]






/*AI14020703*/
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[MobspUpdateProjectFromIssueOrderFromPreOrder]
/*
	EXEC MobspUpdateProjectFromIssueOrderFromPreOrder
*/
(
@ProjectParams NVARCHAR(MAX),
@UserId INT,
@Month1 SMALLINT,
@RegisterDate DATETIME,
@RegisterDatePersian NVARCHAR(8),
@AppType VARCHAR(20), 
@LanguageID INT = NULL,
@DefaultLanguageID INT = NULL,
/**/
@ResErrorMessage NVARCHAR(MAX) OUTPUT
)
AS
BEGIN
/**/
	DECLARE 
		@ProjectId INT,
		@prjTitle NVARCHAR(100),
		@prjPostFix NVARCHAR(200),
		@prjRemindDate VARCHAR(8),
		@prjRemindDateTime DATETIME,
		@prjTitleGroup NVARCHAR(200)


	BEGIN TRY

		IF(ISNULL(@ProjectParams, '') <> '')
		BEGIN
			SELECT
				@ProjectId = PJ.ProjectId,
				@prjTitle = PJ.prjTitle,
				@prjPostFix = PJ.prjPostFix,
				@prjRemindDate = PJ.prjRemindDate,
				@prjTitleGroup = PJ.prjTitleGroup
			FROM OPENJSON(@ProjectParams)
			WITH(
				[ProjectId] INT '$.ProjectId',
				[prjTitle] NVARCHAR(100) '$.prjTitle',
				[prjPostFix] NVARCHAR(200) '$.prjPostFix',
				[prjRemindDate] VARCHAR(8) '$.prjRemindDate',
				[prjTitleGroup] NVARCHAR(200) '$.prjTitleGroup'
			) AS PJ
		END

	END TRY
	BEGIN CATCH
		SET @ResErrorMessage = @ResErrorMessage + '#|^|#' + 'ParseData=Error'
		GOTO EndOfScript
	END CATCH

	IF(ISNULL(@prjRemindDate, '') <> '')
	BEGIN
		SET @prjRemindDateTime = dbo.fnPersianToGregorianDate(@prjRemindDate, NULL)
	END
	ELSE
	BEGIN
		SET @prjRemindDateTime = NULL
	END

	UPDATE [dbo].[CrmProjects]
		SET [prjTitle] = @prjTitle
		,[prjPostFix] = @prjPostFix
		,[prjRemindDate] = @prjRemindDate
		,[prjRemindDateTime] = @prjRemindDateTime
		,[prjTitleGroup] = @prjTitleGroup
		,[UserId] = @UserId
		,[Month1] = @Month1
		,[RegisterDate] = @RegisterDate
		,[RegisterDatePersian] = @RegisterDatePersian
		,[AppType] = @AppType
	WHERE [ProjectID] = @ProjectId

	/******* Update lang table ********/
	UPDATE [dbo].[CrmProjects_Lang]
		SET [prjTitle] = @prjTitle
		,[prjPostFix] = @prjPostFix
		,[prjTitleGroup] = @prjTitleGroup
	FROM dbo.Languages LN 
	WHERE [ProjectID] = @ProjectId
	AND LN.LanguageID <> @DefaultLanguageID	
	AND ISNULL(LN.IsActive,0) = 1

	EndOfScript:
		PRINT 'EndOfScript:'
/**/
END







GO
-- [dbo].[MobspVerificationRequestVerify]



/*AI14010512 - Copied from web*/
/*AI14011223 - Send int msg without updating user receiver*/
/*AI14020129 - Add doc - add leave - add responsible user*/
/*AI14020701 - Add cmpReferUserId and cmpSuppUserId to verifyUser*/
/*AI14020918- Add WSStockSupply and buyOrder*/
/*RZ14021108- Check for a preorder not items*/
/*RZ14030308- Add VerificationWorkFlowId to letter*/
Create PROCEDURE [dbo].[MobspVerificationRequestVerify]
(		
/* 
	EXEC MobspVerificationRequestVerify 		
*/
@VerificationWorkFlowId INT,
@ObjectId INT,
@VerificationRequestId INT,
@VerificationWorkFlowStepId INT,
@VerifyText NVARCHAR(MAX),
@VerifyTextLang NVARCHAR(MAX),
@MsgSubject NVARCHAR(200),
@VerifiedMsgSubject NVARCHAR(200),
@RollId INT,
@RegisterDate DATETIME,
@RegisterDatePersian NVARCHAR(8),
@UserId INT,
@FPId INT,
@Year1 SMALLINT,
@Month1 SMALLINT,
@AppType VARCHAR(20),
@LanguageID INT = NULL,
@DefaultLanguageID INT = NULL,
@MaxDiscountPercent NUMERIC(18,2) OUTPUT,
@Result NVARCHAR(MAX) OUTPUT 
)
AS
BEGIN
/**/
/*===================================== Define variables ==================================*/
DECLARE @ObjectType INT, @SequenceObservation BIT,
@LimitPreOrderDiscountActive BIT,@LimitPreOrderDiscountPercent NUMERIC(18,2),
@LimitOrderDiscountActive BIT,@LimitOrderDiscountPercent NUMERIC(18,2),
@DiscountPercent NUMERIC(18,2),@SumPriceAmount NUMERIC(18,2),
@SumDiscount NUMERIC(18,2),
@MsgTypeId INT,@VerifyUserId INT, @RegisterTime NVARCHAR(8),@InternalMessageId INT
,@TotalPriceSumWithoutDiscount NUMERIC(18,2),@TotalDiscountSum NUMERIC(18,2)
SET @RegisterTime = CAST(@RegisterDate AS TIME)
/*============================= SET Object and @SequenceObservation =======================*/	
SELECT @ObjectType = [ObjectType], @SequenceObservation = [SequenceObservation]
FROM [dbo].[WSVerificationWorkFlows]
WHERE VerificationWorkFlowId = @VerificationWorkFlowId
/*================================= Check Discount Percent ================================*/
SELECT @LimitPreOrderDiscountActive = LimitPreOrderDiscountActive, 
@LimitPreOrderDiscountPercent = LimitPreOrderDiscountPercent,
@LimitOrderDiscountActive = LimitOrderDiscountActive,
@LimitOrderDiscountPercent = LimitOrderDiscountPercent
FROM dbo.CrmsysRolls
WHERE ID = @RollId

IF(ISNULL(@ObjectType,0) = 10)/*PreOrder*/
BEGIN
/**/
	IF(ISNULL(@LimitPreOrderDiscountActive,0) = 1)
	BEGIN
		SET @MaxDiscountPercent = ISNULL(@LimitPreOrderDiscountPercent,0)
	END
	/**/
	IF(ISNULL(@MaxDiscountPercent,0) > 0)
	BEGIN
		DECLARE @pordDiscount NUMERIC(18,2)
		SET @pordDiscount = (SELECT pordDiscount FROM dbo.WSPreOrder WHERE PreOrderID = @ObjectId)
		SELECT 
			@SumPriceAmount = SumPriceAmount,
			@SumDiscount = SumDiscount, 
			@TotalPriceSumWithoutDiscount = TotalPriceSumWithoutDiscount ,
			@TotalDiscountSum = TotalDiscountSum
		FROM [dbo].[MobfnReturnPreOrderSumFooter](@ObjectId)				

		/**/				
		/*RZ14021108- Check for a preorder not items*/
		/*IF(ISNULL(@SumPriceAmount,0) = 0)		
			SET @Result = '-1' /-*DivisionByZero*-/
		ELSE*/	
		IF(ISNULL(@SumPriceAmount,0) = 0 AND ISNULL(@TotalPriceSumWithoutDiscount,0) <> 0 )	
		BEGIN
		/**/
			SET @DiscountPercent = (ISNULL(@TotalDiscountSum,0) * 100) / ISNULL(@TotalPriceSumWithoutDiscount,0)

			IF(ISNULL(@DiscountPercent,0) > ISNULL(@MaxDiscountPercent,0) AND ISNULL(@Result,'') ='')
			SET @Result = '-2' /*The product discount is too high*/
			/**/					
		END
		/*RZ14021108- End*/
		IF(ISNULL(@SumPriceAmount,0) <> 0 AND ISNULL(@TotalPriceSumWithoutDiscount,0) <> 0  AND ISNULL(@Result,'') ='')
		BEGIN
		/**/
			/*SET @DiscountPercent = (ISNULL(@SumDiscount,0) * 100) / ISNULL(@SumPriceAmount,0)*//*calculate product discount*/
			SET @DiscountPercent = (ISNULL(@TotalDiscountSum,0) * 100) / ISNULL(@TotalPriceSumWithoutDiscount,0)

			IF(ISNULL(@DiscountPercent,0) > ISNULL(@MaxDiscountPercent,0) AND ISNULL(@Result,'') ='')
			SET @Result = '-2' /*The product discount is too high*/
			/**/
			SET @DiscountPercent = (ISNULL(@pordDiscount,0) * 100) / ISNULL(@SumPriceAmount,0)/*calculate preorder discount*/
			IF(ISNULL(@DiscountPercent,0) > ISNULL(@MaxDiscountPercent,0) AND ISNULL(@Result,'') ='')
				SET @Result = '-3' /*The pre-order discount is too high*/	
		/**/
		END
		/*RZ14021108- Check for a preorder not items*/
		ELSE IF(ISNULL(@SumPriceAmount,0) = 0 AND ISNULL(@pordDiscount,0) <> 0)	
		BEGIN
			SET @Result = '-1' /*DivisionByZero*/
		END
		/*RZ14021108- End*/

	END
/**/
END
ELSE IF(ISNULL(@ObjectType,0) = 20)/*Order*/
BEGIN
/**/
	IF(ISNULL(@LimitOrderDiscountActive,0) = 1)
	BEGIN
		SET @MaxDiscountPercent = ISNULL(@LimitOrderDiscountPercent,0)
	END
	/**/
	IF(ISNULL(@MaxDiscountPercent,0) > 0)
	BEGIN
		DECLARE @ordDiscount NUMERIC(18,2)
		SET @ordDiscount = (SELECT ordDiscount FROM dbo.WSOrder WHERE OrderID = @ObjectId)
		SELECT 
			@SumPriceAmount = SumPriceAmount,
			@SumDiscount = SumDiscount,
			@TotalPriceSumWithoutDiscount = TotalPriceSumWithoutDiscount ,
			@TotalDiscountSum = TotalDiscountSum
		FROM [dbo].[MobfnReturnOrderSumFooter](@ObjectId)
		/**/				
		/*RZ14021108- Check for a preorder not items*/
		/*IF(ISNULL(@SumPriceAmount,0) = 0)		
			SET @Result = '-1' /-*DivisionByZero*-/
		ELSE*/
		IF(ISNULL(@SumPriceAmount,0) = 0 AND ISNULL(@TotalPriceSumWithoutDiscount,0) <> 0)	
		BEGIN
		/**/
			SET @DiscountPercent = (ISNULL(@TotalDiscountSum,0) * 100) / ISNULL(@TotalPriceSumWithoutDiscount,0)
			/**/
			IF(ISNULL(@DiscountPercent,0) > ISNULL(@MaxDiscountPercent,0) AND ISNULL(@Result,'') ='')
			SET @Result = '-2' /*The product discount is too high*/
		/**/
		END
		/*RZ14021108- End*/
		IF(ISNULL(@SumPriceAmount,0) <> 0 AND ISNULL(@TotalPriceSumWithoutDiscount,0) <> 0 AND ISNULL(@Result,'') ='')
		BEGIN
			/*SET @DiscountPercent = (ISNULL(@SumDiscount,0) * 100) / ISNULL(@SumPriceAmount,0)*//*calculate product discount*/
			SET @DiscountPercent = (ISNULL(@TotalDiscountSum,0) * 100) / ISNULL(@TotalPriceSumWithoutDiscount,0)
			/**/
			IF(ISNULL(@DiscountPercent,0) > ISNULL(@MaxDiscountPercent,0) AND ISNULL(@Result,'') ='')
			SET @Result = '-2' /*The product discount is too high*/
			/**/
			SET @DiscountPercent = (ISNULL(@ordDiscount,0) * 100) / ISNULL(@SumPriceAmount,0)/*calculate order discount*/
			IF(ISNULL(@DiscountPercent,0) > ISNULL(@MaxDiscountPercent,0) AND ISNULL(@Result,'') ='')
				SET @Result = '-3' /*The order discount is too high*/	
		END
		/*RZ14021108- Check for a preorder not items*/
		ELSE IF(ISNULL(@SumPriceAmount,0) = 0 AND ISNULL(@ordDiscount,0) <> 0)	
		BEGIN
			SET @Result = '-1' /*DivisionByZero*/
		END
		/*RZ14021108- End*/
	END
/**/
END
ELSE IF(ISNULL(@ObjectType,0) = 80)/*DyForm*/
BEGIN
/**/
	DECLARE @DyWorkflowId INT = 0;
	SET @DyWorkflowId = (SELECT TOP (1) [WorkflowId]
						FROM [dbo].[CrmDyFormFieldMasterValues]
						WHERE [FormFieldMasterValueId] = @ObjectId)

	IF(ISNULL(@DyWorkflowId,0) <> 0)
	BEGIN
	/**/	
		IF(EXISTS (SELECT [FormId] 
					FROM [dbo].[CrmDyFormFieldMasterValueDetails] VMD 
					WHERE VMD.[MasterValueId] = @ObjectId
					AND  ISNULL(VMD.[IsChecked],0) = 0
					AND VMD.FormId <> (SELECT MV.FormId FROM [dbo].[CrmDyFormFieldMasterValues] MV WHERE MV.FormFieldMasterValueId = @ObjectId)))
		BEGIN
		/**/
			SET @Result = '-6' /*the mastervalue hav masterValueDetails in workflow not checked*/	
		/**/	
		END
	/**/
	END
/**/
END

/*================================== Check steps before current step ======================*/
IF(ISNULL(@SequenceObservation,0) = 1 AND ISNULL(@Result,'') ='')
BEGIN
/**/
	DECLARE @StepBfCurrId INT
	SET @StepBfCurrId = (SELECT TOP 1 [VerificationWorkFlowStepId]       	   
							FROM [dbo].[WSVerificationWorkFlowSteps] VWS
							WHERE VerificationWorkFlowId = @VerificationWorkFlowId
							AND ISNULL([OrderView],[VerificationWorkFlowStepId]) < (SELECT ISNULL([OrderView],[VerificationWorkFlowStepId])  
																						FROM [dbo].[WSVerificationWorkFlowSteps] VWSI 
																						WHERE VWSI.[VerificationWorkFlowStepId] = @VerificationWorkFlowStepId)
							AND [VerificationWorkFlowStepId] NOT IN (SELECT  [VerificationWorkFlowStepId]      
																	 FROM [dbo].[WSVerificationRequestDetails] VRD 
																	 WHERE VRD.[VerificationRequestId] = @VerificationRequestId)	
							ORDER BY ISNULL([OrderView],[VerificationWorkFlowStepId]))
	IF(ISNULL(@StepBfCurrId,0) > 0 )
	BEGIN
		IF(@LanguageID IS NULL OR @LanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID)
		BEGIN
			SET @Result = '-4|' + ISNULL((SELECT [VerificationWorkFlowStepTitle]  	   
											FROM [dbo].[WSVerificationWorkFlowSteps] VWS
											WHERE [VerificationWorkFlowStepId] = @StepBfCurrId),'')
		END
		ELSE IF(@LanguageID <> @DefaultLanguageID)
		BEGIN
			SET @Result = '-4|' + ISNULL((SELECT [VerificationWorkFlowStepTitle] 	   
											FROM [dbo].[WSVerificationWorkFlowSteps_Lang] VWS
											WHERE [VerificationWorkFlowStepId] = @StepBfCurrId AND VWS.LanguageID = @LanguageID),'')	
		END

	END
/**/
END

/*AI14020129 - Add responsible user*/
DECLARE @ResponsibleUserId INT
DECLARE @VerificationReqUserIdI INT
SELECT @VerificationReqUserIdI = [VerificationReqUserId] FROM [dbo].[WSVerificationRequests] WHERE [VerificationRequestId] = @VerificationRequestId
IF(ISNULL(@SequenceObservation,0) = 1)
BEGIN
/**/	
	SELECT TOP 1
		 @VerifyUserId = [VerifyUserId]	
	FROM [dbo].[WSVerificationWorkFlowSteps] VWS
	WHERE [VerificationWorkFlowId] = @VerificationWorkFlowId
	AND [VerificationWorkFlowStepId] NOT IN (SELECT  [VerificationWorkFlowStepId]      
												FROM [dbo].[WSVerificationRequestDetails] VRD 
												WHERE VRD.[VerificationRequestId] = @VerificationRequestId)
	AND [VerificationWorkFlowStepId] <> @VerificationWorkFlowStepId
	ORDER BY ISNULL([OrderView],[VerificationWorkFlowStepId])			
	IF(ISNULL(@VerifyUserId,0) = -101)
	BEGIN
	/**/
		SET @ResponsibleUserId = (SELECT TOP(1) [ResponsibleUserId] FROM [dbo].[Users] U WHERE U.UserId = @VerificationReqUserIdI)
		IF(ISNULL(@ResponsibleUserId,0) = 0)
		BEGIN
		/**/
			SET @Result = '-101' /*Not Set Responsible*/
		/**/
		END
	/**/
	END	
	/*AI14020701- Add cmpReferUserId and cmpSuppUserId to verifyUser*/
	ELSE IF(ISNULL(@VerifyUserId,0) = -102 AND ISNULL(@cmpReferUserId,0) = 0)
	BEGIN
	/**/
		SET @Result = '-102' /*Not Set cmpReferUserId*/
	/**/
	END
	ELSE IF(ISNULL(@VerifyUserId,0) = -103 AND ISNULL(@cmpSuppUserId,0) = 0)
	BEGIN
	/**/
		SET @Result = '-103' /*Not Set cmpSuppUserId*/
	/**/
	END
	/*AI14020701- End*/
/**/
END IF(ISNULL(@SequenceObservation,0) = 0)
BEGIN
/**/
	SELECT TOP 1
		 @VerifyUserId = [VerifyUserId]	
	FROM [dbo].[WSVerificationWorkFlowSteps] VWS
	WHERE [VerificationWorkFlowId] = @VerificationWorkFlowId
	AND [VerificationWorkFlowStepId] NOT IN (SELECT  [VerificationWorkFlowStepId]      
												FROM [dbo].[WSVerificationRequestDetails] VRD 
												WHERE VRD.[VerificationRequestId] = @VerificationRequestId)
	AND [VerificationWorkFlowStepId] <> @VerificationWorkFlowStepId
	AND [VerifyUserId] = -101
	ORDER BY ISNULL([OrderView],[VerificationWorkFlowStepId])

	IF(ISNULL(@VerifyUserId,0) = -101)
	BEGIN
	/**/
		SET @ResponsibleUserId = (SELECT TOP(1) [ResponsibleUserId] FROM [dbo].[Users] U WHERE U.UserId = @VerificationReqUserIdI)
		IF(ISNULL(@ResponsibleUserId,0) = 0)
		BEGIN
		/**/
			SET @Result = '-101' /*Not Set Responsible*/
		/**/
		END
	/**/
	END	
	/*AI14020701- Add cmpReferUserId and cmpSuppUserId to verifyUser*/
	IF(ISNULL(@Result,'') = '')
	BEGIN
	/**/
		SET @VerifyUserId = NULL
		SELECT TOP(1)
			@VerifyUserId = [VerifyUserId]
		FROM [dbo].[WSVerificationWorkFlowSteps] VWS
		WHERE VerificationWorkFlowId = @VerificationWorkFlowId	
		AND [VerifyUserId] = -102
		ORDER BY ISNULL([OrderView],[VerificationWorkFlowStepId])

		IF(ISNULL(@VerifyUserId,0) = -102 AND ISNULL(@cmpReferUserId,0) = 0)
		BEGIN
		/**/
			SET @Result = '-102' /*Not Set cmpReferUserId*/

	END
	
	/*--------*/
	IF(ISNULL(@Result,'') = '')
	BEGIN

		FROM [dbo].[WSVerificationWorkFlowSteps] VWS

		AND [VerifyUserId] = -103
		ORDER BY ISNULL([OrderView],[VerificationWorkFlowStepId])
		IF(ISNULL(@VerifyUserId,0) = -103 AND ISNULL(@cmpSuppUserId,0) = 0)
		BEGIN
		/**/

		/**/

	/*AI14020701- End*/

END
SET @VerifyUserId = NULL
	
/*AI14020129 - End*/
/*===================================== Verify Request =====================================*/
IF(ISNULL(@Result,'') = '')
BEGIN
/**/	
	/*======================== Create Tmp table for language ==========================*/
	/*Sample= @VerifyTextLang = N'[LangId=1033|,|Txt=TextI|,|Subj=subjectI|,|VerifiedSubj=vSubjI|,|]@[LangId=1025|,|Txt=TextII|,|Subj=subjectII|,|VerifiedSubj=vSubjII|,|]@',*/
	DECLARE @TmpTable TABLE (
		[LangId] INT,
		[Txt] NVARCHAR(MAX),
		[Subj] NVARCHAR(200),
		[VerifiedSubj] NVARCHAR(200)
	)
	DECLARE @LangId INT,@Txt NVARCHAR(MAX), @Subj NVARCHAR(200),@Text NVARCHAR(MAX),@VerifiedSubj NVARCHAR(200)
	IF(ISNULL(@VerifyTextLang,'') <> '')
	BEGIN
		/**/	
		DECLARE Crsr Cursor For
		/**/	
			SELECT SplitData  
			FROM fnSplitString(@VerifyTextLang, '@')
		/**/
		OPEN Crsr
		FETCH NEXT FROM Crsr INTO @Text
		WHILE @@Fetch_Status = 0
		BEGIN
		/**/
			/*================================================================================*/
			DECLARE @Field NVARCHAR(MAX) = ''
			SET @LangId = NULL
			SET @Txt = NULL
			SET @Subj = NULL
			SET @VerifiedSubj = NULL
			/**/
			/*****************************************************************/
			IF(ISNULL(@Text,'') <> '') 
			BEGIN
			/**/
				SET @Text = SUBSTRING(@Text, 2, LEN(@Text)-2)			
				/*===================== Get Fields ==========================*/
				WHILE LEN(@Text) > 0
				BEGIN
					IF PATINDEX('%|,|%', @Text) > 0
					BEGIN
						SET @Field = SUBSTRING(@Text, 0, PATINDEX('%|,|%', @Text))
						/*--------------------------------------------------------------------------------*/
						/*Get parameter name and split value form parameter*/
						/*For example => LangId=1065 => LangId= => LangId => Compare => value*/
						DECLARE @FieldValue NVARCHAR(MAX)
						DECLARE @FieldName NVARCHAR(200)
						SET @FieldName = SUBSTRING(@Field, 1, CHARINDEX('=',@Field)-1)                                    
						SET @FieldValue = SUBSTRING(@Field, CHARINDEX('=', @Field)+1, Len(@Field))
						/*--------------------------------------------------------------------------------*/
						/*---------------------------- BEGIN Fill variabele ------------------------------*/
						/*BEGIN*/
						IF @FieldName = 'LangId'
						BEGIN
							SET @LangId = CASE WHEN CAST(ISNULL(@FieldValue,'0') AS INT) = 0 THEN NULL ELSE CAST(@FieldValue AS INT) END
						END
						/*--------------------------*/
						IF @FieldName = 'Txt'
						BEGIN
							SET @Txt = CASE WHEN ISNULL(@FieldValue,'') = '' THEN NULL ELSE @FieldValue END
						END
						/*--------------------------*/
						IF @FieldName = 'Subj'
						BEGIN
							SET @Subj = CASE WHEN ISNULL(@FieldValue,'') = '' THEN NULL ELSE @FieldValue END
						END
						/*--------------------------*/
						IF @FieldName = 'VerifiedSubj'
						BEGIN
							SET @VerifiedSubj = CASE WHEN ISNULL(@FieldValue,'') = '' THEN NULL ELSE @FieldValue END
						END
						/*--------------------------*/				
						/*END */
						/*---------------------------- END Fill variabele --------------------------------*/
						/*---------------------------- Remove current parameter from listparam ------------*/
						SET @Text = SUBSTRING(@Text,LEN(@Field + '|,|') + 1,LEN(@Text))
					END
					ELSE
					BEGIN
						SET @Field = @Text
						SET @Text = NULL       
					END
				END
				/*===========================================================*/
				INSERT INTO @TmpTable ([LangId],Txt,Subj,VerifiedSubj)
				VALUES(@LangId,@Txt,@Subj,@VerifiedSubj)
			/**/
			END
			/*================================================================================*/					    				 					
		/**/	
		FETCH NEXT FROM Crsr INTO @Text
		END
		CLOSE Crsr
		DEALLOCATE Crsr
	/**/
	END		
	/*============================= Insert into WSVerificationRequestDetails ===================*/	
	DECLARE @VerificationRequestDetailId INT
	INSERT INTO [dbo].[WSVerificationRequestDetails]
           ([VerificationRequestId]
           ,[ObjectId]
           ,[ObjectType]
           ,[VerificationWorkFlowId]
           ,[VerificationWorkFlowStepId]
           ,[VerifyUserId]
           ,[VerifyDateTime]
           ,[VerifyText]
           ,[FirstRegisterUserId]
           ,[FirstRegisterDate]
           ,[FirstRegisterDatePersian]
           ,[UserId]
           ,[Year1]
           ,[Month1]
           ,[RegisterDate]
           ,[RegisterDatePersian]
           ,[AppType]
           ,[LanguageID])
    VALUES
           (@VerificationRequestId
           ,@ObjectId
           ,@ObjectType
           ,@VerificationWorkFlowId
           ,@VerificationWorkFlowStepId
           ,@UserId
           ,@RegisterDate
           ,@VerifyText
           ,@UserId
           ,@RegisterDate
           ,@RegisterDatePersian
           ,@UserId
           ,@FPId
           ,@Month1
           ,@RegisterDate
           ,@RegisterDatePersian
           ,@AppType
           ,@DefaultLanguageID)
	SET @VerificationRequestDetailId = SCOPE_IDENTITY()
	IF(ISNULL(@VerificationRequestDetailId,0) > 0)
	BEGIN
	/**/
		/*Insert into lang tabel*/
		INSERT INTO [dbo].[WSVerificationRequestDetails_Lang]
           ([VerificationRequestDetailId]
           ,[LanguageID]
           ,[VerifyText])
		SELECT
            @VerificationRequestDetailId
           ,[LangId]
           ,[Txt]
		FROM @TmpTable
		WHERE [LangId] <> 0
		/**/
		/*============================= IsFixed Object =======================*/	
		IF(ISNULL(@ObjectType,0) > 0)
		BEGIN
		/**/
			/*Add (@ObjectType = 30) ==> BuyOrder,(@ObjectType = 40) ==> Do */
			IF(@ObjectType = 10)/*PreOrder*/
			BEGIN
				UPDATE [dbo].[WSPreOrder]
					SET [pordIsFixed] = 1      
				WHERE PreOrderID = @ObjectId
				SET @MsgTypeId = 5
			END
			ELSE IF(@ObjectType = 20)/*Order*/
			BEGIN
				UPDATE [dbo].[WSOrder]
					SET [ordIsFixed] = 1      
				WHERE OrderID = @ObjectId
				SET @MsgTypeId = 8
			END
			/*AI14020918- Add WSStockSupply and buyOrder*/
			ELSE IF(@ObjectType = 40)/*BuyOrder*/
			BEGIN
			/**/
				UPDATE [dbo].[WSBuyOrder]
					SET [bordIsFixed] = 1      
				WHERE BuyOrderID = @ObjectId
				SET @MsgTypeId = 9	
			/**/
			END
			ELSE IF(@ObjectType = 45)/*StockSupply*/
			BEGIN
			/**/
				UPDATE [dbo].[WSStockSupply]
					SET [stsupIsFixed] = 1      
				WHERE StockSupplyId = @ObjectId
				SET @MsgTypeId = 21	
			/**/
			END
			/*AI14020918- End*/
			ELSE IF(@ObjectType = 80)/*DyForm*/
			BEGIN
			/**/				
				SET @MsgTypeId = 16
			/**/
			END
			/*AI14020129 - add leave and doc*/
			ELSE IF(@ObjectType = 60)/*Doc*/
			BEGIN
			/**/
				UPDATE [dbo].[WSDoc]
					SET [IsFix] = 1      
				WHERE DocID = @ObjectId
				SET @MsgTypeId = 20	
			/**/
			END
			ELSE IF(@ObjectType = 90)/*leave*/
			BEGIN
				UPDATE [dbo].[CrmLeave]
				   SET [VerificationWorkFlowId] = @VerificationWorkFlowId      
				WHERE [LeaveId] = @ObjectId
				SET @MsgTypeId = 6
			END
			ELSE IF(@ObjectType = 100)/*mission*/
			BEGIN
				UPDATE [dbo].[CrmLeave]
				   SET [VerificationWorkFlowId] = @VerificationWorkFlowId      
				WHERE [LeaveId] = @ObjectId
				SET @MsgTypeId = 7
			END
			/*AI14020129-End*/
			/*RZ14030308- Add VerificationWorkFlowId to letter*/
			ELSE IF(@ObjectType = 110) /*Letter*/
			BEGIN
			/**/
				/*UPDATE [dbo].[SecLetters]
					SET [IsFix] = 1      
				WHERE LetterId = @ObjectId*/
				SET @MsgTypeId = 14	
			/**/
			END
			/*RZ14030308- End*/
		END
		/*============================= Find next step =======================*/	
		DECLARE @NextStepId INT,@NextStepVerifyUserId INT
		SELECT TOP 1
			 @NextStepId = [VerificationWorkFlowStepId]
			/*,@NextStepVerifyUserId = [VerifyUserId]	*/			
			/*AI14020129 - Add responsible user*/
			,@NextStepVerifyUserId = (CASE WHEN ISNULL([VerifyUserId],0) = -101
									THEN (SELECT TOP(1) [ResponsibleUserId] 
											FROM [dbo].[Users] U
											WHERE U.UserId = @VerificationReqUserIdI										   
										)
									/*AI14020701- Add cmpReferUserId and cmpSuppUserId to verifyUser*/
									WHEN ISNULL([VerifyUserId],0) = -102
									THEN (@cmpReferUserId)
									WHEN ISNULL([VerifyUserId],0) = -103
									THEN (@cmpSuppUserId)
									/*AI14020701- End*/
									ELSE [VerifyUserId]
								END)
			/*AI14020129- End*/
		FROM [dbo].[WSVerificationWorkFlowSteps] VWS
		WHERE [VerificationWorkFlowId] = @VerificationWorkFlowId
		AND [VerificationWorkFlowStepId] NOT IN (SELECT  [VerificationWorkFlowStepId]      
												 FROM [dbo].[WSVerificationRequestDetails] VRD 
												 WHERE VRD.[VerificationRequestId] = @VerificationRequestId)
		ORDER BY ISNULL([OrderView],[VerificationWorkFlowStepId])			
		/*======================= GET VerificationReqUserId ==================*/
		DECLARE @VerificationReqUserId INT
		SELECT @VerificationReqUserId = [VerificationReqUserId] FROM [dbo].[WSVerificationRequests] WHERE [VerificationRequestId] = @VerificationRequestId
		/*============================= Send Message =======================*/	
		IF(ISNULL(@NextStepId,0) > 0 AND ISNULL(@SequenceObservation,0) = 1)
		BEGIN
		/**/
			/*EXEC [dbo].[MobspInternalMessageLoadReceiver] @crmUserId = @VerificationReqUserId
			EXEC [dbo].[MobspIntMsgUpdateSelectReceiver]
						@crmUserId = @VerificationReqUserId,@UserId = @NextStepVerifyUserId,@Selected = 1, @Mode  = 1  AI14011223*/

			/*AI14011223 - instead of [MobspInternalMessageSend]*/
			EXEC [dbo].[MobspInternalMessageSendSingle]
						@msgMasterId = NULL,
						@msgSubject = @MsgSubject,
						@msgBody = @VerifyText,
						/*@msgActiveInCycle = NULL, AI14011223*/
						@msgdTypeId = @MsgTypeId,
						@msgdFromUserId = @VerificationReqUserId,
						@msgdToUserId = @NextStepVerifyUserId,
						@msgdSendDate = @RegisterDatePersian,
						@msgdSendTime = @RegisterTime,
						@msgdSendDateTime = @RegisterDate,
						@msgdPriorityId = NULL,
						@msgdAttachFile = NULL,
						@msgdAttachRecId = @ObjectId,
						@msgdAttachRecDesc = NULL,
						@msgdGroupId  = NULL,
						@UserId = @UserId,
						@Year1 = @Year1,
						@Month1 = @Month1,
						@RegisterDate = @RegisterDate,
						@RegisterDatePersian = @RegisterDatePersian,
						@AppType = @AppType, 
						@InsertedID = @InternalMessageId OUTPUT, /*AI14011223 - instead of @InternalMessageID*/
						@LanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID,
						/*@FirstRegisterUserId = @UserId,
						@FirstRegisterDate = @RegisterDate,
						@FirstRegisterDatePersian = @RegisterDatePersian,
						@ApiId = NULL, AI14011223*/
						@msgdAttachRecIdI = @NextStepId,
						@msgdAttachRecDescI = NULL

			/*Insert Lang table*/
			IF(ISNULL(@InternalMessageId,0) > 0)
			BEGIN
				INSERT INTO [dbo].[CrmInternalMessage_Lang]
					([InternalMessageID]
					,[LanguageID]
					,[msgSubject]
					,[msgBody])
				SELECT
					@InternalMessageId
					,[LangId]
					,Subj
					,Txt
				FROM @TmpTable 
				WHERE [LangId] <> 0 
			END			
		/**/
		END
		ELSE IF(ISNULL(@NextStepId,0) = 0)
		BEGIN
		/**/
			/*============================= Verify Object =============================*/	
			IF(ISNULL(@ObjectType,0) > 0)
			BEGIN
			/**/
				/*Add (@ObjectType = 30) ==> BuyOrder,(@ObjectType = 40) ==> Do */
				IF(@ObjectType = 10)/*PreOrder*/
				BEGIN
					EXEC dbo.MobspPreOrderVerify @ObjectId,@UserId,@RegisterDatePersian
				END
				ELSE IF(@ObjectType = 20)/*Order*/
				BEGIN
					EXEC dbo.MobspOrderVerify @ObjectId,@UserId,@RegisterDatePersian
				END
				/*AI14020918- Add WSStockSupply and buyOrder*/
				ELSE IF(@ObjectType = 40)/*BuyOrder*/
				BEGIN
				/**/
					EXEC dbo.MobspBuyOrderVerify
					@BuyOrderId = @ObjectId,
					@VerifierUserId = @UserId,
					@RegisterDatePersian = @RegisterDatePersian,
					@RegisterDate = @RegisterDate
				/**/
				END
				ELSE IF(@ObjectType = 45)/*Stocksupply*/
				BEGIN
				/**/
					EXEC dbo.MobspStockSupplyVerify
					@StockSupplyId = @ObjectId,
					@VerifierUserId = @UserId,
					@RegisterDate = @RegisterDate,
					@RegisterDatePersian = @RegisterDatePersian
				/**/
				END
				/*AI14020918- End*/
				ELSE IF(@ObjectType = 80)/*DyForm*/
				BEGIN
				/**/

					DECLARE @DyFrmWorkflowId INT = 0;
					SET @DyFrmWorkflowId = (SELECT TOP (1) [WorkflowId]
										FROM [dbo].[CrmDyFormFieldMasterValues]
										WHERE [FormFieldMasterValueId] = @ObjectId)

					IF(ISNULL(@DyFrmWorkflowId,0) = 0)/*normal form*/
					BEGIN
					/**/	
						EXEC dbo.MobspDyFormCheck 
						@MasterValueId = @ObjectId,
						@CheckerUserId = @UserId,
						@CheckedDatePersian = @RegisterDatePersian,
						@Mode = 1
					/**/
					END
					ELSE
					BEGIN
					/**/	
						EXEC dbo.MobspDyFormCheck 
						@MasterValueId = @ObjectId,
						@CheckerUserId = @UserId,
						@CheckedDatePersian = @RegisterDatePersian,
						@Mode = 2
					/**/
					END					
				/**/
				END
				/*AI14020129 - add leave and doc*/
				ELSE IF(@ObjectType = 60)/*Doc*/
				BEGIN
				/**/
					EXEC dbo.MobspDocVerify
					@DocId = @ObjectId,
					@VerifierUserId = @UserId,
					@VerifiedRegisterDate = @RegisterDate,
					@VerifiedRegisterDatePersian = @RegisterDatePersian
				/**/
				END
				ELSE IF(@ObjectType = 90)/*leave*/
				BEGIN
				/**/
					EXEC dbo.MobspLeaveVerify
					@LeaveId = @ObjectId,
					@VerifierUserId = @UserId,
					@VerifiedRegisterDate = @RegisterDate,
					@VerifiedRegisterDatePersian = @RegisterDatePersian
				/**/
				END
				ELSE IF(@ObjectType = 100)/*mission*/
				BEGIN
				/**/
					EXEC dbo.MobspLeaveVerify
					@LeaveId = @ObjectId,
					@VerifierUserId = @UserId,
					@VerifiedRegisterDate = @RegisterDate,
					@VerifiedRegisterDatePersian = @RegisterDatePersian
				/**/
				END
				/*AI14020129-End*/
				/*RZ14030308- Add VerificationWorkFlowId to letter*/
				ELSE IF(@ObjectType = 110) /*Letter*/
				BEGIN
				/**/
					EXEC [dbo].[MobspLetterVerify]
					@LetterId = @ObjectId,
					@VerifierUserId = @UserId,					
					@RegisterDatePersian = @RegisterDatePersian,
					@RegisterDate = @RegisterDate,
					@AppType = @AppType							
				/**/
				END
				/*RZ14030308- End*/
			END
			/*======================== Expired All Request but current request ========*/
			EXEC MobspUpdateVerificationRequestsExpired 
				@VerificationRequestId = @VerificationRequestId,
				@VerificationWorkFlowId = 0,
				@ObjectId = @ObjectId,
				@ObjectType = @ObjectType,
				@Expired = 1,
				@LanguageID = @LanguageID,
				@DefaultLanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID
			/*================== Send Message for the object is verified ==============*/
			/*EXEC [dbo].[MobspInternalMessageLoadReceiver] @crmUserId = @UserId
			EXEC [dbo].[MobspIntMsgUpdateSelectReceiver]
				@crmUserId = @UserId,@UserId = @VerificationReqUserId,@Selected = 1, @Mode  = 1  */

			/*AI14011223 - instead of [MobspInternalMessageSend]*/
			EXEC [dbo].[MobspInternalMessageSendSingle]
				@msgMasterId = NULL,
				@msgSubject = @VerifiedMsgSubject,
				@msgBody = @VerifyText,
				/*@msgActiveInCycle = NULL, AI14011223*/
				@msgdTypeId = @MsgTypeId,
				@msgdFromUserId = @UserId,
				@msgdToUserId = @VerificationReqUserId,
				@msgdSendDate=@RegisterDatePersian,
				@msgdSendTime = @RegisterTime,
				@msgdSendDateTime = @RegisterDate,
				@msgdPriorityId = NULL,
				@msgdAttachFile = NULL,
				@msgdAttachRecId = @ObjectId,
				@msgdAttachRecDesc = NULL,
				@msgdGroupId  = NULL,
				@UserId = @UserId,
				@Year1 = @Year1,
				@Month1 = @Month1,
				@RegisterDate = @RegisterDate,
				@RegisterDatePersian = @RegisterDatePersian,
				@AppType = @AppType, 
				@InsertedID = @InternalMessageId OUTPUT,  /*AI14011223 - instead of @InternalMessageID*/
				@LanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID,
				/*@FirstRegisterUserId = @UserId,
				@FirstRegisterDate = @RegisterDate,
				@FirstRegisterDatePersian = @RegisterDatePersian,
				@ApiId = NULL, AI14011223*/
				@msgdAttachRecIdI = NULL,
				@msgdAttachRecDescI = NULL
			/*Insert Lang table*/
			IF(ISNULL(@InternalMessageId,0) > 0)
			BEGIN
				INSERT INTO [dbo].[CrmInternalMessage_Lang]
					([InternalMessageID]
					,[LanguageID]
					,[msgSubject]
					,[msgBody])
				SELECT
					@InternalMessageId
					,[LangId]
					,[VerifiedSubj]
					,[Txt]
				FROM @TmpTable 
				WHERE [LangId] <> 0 
			END			
			/*=========================================================================*/
		/**/
		END		
	/**/
	END
	ELSE
		SET @Result = '-5'/*error in insert into WSVerificationRequestDetails*/
/**/
END
/*======================================================================*/
IF(ISNULL(@Result,'') = '')
	SET @Result = '1' /*OK*/
/**/
END
