-- [dbo].[PayRollspCalculateUserCycleItemValues]







/*RZ980126*/
/*RZ14011214- Update CycleNetSalary*/
/*AI14020328 - Fix formula with unknown tags - Round formula result*/
/*RZ14021130- Add orderView to fix calculate*/
Create PROCEDURE [dbo].[PayRollspCalculateUserCycleItemValues]
(		
/*		
	declare @p8 nvarchar(max)
	set @p8=N''
	exec [dbo].[PayRollspCalculateUserCycleItemValues] @CycleId=1154,@UserId=3,@RegisterDate='2023-06-18 10:04:49',@RegisterDatePersian='14020328',@Year1=30,@Month1=3,@AppType='web',@ErrorMessage=@p8 output,@LanguageID=1065,@DefaultLanguageID=1065
	select @p8
*/
@CycleId	INT,
@UserId		INT,
@RegisterDate	DATETIME,
@RegisterDatePersian	VARCHAR(8),
@Year1	SMALLINT,
@Month1	SMALLINT,
@AppType	VARCHAR(20), 
@ErrorMessage NVARCHAR(MAX) OUTPUT,
@LanguageID INT = NULL,
@DefaultLanguageID INT = NULL
)
AS
BEGIN
/**/
/*==================================================*/
DECLARE @PayRollUserCycleItemValueId INT, @ItemId INT, @Value NUMERIC(18,2),
@UsefulIds NVARCHAR(MAX), @ItemTypeId INT,@ItemFormula NVARCHAR(MAX)
/**/
DECLARE Crsr CURSOR FOR
	SELECT 
		[PayRollUserCycleItemValueId]
		,CIV.[ItemId]
		,CIV.[Value]
		,CIV.[UsefulIds]
		/**/
		,PRI.[ItemTypeId]
		,PRI.[ItemFormula]		   
	FROM [dbo].[PayRollUserCycleItemValues] CIV
	INNER JOIN [PayRollItems] PRI 
	ON CIV.ItemId = PRI.PayRollItemId
	WHERE CIV.[CycleId] = @CycleId
	AND PRI.[ItemTypeId] = 2 /*formula*/
	/*AND ISNULL(PRI.[ItemIsActive],0) = 1*/
	ORDER BY PRI.ItemOrderView,CIV.[ItemId]/*RZ14021130- Add orderView to fix calculate*/
/**/
OPEN Crsr
FETCH NEXT FROM Crsr INTO @PayRollUserCycleItemValueId, @ItemId, @Value, @UsefulIds,@ItemTypeId,@ItemFormula
	While @@Fetch_Status = 0
	BEGIN	 	
		/*-------------------------------------------*/				
		/*Set Item Name*/
		DECLARE @ItemName NVARCHAR(300)
		IF(@LanguageID IS NULL OR @LanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID)
			SET @ItemName = (SELECT [ItemName] FROM [dbo].[PayRollItems] PRI WHERE PRI.[PayRollItemId] = @ItemId)
		ELSE
			SET @ItemName = (SELECT [ItemName] FROM [dbo].[PayRollItems_Lang] PRI WHERE PRI.[PayRollItemId] = @ItemId AND PRI.[LanguageID] = @LanguageID)
		
		/*-------------------------------------------------*/
			IF(@ItemFormula IS NOT NULL AND @ItemFormula <> '')
			BEGIN
				/**/
				/*--------------- Replace formula ---------------*/
				SELECT @ItemFormula = REPLACE(@ItemFormula,PRI.[ItemFormulaTag],ISNULL(CAST(CIV.[Value] AS NVARCHAR(300)),PRI.[ItemFormulaTag]))
				FROM [dbo].[PayRollUserCycleItemValues] CIV
				INNER JOIN [PayRollItems] PRI ON CIV.ItemId = PRI.PayRollItemId
				WHERE CIV.CycleId = @CycleId
				AND (PRI.[ItemFormulaTag] IS NOT NULL AND PRI.[ItemFormulaTag] <>'')

				/*AI14020328 - Fix formula with unknown tags*/
				IF(PatIndex('%#%', @ItemFormula) > 0)
				BEGIN
					SELECT @ItemFormula = REPLACE(@ItemFormula, PRI.[ItemFormulaTag], '|#|') FROM [PayRollItems] PRI WHERE
					(PRI.[ItemFormulaTag] IS NOT NULL AND PRI.[ItemFormulaTag] <>'')

					SET @ItemFormula = REPLACE(@ItemFormula, ' ', '')
					/**/
					SET @ItemFormula = REPLACE(@ItemFormula, '+|#|', '+0')
					SET @ItemFormula = REPLACE(@ItemFormula, '|#|+', '0+')
					SET @ItemFormula = REPLACE(@ItemFormula, '+(|#|)', '+(0)')
					SET @ItemFormula = REPLACE(@ItemFormula, '(|#|)+', '(0)+')
					/**/
					SET @ItemFormula = REPLACE(@ItemFormula, '-|#|', '-0')
					SET @ItemFormula = REPLACE(@ItemFormula, '|#|-', '0-')
					SET @ItemFormula = REPLACE(@ItemFormula, '-(|#|)', '-(0)')
					SET @ItemFormula = REPLACE(@ItemFormula, '(|#|)-', '(0)-')
					/**/
					SET @ItemFormula = REPLACE(@ItemFormula, '*|#|', '*1')
					SET @ItemFormula = REPLACE(@ItemFormula, '|#|*', '1*')
					SET @ItemFormula = REPLACE(@ItemFormula, '*(|#|)', '*(1)')
					SET @ItemFormula = REPLACE(@ItemFormula, '(|#|)*', '(1)*')
					/**/
					SET @ItemFormula = REPLACE(@ItemFormula, '|#|/', '0/')
					SET @ItemFormula = REPLACE(@ItemFormula, '/|#|', '/1')
					SET @ItemFormula = REPLACE(@ItemFormula, '(|#|)/', '(0)/')
					SET @ItemFormula = REPLACE(@ItemFormula, '/(|#|)', '/(1)')
					/**/
					SET @ItemFormula = REPLACE(@ItemFormula, '|#|>', '0>')
					SET @ItemFormula = REPLACE(@ItemFormula, '>|#|', '>0')
					SET @ItemFormula = REPLACE(@ItemFormula, '(|#|)>', '(0)>')
					SET @ItemFormula = REPLACE(@ItemFormula, '>(|#|)', '>(0)')
					/**/
					SET @ItemFormula = REPLACE(@ItemFormula, '|#|<', '0<')
					SET @ItemFormula = REPLACE(@ItemFormula, '<|#|', '<0')
					SET @ItemFormula = REPLACE(@ItemFormula, '(|#|)<', '(0)<')
					SET @ItemFormula = REPLACE(@ItemFormula, '<(|#|)', '<(0)')
					/**/
					SET @ItemFormula = REPLACE(@ItemFormula, '|#|=', '0=')
					SET @ItemFormula = REPLACE(@ItemFormula, '=|#|', '=0')
					SET @ItemFormula = REPLACE(@ItemFormula, '(|#|)=', '(0)=')
					SET @ItemFormula = REPLACE(@ItemFormula, '=(|#|)', '=(0)')

					SET @ItemFormula = REPLACE(@ItemFormula, '|#|', '0')
				END
				/*AI14020328-End*/

				/*AND ISNULL(PRI.ItemJustForShow,0) = 0*/
				/*------------- Calculate and Update ------------*/
				BEGIN TRY
					/**/
						/*--------- Calculate formula -------------*/
						DECLARE @Result NUMERIC(18,2), @StrCom NVARCHAR(MAX) = N''
						SET @StrCom ='SELECT @Result = ' + @ItemFormula
						EXECUTE sp_executesql @StrCom, N'@Result NUMERIC(18,2) OUTPUT',@Result = @Result OUTPUT
						/*-----------------------------------------*/
						/**/
						UPDATE [dbo].[PayRollUserCycleItemValues]
							SET  [Value] = CEILING(@Result) /*AI14020328 - round formula result*/
								,[UsefulIds] = @UsefulIds
								,[ValueNotValid] = 0
								,[UserId] = @UserId
								,[Year1] = @Year1
								,[Month1] = @Month1
								,[RegisterDate] = @RegisterDate
								,[RegisterDatePersian] = @RegisterDatePersian
								,[AppType] = @AppType
						WHERE [PayRollUserCycleItemValueId] = @PayRollUserCycleItemValueId
						/**/										
					/**/
				END TRY
				BEGIN CATCH
					/*SET @ErrorMessage += N'Error in formula,Id=' + CAST(@PayRollUserCycleItemValueId AS NVARCHAR(100)) + '|'*/
					SET @ErrorMessage += N'Error in formula,ItemName=' + ISNULL(@ItemName,'') + '@|@'

					/**/
					UPDATE [dbo].[PayRollUserCycleItemValues]
						SET  [ValueNotValid] = 1
					WHERE [PayRollUserCycleItemValueId] = @PayRollUserCycleItemValueId
					/**/
				END CATCH
				/*-----------------------------------------------*/									
				/**/
			END
			ELSE
			BEGIN						
				/*SET @ErrorMessage += N'Formula is null,Id=' + CAST(@PayRollUserCycleItemValueId AS NVARCHAR(100)) + '|'*/
				SET @ErrorMessage += N'Formula is null,ItemName=' + ISNULL(@ItemName,'') + '@|@'
				/**/
				UPDATE [dbo].[PayRollUserCycleItemValues]
					SET  [ValueNotValid] = 1
				WHERE [PayRollUserCycleItemValueId] = @PayRollUserCycleItemValueId
				/**/
			END						
								
		/*-------------------------------------------*/
		FETCH NEXT FROM Crsr INTO @PayRollUserCycleItemValueId, @ItemId, @Value, @UsefulIds,@ItemTypeId,@ItemFormula
	END
CLOSE Crsr
DEALLOCATE Crsr					
/*==================================================*/									
/*RZ14011214- Update CycleNetSalary*/
UPDATE [dbo].[PayRollUserCycle]
SET [CycleNetSalary] = ISNULL((
							ISNULL((SELECT SUM(ISNULL([Value],0))
								 FROM [dbo].[PayRollUserCycleItemValues] CIV
								 WHERE CIV.[CycleId] = @CycleId
								 AND CIV.[ItemId] IN (
									SELECT [PayRollItemId]FROM [dbo].[PayRollItems] PRI
									WHERE PRI.[ItemKind] = 1
									AND ISNULL(PRI.[ItemJustForShow],0) = 0)),0)								 			
							-
							ISNULL((SELECT SUM(ISNULL([Value],0))
								 FROM [dbo].[PayRollUserCycleItemValues] CIV
								 WHERE CIV.[CycleId] = @CycleId
								 AND CIV.[ItemId] IN (
									SELECT [PayRollItemId]FROM [dbo].[PayRollItems] PRI
									WHERE PRI.[ItemKind] = 2
									AND ISNULL(PRI.[ItemJustForShow],0) = 0)),0)
						),0)	
WHERE [PayRollUserCycleId] = @CycleId
/*RZ14011214- End*/
/*==================================================*/
/**/
END

-- [dbo].[SmsReturnBodyTextLang]





--ADBF960911
CREATE FUNCTION [dbo].[SmsReturnBodyTextLang]
(	-- Select * From SmsReturnBodyTextLang('tyu')
@input VARCHAR(4000)
)
RETURNS @tbl TABLE (BodyLang VARCHAR(5), BodyLen INT, BodyPageCount INT )
AS 
BEGIN
DECLARE 
@Lan VARCHAR(10), @BodyCount INT  
SET @BodyCount = LEN(REPLACE(@input, CHAR(10),''))
/**/
DECLARE @EnglishChar char(36) = 'abcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz0123456789';
DECLARE @index int = 1
WHILE @index <= LEN(@input)
BEGIN
IF CHARINDEX(SUBSTRING(@input,@index,1),@EnglishChar)=0
	BEGIN
	SET @Lan = 'FA';
	BREAK;
	END
ELSE
	BEGIN
	SET @Lan = 'EN';
	SET @index = @index+1;
	END	  
END
/**/
DECLARE @x int
IF @Lan = 'En'
BEGIN
	SET @x= @BodyCount
	SET @BodyCount = 0
	WHILE @x > 0
	BEGIN
		SET @BodyCount = @BodyCount + 1
		IF @BodyCount = 1 SET @x = @x - 159
		ELSE
		IF @BodyCount = 2 SET @x = @x - 146 - 1
		ELSE
		IF @BodyCount > 2 SET @x = @x - 152 - 1
	END 
/**/
END
ELSE
IF @Lan = 'Fa'
BEGIN				
	SET @x= @BodyCount
	SET @BodyCount = 0
	WHILE @x > 0
	BEGIN
		SET @BodyCount = @BodyCount + 1
		IF @BodyCount = 1 SET @x = @x - 70
		ELSE
		IF @BodyCount = 2 SET @x = @x - 63 - 1
		ELSE
		IF @BodyCount > 2 SET @x = @x - 66 - 1
	END 
END
/**/
INSERT INTO @tbl
SELECT @Lan ,LEN(REPLACE(@input, CHAR(10),'')), @BodyCount
/**/
RETURN
END

-- [dbo].[WSfnMakeAndReturnPrdCode]



/*ADBF899510*/
CREATE Function [dbo].[WSfnMakeAndReturnPrdCode] ( @FGId INT, @SGId INT)
/*-- Select dbo.WSfnMakeAndReturnPrdCode (1, 1) */
Returns VARCHAR(50)		
As
BEGIN
/**/
DECLARE @FGCode VARCHAR(5), @SGCode VARCHAR(5), @PrdCode VARCHAR(50)
/**/
SELECT  @PrdCode = ISNULL(MAX( RIGHT(PrdCode, 5) ), 0)
FROM dbo.WSProducts
WHERE PrdGroupID = @SGId
SELECT @FGCode = ISNULL(FGCode, 0) FROM dbo.WSProductFirstGroup WHERE PrdFirstGroupID = @FGId
SELECT @SGCode = ISNULL(SGCode, 0) FROM dbo.WSProductSecondGroup WHERE PrdSecondGroupID = @SGId
/*--*/
SET @FGCode = RIGHT('000' + IsNull(@FGCode, 0), 3) 
SET @SGCode = RIGHT('000' + ISNULL(@SGCode, 0), 3)
/*--PRINT @FGCode PRINT @SGCode*/
SET @PrdCode = @PrdCode + 1
SET @PrdCode = RIGHT('0000' + @PrdCode, 4)
/*--PRINT @PrdCode*/
SET @PrdCode = RIGHT( @FGCode + @SGCode + @PrdCode, 20)
/*--PRINT @PrdCode*/
RETURN(@PrdCode)
/**/
END

-- [dbo].[WSfnReturnOrderDailyCounter]







/*RZ14011109*/
Create Function [dbo].[WSfnReturnOrderDailyCounter]
(			
/* 
	Select dbo.WSfnReturnOrderDailyCounter(1, '2023-01-29')
*/
@OrderOprId INT,
@CurrentDate DATETIME 
)
RETURNS BIGINT		
AS
BEGIN
/**/

DECLARE @DailyCounter BIGINT, @ordDailyCounter BIGINT

SET @DailyCounter = ISNULL((SELECT TOP (1) [DailyCounter] FROM [dbo].[WSOrderOpr] WHERE [OrderOprID] = @OrderOprId),0)
IF(@DailyCounter > 0)
BEGIN
/**/
	SET @DailyCounter = @DailyCounter - 1
/**/
END
/**/
SELECT 
	@ordDailyCounter = ISNULL(MAX([ordDailyCounter]), @DailyCounter)
FROM [dbo].[WSOrder]
WHERE OrderOprID = @OrderOprId 
AND CAST([ordDateTime] AS DATE) = CAST(@CurrentDate AS DATE)

SET @ordDailyCounter = @ordDailyCounter + 1

RETURN(@ordDailyCounter)

/**/
END

-- [dbo].[WSfnReturnOrderNumber]



/*ADBF89061907059403*/
/*RZ990811- Change type from int to bigint*/
Create Function [dbo].[WSfnReturnOrderNumber]
(			
/* Select dbo.WSfnReturnOrderNumber(1, 2)*/
@FinancialPeriod INT,
@OrderOprId INT 
)
RETURNS BIGINT	/*RZ990811- Change type from int to bigint*/	
AS
BEGIN
/**/
/*DECLARE @Number INT, @CurrentYear INT, @BeginNumber INT */
DECLARE @Number BIGINT, @CurrentYear INT, @BeginNumber BIGINT /*RZ990811- Change type from int to bigint*/

SELECT @CurrentYear = SUBSTRING(CAST(FDate AS VARCHAR(4)), 3, 2)
FROM dbo.WSFinancialPeriod 
Where Id = @FinancialPeriod

/*SET @BeginNumber = CAST(CAST(@CurrentYear AS VARCHAR(4))+CAST(@OrderOprId AS VARCHAR(5)) + '0000' AS INT)*/
SET @BeginNumber = CAST(CAST(@CurrentYear AS VARCHAR(4))+CAST(@OrderOprId AS VARCHAR(5)) + '0000' AS BIGINT) /*RZ990811- Change type from int to bigint*/

SELECT @Number = ISNULL(MAX( ordNumber), @BeginNumber)
FROM dbo.WSOrder
WHERE OrderOprID = @OrderOprId 
AND FPId = @FinancialPeriod

SET @Number = @Number + 1

RETURN(@Number)

/**/
END

-- [dbo].[WSfnReturnOrderSerialNumber]


/*ADBF9407*/
CREATE Function [dbo].[WSfnReturnOrderSerialNumber]
(			-- Select dbo.WSfnReturnOrderSerialNumber(1, 2)
@FinancialPeriod INT,
@OrderOprId INT 
)
Returns INT		
As
BEGIN

DECLARE @SNumber INT, @CurrentYear INT, @BeginNumber INT 

SELECT @CurrentYear = SUBSTRING(CAST(FDate AS VARCHAR(4)), 3, 2)
FROM dbo.WSFinancialPeriod 
Where Id = @FinancialPeriod

SET @BeginNumber = CAST(CAST(@CurrentYear AS VARCHAR(4))+
		CAST(@OrderOprId AS VARCHAR(5)) + '0000' AS INT) 

SELECT @SNumber = ISNULL(MAX( ordSerial), @BeginNumber)
FROM dbo.WSOrder
WHERE OrderOprID = @OrderOprId 
AND FPId = @FinancialPeriod

SET @SNumber = @SNumber + 1

RETURN(@SNumber)
end

-- [dbo].[WSfnReturnOrderSumFooter]








/*--890621ADBF9105039211930294109411*/
/*RZ971109- Change price to decimals*/
/*RZ990817- Add fields*/
/*RZ991008- Modify SumPriceAmount*/
/*RZ14001018- Add TotalPriceSumWithoutDiscount*/
Create Function [dbo].[WSfnReturnOrderSumFooter]
(			/* Select * from dbo.WSfnReturnOrderSumFooter(17241) */
@OrderID INT 
)
RETURNS TABLE
AS
RETURN
(
	SELECT /*Round(*//*RZ971109- Change price to decimals*/
		/*CAST(SUM(
		  (ISNULL(ordIQuantity, 0) * ISNULL( ordIFCR, 1)* ISNULL( ordIFCRI, 1)) *
		( ISNULL(ordIPrice, 0) - ( ISNULL(ordIDiscount, 0) + ISNULL(ordIDiscountI, 0) + ISNULL(ordIRound, 0)) 
			/*960703*/
			+ ISNULL(ordIRoundPlus, 0) )		
			+ ISNULL(ordITax, 0) * ISNULL(ordIQuantity, 0) * ISNULL( ordIFCR, 1)* ISNULL( ordIFCRI, 1)		
		) AS NUMERIC(18,2))
		/*, 0)*/ SumPriceAmount, */

		/*RZ991008- Modify SumPriceAmount*/
		CAST(SUM(ISNULL(ordIPriceSum,0) + ISNULL(ordITaxSum,0)) AS NUMERIC(18,2)) SumPriceAmount, 
		/*RZ991008- End*/
		SUM(ordIPrice) SumPrice,
		SUM(ordIQuantity) SumQuantity,
		SUM(ordIDiscount) SumDiscount, 
		SUM(ordIDiscountI) SumDiscountI, 
		SUM(ordITax) SumTax,
		SUM(ordIFCR) SumFCR,
		SUM(ordIFCRI) SumFCRI,
		SUM(ordIRound) SumRound,
		/*RZ990817- Add fields*/
		SUM(ordIPriceSum) TotalPriceSum,	
		SUM(ordIDiscountSum) TotalDiscountSum,
		SUM(ordIDiscountISum) TotalDiscountISum,
		SUM(ordITaxSum) TotalTaxSum, 
		/*RZ990817- End*/ 
		/*RZ14001018- Add TotalPriceSumWithoutDiscount*/
		SUM(
			ISNULL(ordIPriceSum,0) + ISNULL(ordIDiscountSum,0)		
		)TotalPriceSumWithoutDiscount
		/*RZ14001018- End*/
	FROM dbo.WSOrderItems
	WHERE OrderId = @OrderID
	AND ISNULL(ordINotInSum, 0) = 0
)

-- [dbo].[WSfnReturnPreOrderNumber]





/*--ADBF890619*/
/*RZ990811- Change type from int to bigint*/
/*RZ14000116- Add pordTypeId*/
Create Function [dbo].[WSfnReturnPreOrderNumber]
(	/* Select dbo.WSfnReturnPreOrderNumber(30,6)*/
@FinancialPeriod INT,
@pordTypeId INT = NULL /*RZ14000116- Add pordTypeId*/
)
RETURNS BIGINT	/*RZ990811- Change type from int to bigint*/	
AS
BEGIN

DECLARE @Number BIGINT, @CurrentYear INT, @BeginNumber BIGINT/*RZ990811- Change type from int to bigint*/

SELECT @CurrentYear = SUBSTRING(CAST(FDate AS VARCHAR(4)), 3, 2)
FROM dbo.WSFinancialPeriod 
Where Id = @FinancialPeriod
/**/
/*SET @BeginNumber = CAST(CAST(@CurrentYear AS VARCHAR(4)) + '0000' AS INT) */
SET @BeginNumber = CAST(CAST(@CurrentYear AS VARCHAR(4)) + '0000' AS BIGINT) /*RZ990811- Change type from int to bigint*/
/**/
/*SELECT @Number = ISNULL(MAX( pordNumber), @BeginNumber)
FROM dbo.WSPreOrder
Where FPId =  @FinancialPeriod*/
/*--------------------------------------------------------*/
/*RZ14000116- Add pordTypeId*/
IF(ISNULL((SELECT TOP 1 [IsActivePordNumberByType] FROM dbo.CrmsysSetting),0) = 1 AND ISNULL(@pordTypeId,0) <> 0)
BEGIN
/**/
	SELECT @Number = ISNULL(MAX( pordNumber), @BeginNumber)
	FROM dbo.WSPreOrder
	Where FPId =  @FinancialPeriod
	AND pordTypeId = @pordTypeId
/**/
END
ELSE 
BEGIN
/**/
	SELECT @Number = ISNULL(MAX( pordNumber), @BeginNumber)
	FROM dbo.WSPreOrder
	Where FPId =  @FinancialPeriod
/**/
END
/*RZ14000116- End*/
/*--------------------------------------------------------*/
/**/
SET @Number = @Number + 1
/**/
RETURN(@Number)
/**/
END

-- [dbo].[WSfnReturnPreOrderNumberRevision]




/*RZ990821*/
Create Function [dbo].[WSfnReturnPreOrderNumberRevision]
(	
	/* 
		Select dbo.WSfnReturnPreOrderNumberRevision(2481,30)
	*/
@PorOrderId INT,
@FinancialPeriod INT
)
RETURNS BIGINT	
AS
BEGIN
/**/
	DECLARE @NumberRevision BIGINT
	/**/
	SELECT @NumberRevision = ISNULL(MAX(pordNumberRevision), 0)
	FROM dbo.WSPreOrder PO
	WHERE PO.pordNumber =  (SELECT TOP 1 P.pordNumber FROM WSPreOrder P WHERE P.PreOrderID = @PorOrderId)
	AND PO.FPId =  @FinancialPeriod
	/**/
	SET @NumberRevision = @NumberRevision + 1
	/**/
	RETURN(@NumberRevision)
/**/
END






GO
-- [dbo].[WSfnReturnPreOrderSumFooter]






/*--890621ADBF9105039211
--ADBF93029507*/
/*RZ971109- Change price to decimals*/
/*RZ990817- Add fields*/
/*RZ991008- Modify SumPriceAmount*/
/*RZ14001018- Add TotalPriceSumWithoutDiscount*/
Create Function [dbo].[WSfnReturnPreOrderSumFooter]
(			/* Select * from dbo.WSfnReturnPreOrderSumFooter(3020) */
@PreOrderID INT 
)
RETURNS TABLE
AS
RETURN
(
SELECT 
   /*ROUND(*//*RZ971109- Change price to decimals*/
	/*	CAST(SUM(
		 (ISNULL(pordIQuantity, 0) * ISNULL(pordIFCR, 1) * ISNULL(pordIFCRI, 1)) 
		 * 
		 (( ISNULL(pordIPrice, 0) - ( ISNULL(pordIDiscount, 0) + ISNULL(pordIDiscountI, 0) + ISNULL(pordIRound, 0))
		 /*ADBF960120*/
		 + ISNULL(pordIRoundPlus, 0)
		 ))	  
	 		+ (ISNULL( pordITax, 0) * ISNULL(pordIQuantity, 0) * ISNULL(pordIFCR, 1) * ISNULL(pordIFCRI, 1))
		) AS NUMERIC(18,2))
	/*, 0)*/ SumPriceAmount, */

	/*RZ991008- Modify SumPriceAmount*/
	CAST(SUM( ISNULL(pordIPriceSum,0) + ISNULL(pordITaxSum,0)) AS NUMERIC(18,2)) SumPriceAmount, 	
	/*RZ991008- End*/

	SUM(pordIPrice) SumPrice,
	SUM(pordIQuantity) SumQuantity,
	SUM( pordIDiscount) SumDiscount, 
	SUM( pordIDiscountI) SumDiscountI, 
	SUM(pordITax) SumTax,
	SUM(pordIFCR) SumFCR,
	SUM(pordIFCRI) SumFCRI,
	SUM(pordIRound) SumRound,
	/*RZ990817- Add fields*/
	SUM(pordIPriceSum) TotalPriceSum,	
	SUM(pordIDiscountSum) TotalDiscountSum,
	SUM(pordIDiscountISum) TotalDiscountISum,
	SUM(pordITaxSum) TotalTaxSum, 
	/*RZ990817- End*/ 
	/*RZ14001018- Add TotalPriceSumWithoutDiscount*/
	SUM(
		ISNULL(pordIPriceSum,0) + ISNULL(pordIDiscountSum,0)		
	)TotalPriceSumWithoutDiscount
	/*RZ14001018- End*/
FROM dbo.WSPreOrderItems
WHERE PreOrderId = @PreOrderID
AND ISNULL(pordINotInSum, 0) = 0
)







GO

Create Function [dbo].[WSfnReturnStockSupplyNumber]
(	
/* 
	Select dbo.WSfnReturnStockSupplyNumber(1)
*/
@StockSupplyOprId INT 
)
RETURNS BIGINT	
AS
BEGIN
/**/	
	DECLARE @Number BIGINT

	SELECT @Number = ISNULL(MAX([stsupNumber]), 0)
	FROM [dbo].[WSStockSupply]
	WHERE [StockSupplyOprId] = @StockSupplyOprId 

	SET @Number = ISNULL(@Number,0) + 1

	RETURN(@Number)
/**/
END
